### [1] [OptiMAS: Automatically Optimize Multi-Agent System](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21918)
*Yuxin Cheng,Chang Liu,Hanxin Yu,Haochen Tan,Taiqiang Wu,Weiqiang Jin,Jie Ran,Kaibo Wang,Xiaoguang Li,Haoli Bai,Graziano Chesi,Ngai Wong*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了OptiMAS，一个基于统一ReAct基础设施的任务无关智能体优化器，通过文本交互轨迹和任务反馈作为损失信号实现端到端的多智能体系统（MAS）进化，有效解决了现有搜索范式在优化范围与进化稳定性之间的权衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于搜索的多智能体系统自动化进化方法面临根本性权衡：扩大优化范围会加剧进化不稳定性，而离散的分支丢弃搜索则隔离了不同谱系间的洞见。需要一种既能保持广泛优化范围又能维持操作稳定性的新范式。

Method: 提出了一个基于统一ReAct基础设施的连续数据驱动优化范式。设计了OptiMAS任务无关智能体优化器，利用文本交互轨迹和任务反馈作为损失信号进行端到端MAS进化。引入了新颖的双轨记忆机制以在扩展的优化时域上维持性能提升。

Result: 在四个异构智能体基准和三种不同规模与可访问性的LLM骨干网络上进行评估，OptiMAS在准确性方面始终达到与领域专门化手工系统和现有进化方法相当或更优的性能表现。

Conclusion: 本研究为稳健、自动化的MAS进化建立了实用里程碑，证明了连续数据驱动优化范式的有效性，为减少LLM智能体架构设计与优化的手动工作提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Automated evolution of Multi-Agent Systems (MAS) holds significant potential for reducing the manual effort required to design and optimize LLM-based agent architectures. However, extant search-based paradigms face a fundamental trade-off, where an expanded optimization scope exacerbates evolutionary instability, while discrete branch-and-discard search isolates insights across lineages. To address these limitations, we propose a continuous, data-driven optimization paradigm built upon a unified ReAct-based infrastructure that reconciles a broad optimization scope with operational stability. Under this paradigm, we present OptiMAS, a task-agnostic agentic optimizer that leverages textual interaction trajectories and task feedback as loss signals for end-to-end MAS evolution. Equipped with a novel dual-track memory mechanism, OptiMAS sustains performance improvement over extended optimization horizons. Evaluation on four heterogeneous agentic benchmarks with three varying scale and accessibility LLM backbones, demonstrates that OptiMAS consistently achieves competitive or superior accuracy relative to both domain-specialized hand-crafted systems and existing evolutionary methods. Our work establishes a practical milestone toward robust, automated MAS evolution.

</details>


### [2] [Opinion-Guided Layered Strategies for Decentralized Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22104)
*Shuhao Qi,Zhiyong Sun,Siep Weiland,Sofie Haesaert*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出观点引导策略，利用非线性观点动力学实现自主智能体在无通信条件下与任意独立智能体达成协调，打破传统策略无法区分相同策略智能体的对称性限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主智能体间的交互通常存在多种可接受的联合行为。当两个智能体偏好不同行为时，其独立策略可能不兼容；当偏好相同时，智能体无法在必要时区分各自角色。现有策略缺乏对任意智能体的通用协调能力。

Method: 提出观点引导策略，保留所有可接受联合行为，将选择推迟到执行时刻。采用分层实现的非线性观点动力学，根据另一智能体的演化行为实时引导智能体达成共同的可接受联合行为，无需通信。形式化建立策略对所有可能偏好保持鲁棒性的条件。

Result: 形式化证明了观点引导策略对另一智能体可能持有的任何偏好保持鲁棒；该鲁棒性使得运行相同策略的两个智能体能够在必要时打破对称性。三个跨领域案例研究表明，只要另一方愿意实现某个可接受联合行为，该策略就能与其达成协调。在一般和博弈中，该策略保持所有纳什均衡开放并保证智能体在运行时交互中达成其中一个。

Conclusion: 观点引导策略为多智能体协调提供了新范式，避免了传统方法预先寻找唯一纳什均衡的限制，实现了运行时的自适应协调，为自主智能体的复杂交互场景提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous agents increasingly interact with other independent agents, and such interactions typically admit multiple joint behaviors. When two agents prefer different ones, their independent strategies may be mutually incompatible and fail to reach a coordinated outcome; when they are identical, neither can differentiate its role when needed. Ideally, an agent should coordinate with any agent it encounters, regardless of which admissible joint behavior that agent aims to realize. We therefore propose a new form of strategy, the opinion-guided strategy, which keeps all the admissible joint behaviors available and postpones the selection to execution time, when the other agent's behavior reveals which one to realize. To realize this, nonlinear opinion dynamics are leveraged in a layered realization to guide the agent to a common admissible joint behavior in response to the other agent's evolving behavior, even without communication. We formally establish the conditions under which the strategy remains robust to every preference the other agent may hold. This robustness has an important implication: two agents running identical strategies can break symmetry when needed, a capability that conventional strategies lack. Three case studies across different applications show that the opinion-guided strategy coordinates with every randomly encountered agent, as long as it is willing to realize one of the admissible joint behaviors. One of them corresponds to a general-sum game: unlike conventional approaches devoted to finding a unique Nash equilibrium in advance, the opinion-guided strategy keeps every equilibrium open and guarantees the agents reach one, decided by their runtime interaction.

</details>


### [3] [PropUQ-MAS: Propagation-Aware Uncertainty Quantification for LLM Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22130)
*Yaokun Liu,Yifan Liu,Daniel Yue Zhang,Ruichen Yao,Zelin Li,Dong Wang*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了PropUQ-MAS框架，通过考虑误差传播来解决多智能体系统中的不确定性量化问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM多智能体系统中，智能体间依赖关系引入可靠性风险（如中间消息错误会被下游智能体继承和放大），而现有不确定性量化方法仅针对单智能体场景，无法捕捉MAS中的不确定性传播。

Method: 提出PropUQ-MAS框架：将MAS执行过程建模为通信结构图，通过融合局部不确定性与来自上游消息的继承不确定性来估计每个步骤的可靠性。

Result: 实验表明PropUQ-MAS在MAS不确定性量化任务上持续改善，AUROC平均相对提升+6.10%，PRR平均相对提升+47.58%。

Conclusion: PropUQ-MAS成功解决了多智能体系统中的不确定性传播问题，显著提升了不确定性量化的性能。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) solve complex tasks through communication among role-specialized agents. However, inter-agent dependencies introduce reliability risks beyond isolated agent failures. For instance, errors in intermediate messages could be inherited and amplified by downstream agents. Existing uncertainty quantification (UQ) methods mainly target isolated responses or single-agent reasoning, and therefore fail to capture uncertainty propagation in MAS. To this end, we propose PropUQ-MAS, an error propagation-aware UQ framework that represents MAS execution as a communication-structured graph and estimates each step's reliability by combining local uncertainty with uncertainty inherited from upstream messages. Extensive experiments demonstrate that PropUQ-MAS consistently improves UQ in MAS, with average relative gains of +6.10% in AUROC and +47.58% in PRR.

</details>


### [4] [Minimal Local Simulation Foundations for LLM- and VLM-Driven Agents in 2D and 3D Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22833)
*Ryuki Hyodo*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 提出了两个轻量级模拟平台(SD-AgentFoundry-2D/3D),支持本地运行LLM/VLM驱动的多智能体仿真,旨在教育学习和快速原型开发。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM/VLM驱动的智能体仿真平台难以研究、修改或在普通计算机上运行,缺乏适合教育和快速原型开发的轻量级工具。

Method: 开发两个极简仿真基础平台:SD-AgentFoundry-2D提供二维多智能体环境,支持本地LLM智能体的移动、通信、响应空间占用和火灾事件;SD-AgentFoundry-3D提供三维数字孪生环境,本地VLM接收第一人称图像并生成自然语言移动指令。两个代码库均支持macOS、Windows、Linux本地运行,并保持开放修改。

Result: 成功构建了两个可本地运行的仿真平台,能够支持LLM智能体在二维环境中的空间交互行为,以及VLM智能体在三维环境中基于视觉的运动控制。

Conclusion: 这两个平台为学习生成式社会仿真和构建领域特定扩展提供了易于访问的起点,填补了教育和快速原型开发工具的空白。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and vision-language models (VLMs) are expanding the range of behaviors that can be represented in agent-based simulations, but many contemporary platforms are difficult to study, modify, or run on ordinary computers. We present two intentionally minimal simulation foundations for education and rapid prototyping. SD-AgentFoundry-2D provides a two-dimensional multi-agent environment in which locally hosted LLM agents move, communicate, respond to place occupancy, and encounter spatially localized fire events. SD-AgentFoundry-3D provides a three-dimensional digital-twin environment in which a locally hosted VLM receives first-person images and produces natural-language movement instructions. Both codebases are designed to run locally on macOS, Windows, and Linux and are deliberately left open to modification rather than developed as finished applications. Together, they offer accessible starting points for learning about generative social simulation and for building domain-specific extensions.

</details>


### [5] [Predicting the scale limits of social mechanisms in agent societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22884)
*Zengqing Wu,Chuan Xiao*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种审计方法，用于预测语言模型智能体社会中社会机制随种群规模扩大的有效性，为大规模社会模拟提供了前瞻性的机制筛选工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型智能体社会为大规模研究集体行为提供了可控可重复的方法，但社会机制在小群体中有效并不意味着在大规模群体中仍然有效，而直接测试大规模运行成本高昂，因此需要一种方法来预测机制在种群增长时的表现。

Method: 提出一种审计方法，通过评估三个关键因素来预测社会机制的规模效应：机制能够运作的频率、智能体是否使用其提供的信息、测量过程本身是否产生表面上的规模效应。通过控制实验分析互惠、共识、惩罚、流言等机制在不同规模下的表现。

Result: 单个结构项可以决定互惠、共识或惩罚机制在规模扩大时是否存续；流言机制失效的种群规模由消息的传播范围和生命周期决定；语言模型智能体不仅对社会信息内容有反应，还对信息表达方式敏感（计数和百分比导致不同的规模行为）；预测在第三方代码和第二个模型系列上得到验证，一个失败的预测揭示了发现的边界。

Conclusion: 该审计方法为判断哪些社会机制可以跨种群规模进行解释提供了前瞻性途径，能够有效预测社会机制在规模扩大后的命运，提高了语言模型社会模拟的科学价值和应用可靠性。

Abstract: Societies of interacting language-model agents offer a controllable and repeatable way to study collective behaviour at scales that would be difficult to test with people. Their scientific value, however, depends on whether a social mechanism that works in a small group still operates when thousands of agents interact, and testing this directly requires costly large-scale runs. Here we introduce an audit that predicts a mechanism's fate as a population grows. It asks how often the mechanism can act, whether agents use the information it supplies, and whether the measurement itself creates apparent scale effects. Controlled experiments show that a single structural term can decide whether reciprocity, consensus or punishment survives scaling. For gossip, the population at which the mechanism fails is set by the reach and lifetime of its messages. In language-model societies, agents respond not only to social information but to how it is expressed: counts and percentages led to different scale behaviour. Predictions made before execution held on third-party code and a second model family, while a failed prediction exposed the boundary of the finding. The audit provides a prospective way to decide which social mechanisms can be interpreted across population scales.

</details>


### [6] [First Demonstration of Multi-Agent LLM System for Million-Scale Optical Link Management in Global Production AIDCs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23145)
*Jingyi Su,Yihao Zhang,Dianxuan Fu,Leiyan Fei,Juan Wang,Mengfan Dai,Qing Liu,Xiong Wu,Yufeng Jiang,Cheng Chen,Bowen Zhang,Peilong Wang,Xi Chen,Zonglong He,Hongchen Yu,Zhicheng Ye,Weisheng Hu,Qunbi Zhuge*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个用于生产环境中数百万光链路自主故障管理的LLM多智能体系统，通过SFT和持续记忆演化，在十周现场数据评估中达到97.7% F1分数并减少超过60%的故障事件，超越了最先进LLM的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI数据中心（AIDC）规模的扩大，数百万光链路的故障管理面临巨大挑战。传统方法难以有效处理如此大规模的自动化故障管理需求，因此需要开发能够自主管理故障的智能系统。

Method: 提出了一个基于大语言模型（LLM）的多智能体系统，通过监督微调（SFT）和持续记忆演化技术进行优化，实现生产环境中数百万光链路的自主故障管理。

Result: 系统在十周现场数据评估中达到97.7%的F1分数，实现了超过60%的故障事件减少，性能超越了当前最先进的LLM方法。

Conclusion: 该LLM多智能体系统成功实现了大规模光链路的自主故障管理，证明了LLM在实际工业场景中的应用潜力，为AIDC的智能化运维提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: We present the first LLM-powered multi-agent system for autonomous fault management across millions of optical links in production AIDCs. Refined via SFT and continuous memory evolution, it achieves 97.7% F1 and over 60% fault-incident reduction, outperforming SOTA LLMs on a ten-week field data evaluation.

</details>


### [7] [Right-Sizing LLM-Agent Decomposition in VAT Determination: A Pilot Controlled Sweep](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23395)
*Pedro Santos*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文在跨境VAT确定任务上对比了单智能体与多智能体分解策略，发现中等粒度的智能体配置准确率最高（0.830），但未达到预设标准；单智能体并未帕累托占优，且性能差异可能与提示词预算相关；故障注入实验显示不同配置对错误类型有不同鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体系统在设计选择上存在分歧：是将任务分解给多个专用智能体，还是使用一个强能力的工具调用智能体？本研究旨在通过受控实验探索这一设计选择，填补智能体粒度设计的实证研究空白。

Method: 在跨境VAT确定任务（含反向收费）上进行对照实验：固定活动面（子任务、工具、I/O模式、验证、编排器、基座模型、合并策略），仅改变子任务到工作者的分配，测试四种编排配置（从1个宽工作者到5个窄工作者）与单智能体基线S0对比；使用确定性规则引擎作为oracle，共进行4,400次运行，包含40案例五重复主扫掠、匹配token臂、三组故障注入臂，并预设证伪标准。

Result: 中等粒度配置准确率领先（0.830 vs 端点0.720/0.770），但未达预设标准；单智能体未帕累托占优；匹配token标准触发：预算匹配的单智能体比领先者低6.5个百分点，但置信区间包含零；可用性故障在各粒度下被吸收，宽范围重启超基线+0.160；而一个符合模式的幻觉记录使所有配置退化并颠倒排序，碎片化配置受损最严重。

Conclusion: 提供了一个有界、预注册的试点启发式方法，用于确定合适的任务分解粒度（在依赖层中点放置一个分区边界），并发布oracle、数据集、测试框架、原始轨迹和分析流水线，为智能体系统设计提供实证指导。

Abstract: Recent LLM-agent systems make conflicting design bets: decompose work across many narrow agents, or use one strong tool-using agent. This pilot studies that choice on bounded cross-border VAT determination with reverse charge, where every case has an oracle label and each intermediate decision is independently scoreable. We hold the activity surface fixed (subtasks, tools, I/O schemas, validation checks, orchestrator, base model, and merge policy) and vary only the assignment of subtasks to workers across four orchestrated configurations, from one wide worker to five narrow ones, against S0, a tuned no-orchestrator single agent, with a deterministic rule engine as oracle. The program spans 4,400 runs: a 40-case, five-repeat main sweep, matched-token arms separating prompt-budget from agent-count effects, and three failure-injection arms, all judged against pre-registered falsification criteria. The two intermediate configurations lead on accuracy (0.830, against endpoints at 0.720 and 0.770) but miss the pre-stated bar against the fine endpoint, so the intermediate-optimum hypothesis remains unsupported at pilot scale. The single agent does not Pareto-dominate the orchestrated set. The matched-token criterion fires: the budget-matched single agent lands 6.5 points below the leader, but the interval includes zero, so any advantage is consistent with a prompt-budget explanation. Under injection, availability faults are absorbed at every granularity, with wide-scope restart over-recovering its baseline by +0.160, while one schema-conforming hallucinated record degrades every configuration and inverts the ordering, hitting fragmented configurations hardest. The contribution is a bounded, preregistered pilot heuristic for right-sizing decomposition (place one partition boundary at the dependency-layer midpoint), released with oracle, dataset, harness, raw traces, and analysis pipeline.

</details>


### [8] [The Interaction Tax: When Communication Erases Diversity in Multi-Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23541)
*Summer Eunhyung Ann,Haokun Liu,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM交互的效果存在矛盾：完全输出交互会导致多样性丧失（交互税），独立生成更有效；性能取决于交换的信息而非智能体数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究对多智能体LLM交互效果存在矛盾结论：部分研究显示辩论、批评循环和智能体混合能带来提升，而其他研究发现在相同预算下交互仅增加成本却不改善质量，或认为独立采样已能获得多智能体收益。作者指出这一矛盾源于缺少关键区分：并非所有多智能体通信都是等效的。

Method: 在匹配预算条件下测试11个验证器评分的优化任务。对比完全输出交互与独立提案生成的效果，分析智能体阅读彼此完整输出时解决方案趋同的现象。

Result: 完全输出交互会导致解决方案在一轮内收敛，丧失使用多模型的多样性动机（称为“交互税”）。完全输出交互是弱默认策略，智能体倾向于停留在首个看到的方案而非尝试不同方法。批评仅在违规规则易于LLM发现和修复时有效。独立提案生成避免了这种坍塌。

Conclusion: 多智能体性能更依赖于交换的信息内容而非智能体数量，交互仅在智能体在正确时机分享正确信息时才有帮助。

Abstract: Does multi-agent LLM interaction help or hurt? Some work reports gains from debate (Du et al., 2024), critique loops (Chen et al., 2025), and mixture-of-agents synthesis (Wang et al., 2025), while other work finds that interaction adds cost without improving quality under equal budgets (Tran & Kiela, 2026; Xu et al., 2026; Jarrett et al., 2025), or that independent sampling already captures multi-agent gains (Li et al., 2024). We argue this contradiction partly reflects a missing distinction, because not all multi-agent communication is equal. Different model families find structurally different solutions, but when agents read each other's complete outputs, their proposals converge within one round, erasing the diversity that motivates using multiple models. We call this the interaction tax. We test 11 verifier-scored optimization tasks under matched budgets and find that full-solution interaction is a weak default. Independent proposal generation avoids this collapse. Full-solution interaction mainly makes agents stay close to the first solution they see instead of trying different approaches, and critique helps only if the violated rule is easy for the LLM to find and fix. These results suggest that multi-agent performance depends less on the number of agents than on the information they exchange, and interaction helps only when agents share the right information at the right time.

</details>

### [9] [Programmable Compute-in-Transit using Integrated Photonics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21536)
*Imon Kundu,Livi Hammond,Jamie Todd,Kriti Goel,Peter Simpson,Jaganath Rajendra,Florent Michel,Jack Crawford,Flavio Bergamaschi,Robert Todd,Nick New*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: Optalysys展示了可编程光子硬件，能够在数据传输过程中直接进行数学运算，实现数十GFLOPs的计算性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代AI和密码学硬件设计将数据传输与处理分离，导致效率瓶颈。传统架构中数据需要在传输和处理单元之间来回移动，限制了整体性能提升。

Method: 开发可编程光子硬件，利用光学技术在数据传输路径上直接执行数学函数运算，实现"传输即计算"的新型处理范式。

Result: 成功演示了可在数据链路上执行数十GFLOPs操作的可编程光子计算硬件，证明了在数据传输过程中进行实时计算的可行性。

Conclusion: 该技术打破了传统数据传输与处理分离的架构限制，为AI和密码学应用提供了一种高效率、低延迟的新型计算方案。

Abstract: Modern hardware designs for AI and cryptography treat data transit and processing separately. At Optalysys we have demonstrated programmable Photonic hardware that computes mathematical functions on data that is in transit, enabling tens of GFLOPs of operations on data links.

</details>


### [10] [Model Compression and Hardware-Aware Acceleration for Deep Learning on FPGAs: A Co-Design Taxonomy and Comparative Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21657)
*Peter Forcha,H. Kajekusumadhar,Mbua Peter,Muhammed Kawser,Audrey Cyriell Mo,Christophe Bobda*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文系统调研了2015-2026年间25项FPGA上的压缩-硬件协同设计案例研究，建立了基于FPGA资源影响的五类分类法，并揭示了该领域在标准化评估指标（压缩比、精度变化、能效）方面存在严重的表征缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有跨平台研究仅停留在定性权衡层面，缺乏针对FPGA资源影响的具体分析。为填补这一空白，本文聚焦FPGA平台，旨在建立系统的分类体系并量化分析现有工作的实际效果与评估标准问题。

Method: 提出了基于FPGA资源重塑方式的五类分类法（DSP消除、DSP重用/混合精度、稀疏性利用、内存层次驱动、工具链/部署层），对25项案例研究在压缩比、精度变化、吞吐量、能效和资源利用率等维度进行了标准化元分析。

Result: 发现严重的表征缺口：25项工作中仅1项基于统一基准报告了压缩比和精度变化，仅2项基于统一GPU基准报告了能效。现有工具链（FINN、HLS4ML、Vitis AI、DNNWeaver）均无法单独解决此问题。

Conclusion: 归纳了六大开放挑战：工具链碎片化、精度-效率表征、自动化混合精度优化、稀疏计算可靠性、持续内存瓶颈、FPGA训练，并为每项挑战提供了基于现有技术的具体解决方案，而非泛泛的未来工作呼吁。

Abstract: Deploying deep neural networks on Field-Programmable Gate Arrays (FPGAs) requires joint reasoning about model compression and hardware acceleration, however the most comprehensive existing cross-platform treatment of this space, Deng et al.~\cite{deng2020model}, compared compression techniques against CPU, GPU, FPGA, and ASIC targets at the level of broad, qualitative trade-offs, and not specific FPGA resource consequences. This survey instead restricted the scope to FPGAs alone and organized 25 compression-hardware co-design case studies (2015--2026) into a five-category taxonomy defined by which FPGA resources each strategy primarily reshapes: DSP-eliminating, DSP-repurposing/mixed-precision, sparsity-exploiting, memory-hierarchy-driven, and toolchain/deployment-level. Normalizing these case studies along a common set of dimensions (compression ratio, accuracy change, throughput, energy efficiency, and DSP/LUT/BRAM utilization) surfaces a central, quantitative finding; of the 25 reviewed works, only \emph{one} reported a compression ratio and accuracy change measured against a single common baseline, and only \emph{two} reported energy efficiency normalized against a common GPU baseline, exposing a field-wide characterization gap that no individual toolchain (FINN, HLS4ML, Vitis AI, or DNNWeaver) resolves on its own. Building on this taxonomy and meta-analysis, we formalize six open challenges: toolchain fragmentation, accuracy--efficiency characterization, automated mixed-precision optimization, sparse computation reliability, persistent memory bottlenecks, and FPGA-based training. Each is paired with a concrete next step grounded in extending an existing, cited technique, not a general call for future work.

</details>


### [11] [TherMapNet Attention-Guided Runtime Full-Chip Thermal Map Prediction from Performance Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21887)
*Qin Gu,Chaofang Ma,Mingyu Yang,Yipu Zhang,Jiliang Zhang,Wei Zhang,Lin Jiang*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出TherMapNet，一种基于注意力机制的热仿真器，可直接从性能指标预测全芯片热图，无需中间功率估算步骤，实现了高精度（RMSE < 0.26°C）和快速推理（< 2.4 ms）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高性能芯片的运行时热管理依赖于快速准确的全芯片热图。传统热仿真器需要先从性能指标估算功耗轨迹，这增加了计算开销和延迟，无法满足运行时管理的实时性需求。

Method: 提出TherMapNet框架：使用Transformer编码器处理时间序列数据，将每个指标的时间序列视为token以捕获时序演化；采用CNN提取细粒度空间特征，并引入双分支通道-空间注意力卷积模块（DACM）和三元组损失来增强空间学习能力和重构精度。

Result: 在AMD Ryzen 7 4800U多核CPU和NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4060众核GPU上验证，TherMapNet优于现有热仿真器，RMSE低于0.26°C，在RTX 3090 GPU上推理时间少于2.4 ms。

Conclusion: TherMapNet能够为现代多核芯片提供高质量、实时的运行时热管理支持，解决了传统方法中功率估算带来的开销问题，实现了精度与速度的良好平衡。

Abstract: Runtime thermal management of high-performance chips depends on fast and accurate full-chip thermal maps. Conventional simulators typically estimate power traces from performance metrics first, which adds overhead. This work proposes TherMapNet, an attention-guided thermal simulator that predicts full-chip thermal maps directly from performance metrics. A Transformer encoder captures temporal evolution by treating the time series of each metric as a token, improving modeling of dynamic workloads. A CNN then extracts fine-grained spatial features. For the CNN, a dual-branch channel-spatial attention convolution module (DACM) and a triplet loss are used to improve spatial learning and reconstruction accuracy. TherMapNet is applied to a multi-core CPU (AMD Ryzen 7 4800U) and a many-core GPU (NVIDIA GeForce RTX 4060). Experiments show that it outperforms prior thermal simulators, with RMSE below 0.26 C and inference under 2.4 ms on an NVIDIA GeForce RTX 3090 GPU. These results indicate that TherMapNet can support high-quality runtime thermal management of modern multi-core chips.

</details>


### [12] [NoTB: Oracle-Free Triage of LLM-Generated RTL via Cross-Model Formal Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21962)
*Elisavet Lydia Alvanaki,Je Yang,Biruk Seyoum,Luca P. Carloni*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出NoTB框架，通过多模型形式化一致性验证LLM生成的RTL设计的正确性，无需测试平台即可实现高精度筛选。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型用于RTL设计生成日益增多，但早期功能正确性评估仍是根本挑战。现有无oracle方法依赖仿真一致性（需要LLM生成的测试平台，可能失败或因模型而异）或LLM作为评判者的启发式方法（产生不一致预测），缺乏可靠的无oracle验证手段。

Method: 引入NoTB无oracle分类框架：(1)从多个独立训练的LLM家族生成RTL实现；(2)应用序列等价性检查(SEC)识别可证明等价的设计；(3)利用SEC等价簇内模型家族的多样性产生校准的正确性信号，实现无需测试平台的风险-覆盖率权衡。

Result: 在78个CVDP RTL生成任务上：四家族形式化共识在27%覆盖率下达到94.7%精度；三家族共识在33%覆盖率下达到87%精度。为设计者提供了可调节的接受/延迟规则，在可信测试平台或黄金RTL可用之前进行决策。

Conclusion: 证明了跨模型形式化一致性为高置信度分类提供了可靠基础，无需依赖模型相关的oracle，为LLM生成RTL设计的早期验证提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate register-transfer-level (RTL) designs from natural-language specifications. However, assessing functional correctness at early stages remains a fundamental challenge. Existing oracle-free approaches rely either on simulation-based agreement, which depends on LLM-generated testbenches that can fail or vary across models, or on LLM-as-a-judge heuristics, which produce inconsistent predictions. We introduce NoTB, an oracle-free triage framework that infers correctness from cross-model formal consensus. NoTB generates RTL implementations from multiple independently trained LLM families and applies Sequential Equivalence Checking (SEC) to identify designs that are provably equivalent. We show that the diversity of model families within an SEC-equivalent cluster induces a calibrated correctness signal, enabling risk-coverage tradeoffs without requiring testbenches. On 78 CVDP RTL-generation tasks, four-family formal consensus achieves 94.7% precision at 27% coverage; three-family consensus achieves 87% precision at 33% coverage. These operating points give designers a tunable accept/defer rule before a trusted testbench or golden RTL is available. Overall, NoTB demonstrates that formal cross-model agreement provides a reliable basis for high-confidence triage without model-dependent oracles

</details>


### [13] [Precision-Aware Variable Bit Processing Elements for Hardware-Efficient Systolic Array Designs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22378)
*Dantu Nandini Devi,Madhav Rao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文研究脉动阵列中浮点乘法器的近似计算技术，通过部分积矩阵列截断和正负压缩器优化设计空间，在保持CNN精度的同时实现了显著的硬件改进（面积节省66-92%，功耗降低60-93%，延迟改善21-54%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脉动阵列已成为深度学习矩阵运算的主要硬件加速器，而浮点数格式支持跨计算域的精度控制。研究旨在探索权重固定脉动阵列中FP乘法器的近似计算技术，优化计算效率与精度之间的权衡。

Method: 将部分积矩阵（PPM）列截断与正负压缩器集成到FP乘法器架构中；采用NSGA-II优化算法探索设计空间；针对IEEE 754（FP32）、TensorFloat-32（TF32）和Brain Floating point（BF16）三种格式进行优化。

Result: 在MNIST、F-MNIST和CIFAR-10数据集上训练的CNN模型保持了可比拟的精度；CIFAR-10数据集上表现前10的FP近似SA设计实现了66%-92%面积节省、60%-93%功耗降低和21%-54%延迟改善；TF32和BF16近似SA设计也获得显著增益并保持可比较的CNN精度。

Conclusion: FP乘法器设计中的定向近似显著提高了容错应用中硬件加速器的效率，为现代计算架构中的硬件资源优化建立了有效方法。

Abstract: Systolic arrays (SAs) have emerged as prominent hardware accelerators for matrix operations in deep learning, while floating point number formats enable precision control across computational domains. This research investigates approximate computing techniques for floating point (FP) multipliers in Weight Stationary Systolic Arrays, focusing on IEEE 754 (FP32), TensorFloat-32 (TF32), and Brain Floating point (BF16) formats. By integrating partial product matrix (PPM) column truncation with positive and negative compressors in the FP multiplier architecture, we optimize the trade-off between computational efficiency and accuracy. NSGA-II optimization algorithm was employed to explore the vast design space for evolving FP multiplier designs, towards achieving substantial hardware improvements while maintaining acceptable output quality. Substantial hardware benefits were observed in the FP multiplier designs across various applications, while preserving output quality. The FP approximated Processing Elements designed in the SA was found to offer comparable CNN accuracy for models trained on MNIST, F-MNIST, and CIFAR-10 dataset. The FP approximated SA designs that fall in the top 10 CNN performance offered substantial hardware gains in the range of 66% to 92% footprint savings, 60% to 93% of power benefits with 21% to 54% improvement in the delay when compared with the corresponding exact implementations mentioned in the literature for running the model trained on CIFAR-10 dataset. The TF32 and BF16 approximated SA designs also achieved substantial gains while maintaining comparable CNN accuracy. Our findings confirm that targeted approximation in FP multiplier design significantly improves the efficiency of hardware accelerators for error-tolerant applications, establishing an effective approach to hardware resource optimization in contemporary computing architectures.

</details>


### [14] [Architecting the Next Generation of Asynchronous, Distributed GPUs for the AI Era](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22602)
*Junrui Pan,Weili An,Cesar Avalos Baddouh,Christin David Bose,Ni Kang,Aaron Barnes,Ahmad Alawneh,Fangjia Shen,Yechen Liu,Anusuya Nallathambi,Atthin Chandrashekar,Timothy G. Rogers*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个周期级GPU模拟框架，针对现代GPU架构（Ampere、Hopper、Blackwell）进行建模，在H100 GPU上实现99%的Pearson相关系数和13.4%的平均绝对周期误差，用于评估芯粒拓扑扩展、SRAM扩容和GPU间预取等新兴设计方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习工作负载的快速演进推动了GPU硬件向多芯片模块（MCM）拓扑、异步执行原语和持久化多阶段内核行为方向发展，然而周期级模拟基础设施严重滞后，缺乏对现代GPU物理非均匀性和大规模AI工作负载的原生建模能力。

Method: 开发了周期级模拟框架，能够原生建模现代GPU代际架构（Ampere、Hopper、Blackwell）的物理非均匀性，并通过物理硅片进行严格验证。

Result: 模拟器在H100 GPU上达到99%的Pearson相关系数和13.4%的平均绝对周期误差，证明了其高精度建模能力。

Conclusion: 该框架成功弥合了模拟基础设施与现代GPU硬件发展之间的差距，通过架构案例研究评估了芯粒拓扑扩展、SRAM容量与带宽扩展、以及GPU间预取策略等新兴设计轨迹。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of machine learning workloads has fundamentally transformed GPU hardware, driving architectures toward Multi-Chip Module (MCM) topologies, asynchronous execution primitives, and persistent, multi-phase kernel behaviors. Despite these shifts, cycle-level simulation infrastructure has lagged behind, lacking the native capability to model the physical non-uniformity of modern GPUs alongside the massive scale of state-of-the-art AI workloads. To bridge this gap, we present a cycle-level simulation framework designed to accurately model modern GPU generations, including Ampere, Hopper, and Blackwell. Rigorously validated against physical silicon, the simulator achieves a 99% Pearson correlation coefficient and a 13.4% mean absolute cycle error on the H100 GPU. Utilizing this infrastructure, we conduct architectural case studies to evaluate emerging design trajectories, including chiplet topology scaling, expanded SRAM capacity and bandwidth, and inter-GPU prefetching strategies.

</details>


### [15] [NOVA: Technology-Architecture Co-Design of Near-Memory Processing for Attention-SSM-MoE Hybrid LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22613)
*In-Jun Jung,Jaeha Min,Joo-Young Kim*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: NOVA是一种技术-架构协同设计的近存计算系统，通过4F² VCT DRAM单元实现约2倍存储密度，并采用双层NMP架构支持混合LLM的异构计算，在多个主流LLM上实现平均4.5倍吞吐量提升、69.8%延迟降低和5倍能效提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合大语言模型（结合GQA、SSM和MoE层）对近存计算架构带来两大挑战：技术壁垒——传统6F² DRAM单元在10nm节点接近物理极限，难以满足MoE模型数百个专家的存储容量需求；架构壁垒——现有NMP设计针对狭窄的计算强度范围，无法高效支持混合LLM跨越存储受限SSM层、计算密集型GQA层及专家间大Op/B变化的异构计算特性。

Method: 技术上，NOVA结合4F²垂直通道晶体管(VCT) DRAM单元与单元上方外围(POC)结构，在等面积下实现约2倍存储密度，支持sub-10nm节点继续缩放；架构上，重新利用POC外围裸片承载处理单元，形成双层NMP架构：Tier-1（外围裸片NMP）处理低至中Op/B操作，Tier-2（基底裸片NMP）处理中至高Op/B操作，通过跨层并行执行支持混合LLM的多样化计算模式。

Result: 在包括Nemotron3-Nano、Nemotron3-Super、Falcon-H1R和Qwen3等最先进的混合和MoE LLM上评估，NOVA相比GPU基线平均实现4.5倍更高吞吐量、69.8%更低的端到端延迟和5倍更好的能效，仅增加3.9%面积开销且无存储容量损失。

Conclusion: NOVA通过技术-架构协同设计成功突破了混合LLM在近存计算中面临的技术与架构双重壁垒，为未来LLM推理加速提供了一种高效的解决方案，证明了新型存储技术与分层计算架构协同优化的有效性。

Abstract: The rapid evolution of hybrid large language models (LLMs), which interleave grouped-query-attention (GQA), state-space model (SSM), and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layers, introduces two fundamental challenges for near-memory processing (NMP) architectures. First, the Technology Wall: the conventional 6F^2 DRAM cell is approaching its physical scaling limits at 10nm-class nodes, making it difficult to meet the memory capacity demands of MoE models with hundreds of experts. Second, the Architecture Wall: existing NMP designs target narrow arithmetic intensity (Op/B) ranges and cannot efficiently support the heterogeneous compute characteristics of hybrid LLMs, spanning memory-bound SSM layers, compute-intensive GQA layers, and large Op/B variations across experts. We propose NOVA, a technology-architecture co-designed NMP system that overcomes both walls. On the technology side, NOVA combines a 4F^2 vertical channel transistor (VCT) DRAM cell with a peri-over-cell (POC) structure to achieve approximately 2x memory density at iso-area over conventional 6F^2-based DRAM, enabling continued scaling into sub-10nm nodes. On the architecture side, NOVA repurposes the POC peripheral-die (peri-die) to host processing units, forming a 2-tier NMP architecture: Tier-1 (peri-die NMP) for low-to-mid Op/B operations, and Tier-2 (base-die NMP) for mid-to-high Op/B operations. Parallel execution across tiers supports diverse compute patterns for hybrid LLMs, maximizing inference performance. Evaluated on state-of-the-art hybrid and MoE LLMs including Nemotron3-Nano, Nemotron3-Super, Falcon-H1R, and Qwen3, NOVA achieves on average 4.5x higher throughput, 69.8% lower end-to-end latency, and 5x better energy efficiency over a GPU baseline, with only 3.9% area overhead and no loss in memory capacity.

</details>


### [16] [A Process-Aware Hybrid Si/IGO Monolithic-3D 6T SRAM with BEOL Pass-Gates for the 2nm Node](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22741)
*Po-Chuan Wang,Dongwon Jang,Eknath Sarkar,Alexei Svizhenko,Md. Nahid Haque Shazon,Piyush Kumar,Suman Datta,Azad Naeemi*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于2nm节点的单片三维（M3D）6T SRAM设计，通过将BEOL IGO传输管与全硅纳米片锁存器集成，实现了比传统硅基线面积减小25%，写入延迟降低42.2%，能耗延迟积（EDP）改善9.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着工艺节点推进至2nm，传统硅基SRAM面临尺寸缩放极限和性能瓶颈。为突破传统平面工艺的限制，本研究旨在通过单片三维集成技术，利用BEOL氧化物半导体晶体管（IGO）与硅纳米片器件的异构集成，实现SRAM单元的面积缩减和性能提升。

Method: 采用TCAD仿真校准双层门IGO晶体管（三层HZH栅极堆叠），结合虚拟制造和三维寄生参数提取，实现了首个工艺感知的M3D 6T SRAM布局设计。关键技术包括：BEOL IGO传输管与全硅纳米片锁存器的集成、埋入式电源轨道（BPR）、Ru互连、以及创新的邻居单元共享源/漏位线设计（扩大IGO接触面积以降低接触电阻）。

Result: 相比高性能（HP）122 Si基线，单元面积减小25%，并在宽电压范围内保持稳定的静态噪声容限（SNM）。在128×256子阵列层面，相比高密度（HD）111 Si基线，写入延迟降低42.2%，能耗延迟积（EDP）改善9.7%，这归因于减小的单元寄生参数和更小面积带来的字线负载降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的M3D 6T SRAM架构通过创新的共享位线设计和工艺感知布局优化，成功实现了面积缩减与性能提升的双重目标，证明了IGO传输管与硅纳米片锁存器单片三维集成的可行性，为未来2nm及以下节点的SRAM设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: We propose a monolithic-3D (M3D) 6T SRAM at the 2nm node, integrating BEOL IGO pass-gates (PGs) with an all-silicon nanosheet latch, buried power rails (BPRs), and Ru interconnects. TCAD calibrated to a state-of-the-art double-gate IGO transistor with a tri-layer HfO$_2$/ZrO$_2$/HfO$_2$ (HZH) gate stack is combined with virtual fabrication and 3D parasitic extraction to realize the first process-aware layout of this topology. A novel neighbor-cell shared source/drain (S/D) bitline (BL) design enlarges the IGO contact area to mitigate contact resistance and restore PG drive without area penalty. The resulting cell achieves a 25\% footprint reduction vs the high-performance (HP) 122 Si baseline while maintaining robust static noise margin (SNM) over a wide supply voltage range. At the 128$\times$256 subarray-level, it reduces write delay by 42.2\% and EDP by 9.7\% compared to the high-density (HD) 111 Si baseline, owing to reduced cell parasitics and wordline (WL) loading from the smaller footprint.

</details>


### [17] [SYNTLOG: FSM Benchmarks Evaluation for FPGA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23288)
*Samary Baranov,Danila Gorodecky*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个包含101个有限状态机（FSM）描述的基准测试集，并开发了架构无关的综合工具SYNTLOG。相比Xilinx Vivado，SYNTLOG在LUT数量上减少45%-66%，逻辑深度更优，运行速度快1-2个数量级，能成功处理Vivado无法完成的大型设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FPGA综合工具在处理大型有限状态机时存在效率低下甚至无法完成的问题，缺乏系统的基准测试来评估不同综合工具的性能。需要一个架构无关的综合工具来提高FSM综合的效率和效果，并提供标准化的基准测试集用于公平比较。

Method: 构建了包含101个FSM描述的基准测试集，按规模分为五个类别（小型、中型、大型、巨型、超巨型），涵盖数十到数千个状态。开发了架构无关的综合工具SYNTLOG，支持面积优化和延迟优化两种模式，并在六种优化策略下与Xilinx Vivado进行技术映射层面的对比实验，评估三个后综合参数：LUT数量、逻辑层级数和运行时间。

Result: SYNTLOG在面积优化模式下LUT使用量比Vivado面积基准减少45%至66%；在延迟优化模式下逻辑深度优于Vivado默认综合模式；运行速度比Vivado快1至2个数量级；对于巨型和超巨型电路，SYNTLOG能在几分钟内完成综合和验证，而Vivado在数小时后仍未终止。

Conclusion: SYNTLOG在处理各种规模的FSM设计时均显著优于现有商业工具，特别是在大型设计方面展现出明显优势，为FSM综合提供了更高效的解决方案。该基准测试集为未来工具评估提供了标准化平台。

Abstract: We introduce a curated benchmark collection of \num{101} FSM descriptions organized into five size classes (\emph{small}, \emph{medium}, \emph{large}, \emph{huge}, \emph{super-huge}), spanning tens to thousands of states and up to hundreds of inputs and outputs. Using these benchmarks we compare an architecture-independent synthesis tool SYNTLOG against Xilinx Vivado at the synthesis (technology-mapping) level, under six optimization strategies. We compare three post-synthesis parameters: number of LUTs, number of logic levels, and runtime. SYNTLOG in area-driven mode uses substantially fewer LUTs than Vivado's area baseline at every size ($-45\%$ to $-66\%$), and its delay-driven mode is simultaneously shallower in logic depth than Vivado's default synthesis mode. SYNTLOG synthesis includes embedded functional validation. Runtime of SYNTLOG is faster by one to two orders of magnitude than Vivado synthesis. The proposed tool synthesizes and validates every \emph{huge} and \emph{super-huge} circuit in minutes, whereas Vivado fails to terminate on these designs after hours.

</details>


### [18] [VIPER: Architecture-Aware Performance Modeling for Processing-in-Memory Design-Space Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23404)
*Haoran Geng,Tomas Sousa Pereira,Xiaoyang Lu,Xian-He Sun,Michael Niemier,X. Sharon Hu*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: VIPER是一个轻量级、架构感知的PIM性能评估框架，通过一次性主机性能分析与PIM感知解析引擎相结合，在无需周期精确仿真的情况下实现快速准确的端到端性能预测，相比传统方法将评估时间从数小时缩短至一分钟内，误差控制在10-12%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PIM评估方法存在明显局限：电路/器件级工具无法捕捉端到端性能因素（主机执行、数据传输、容量限制、编程延迟等），而周期精确仿真速度过慢无法支持迭代的早期设计空间探索（DSE），导致难以快速评估不同PIM设计配置的实际性能收益。

Method: VIPER采用主机端一次性性能剖析，将测得的主机行为与PIM感知解析引擎相结合。该框架支持近存计算（PNM）和存内计算（PUM）两种架构，涵盖任务卸载和数据触发两种执行模式，通过建模主机-PIM数据传输、阵列访问、存内计算、器件编程延迟和容量分区等关键因素，在候选设计空间中快速扫描PIM侧参数，提供架构感知的性能估计。

Result: 在UPMEM商用系统和400多种gem5周期精确仿真配置上验证，VIPER能准确预测UPMEM卸载决策和盈亏平衡区域；改进的传输模型捕捉了峰值-下降行为，DPU扫描范围内平均加速误差为12%（256-DPU峰值前误差为6%）；对比gem5误差小于10%，评估时间从数小时缩减至一分钟内。案例研究表明架构感知DSE能揭示器件级评估忽略的关键性能权衡。

Conclusion: VIPER填补了PIM设计空间探索领域的关键空白，首次提供了兼具速度与精度的统一评估框架。该框架使设计者能在早期快速探索大规模设计空间，识别最优配置，避免了传统方法耗时长或精度不足的问题，对UPMEM、ReRAM/FeFET交叉阵列和IMCRYPTO等多样化PIM架构具有实用价值。

Abstract: Processing-in-Memory (PIM) promises to reduce data movement overhead by executing computation in or near memory, but its realized application speedup remains highly design-dependent. Non-offloadable host execution, host-PIM transfers, limited PIM capacity, and device programming latency can limit end-to-end speedup, making fast early-stage design-space exploration (DSE) essential. However, existing PIM evaluation methods remain limited: circuit- and device-level tools cannot capture these end-to-end PIM performance factors, while cycle-accurate simulation is too slow for iterative DSE. To address this gap, we present VIPER, a unified, lightweight, and architecture-aware performance evaluation framework for PIM DSE. VIPER profiles host execution once and combines the measured host behavior with a PIM-aware analytical engine that sweeps PIM-side parameters across candidate designs. It supports both Processing Near Memory (PNM) and Processing Using Memory (PUM) under task-offloading and data-triggered execution by capturing host-PIM transfer, array access, in-memory computation, device programming latency, and capacity-induced partitioning, providing rapid architecture-aware performance estimates for iterative DSE without repeated cycle-accurate simulation. We validate VIPER against a commercial UPMEM system and more than 400 cycle-accurate gem5 configurations. VIPER predicts the UPMEM offloading decision and break-even region a priori, and, with a refined transfer model, captures the measured peak-and-rolloff behavior with 12\% mean speedup error across the DPU sweep (6\% up to the 256-DPU peak). Against gem5, VIPER achieves less than 10\% error while reducing evaluation time from hours to under one minute. Case studies of UPMEM, ReRAM/FeFET crossbars, and IMCRYPTO show that architecture-aware DSE reveals key performance trade-offs that device-level evaluation misses.

</details>

### [19] [PowerSlider: Exploiting Phase Asymmetry for LLM Serving under Demand Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21719)
*Yueying Li,Jiayang Chen,Yuanfan Chen,Leo Han,Haoran Qiu,Esha Choukse,Rodrigo Fonseca,Udit Gupta*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对AI推理集群的动态功率管理方法，在功率上限变化时保持高吞吐量和低延迟，相比现有基线方法在30%功率削减下吞吐量提升1.64倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI推理集群受到瞬时功率限制的约束，电网运营商对新增容量要求需求响应，施加时变的功率上限。现有的LLM服务系统要么优化静态能源目标，要么在负载下丢弃固定优先级层级，当功率包络变化时有效吞吐量会急剧下降。LLM流水线不是均匀负载：计算密集的预填充阶段随GPU频率几乎线性损失吞吐量，内存受限的解码阶段在降至标称频率0.57倍时仍能维持吞吐量，而推理的思考阶段将KV缓存容量与调度耦合。

Method: 系统采用三个核心机制：(1)新的Flex SLO合约，将有界的用户松弛时间转化为优化约束；(2)预填充-思考-回答解耦，暴露每阶段的频率和KV控制；(3)Karush-Kuhn-Tucker (KKT)在线求解器，在每个功率上限变化后7.7毫秒内重新求解，并配有综合故障安全机制，当DVFS在静态功耗上触底时对已排空的实例进行功率门控。

Result: 在SGLang上使用生产负载跟踪测试，系统在30%功率削减下维持78.3%的在线吞吐量，而五个基线中最佳仅为47.6%（提升1.64倍）。延迟关键尾延迟保持在标称值的1.3倍以内（基线为2.3-6倍，最高达12倍）。在重放的CAISO电网紧急日测试中，系统在最低点0.41倍功率时仍保持92%的平均吞吐量（低谷时54%；所有基线均低于7%）。

Conclusion: 该系统成功实现了在动态功率限制下的高效LLM推理服务，通过细粒度的阶段解耦和实时优化显著优于现有方法，证明了将功率上限引导到每瓦性能成本最低的位置的有效性。

Abstract: AI inference clusters are increasingly constrained by instantaneous power, not just energy: grid operators condition new capacity on demand response, imposing time-varying power caps. Existing LLM serving systems optimize a static energy objective or shed fixed priority tiers under load; either way, goodput collapses when the power envelope moves. An LLM pipeline is not a uniform load: compute-bound prefill loses throughput almost linearly with GPU frequency, memory-bound answer decode sustains it down to $0.57\times$ nominal, and reasoning's thinking phase couples KV-cache capacity to scheduling -- so a cap should be steered to where each watt costs the least performance. \sys{} does so with a new Flex SLO contract that turns bounded user slack into an optimization constraint, prefill--think--answer disaggregation exposing per-stage frequency and KV control, and a Karush--Kuhn--Tucker (KKT) online solver re-solving within 7.7 ms of every cap change, backed by a consolidated fail-safe that power-gates drained instances when DVFS bottoms out on static power. On SGLang with production traces, \sys{} sustains 78.3\% online goodput at a 30\% cap reduction versus 47.6\% for the best of five baselines ($1.64\times$), holds latency-critical tails within $1.3\times$ of nominal (baselines: $2.3$--$6\times$, up to $12\times$), and delivers 92\% mean goodput through a replayed CAISO grid-emergency day bottoming at $0.41\times$ (54\% at the trough; every baseline below 7\%).

</details>


### [20] [Rapid Earthquake-to-Tsunami Waveform Generation via Large-Scale Multi-GPU FFT Convolution Applied to the Cascadia Subduction Zone](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21763)
*Bowen Shi,Sreeram Venkat,Stefan Henneking,Omar Ghattas*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于线性时不变系统特性的高效计算框架，通过预计算格林函数和伴随响应，将高保真地震和海啸模拟的计算成本大幅降低，实现了毫秒级的波形生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动的地震和海啸预警方法依赖于大量破裂情景及其波形的集成，但使用传统高保真模拟方法重复生成这些数据集的计算成本极其昂贵，成为制约此类方法应用的主要瓶颈。

Method: 利用地震和海啸动力学的线性时不变特性，预计算弹性格林函数和声重力伴随响应，将震源到波形的映射简化为两个连续的卷积算子。采用分布式FFT加速的GPU流水线，将大型海底网格分区到多个GPU上，直接生成最终观测波形。

Result: 在Cascadia俯冲带模型（963个子断层、241.6万个海底网格点、64个观测点、256个时间步长）上验证，聚合GPU内存需求9.45 TiB。在单个NVL72域内的64个GB200 GPU上，响应算子驻留后每个破裂情景的波形生成仅需24毫秒，支持在几分钟内评估大量破裂情景集合。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了数据驱动预警系统中大规模情景模拟的计算瓶颈，显著提高了计算效率，为快速生成大规模高保真地震和海啸波形数据集提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Data-driven methods for earthquake and tsunami early warning rely on large ensembles of rupture scenarios and their resulting waveforms, but generating such datasets with repeated high-fidelity seismic and tsunami simulations is prohibitively expensive. We exploit the linear time-invariant structure of both dynamics to precompute elastic Green's functions and acoustic-gravity adjoint responses, reducing the source-to-waveform map to two consecutive convolution operators. We evaluate these convolutions with a distributed, FFT-accelerated GPU pipeline that partitions the large seafloor grid across GPUs and directly generates the final observation waveforms. We demonstrate the scalability of this pipeline for the Cascadia Subduction Zone with 963 subfaults, 2,416,530 seafloor grid points, 64 observation locations, and 256 timesteps, requiring 9.45 TiB of aggregate GPU memory. On 64 GB200 GPUs within one NVL72 domain, the pipeline generates waveforms in 24 ms per rupture once the response operators are resident, enabling large rupture ensembles to be evaluated within minutes.

</details>


### [21] [A Concurrent Queue System for Multi-GPU Platforms: Application to Bellman-Ford SSSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21826)
*Beyza Cavusoglu*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文基于NVSHMEM设计了首个多GPU并发队列系统，在四卡A100上实现最大3.92倍加速，应用于Bellman-Ford算法获得3.03倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多GPU并发队列系统在并行计算中具有重要价值，但目前缺乏有效的实现方案。Bellman-Ford算法作为典型应用案例，其多GPU实现尚未出现，存在性能优化需求。

Method: 利用NVIDIA的NVSHMEM技术设计并实现了多GPU并发FIFO队列系统，以Bellman-Ford单源最短路径算法作为案例研究进行性能评估。

Result: 在四块NVIDIA A100 GPU上，多GPU队列实现相比单GPU基线获得最大3.92倍、平均3.04倍加速；应用于Bellman-Ford SSSP算法时，在10个不同类型图数据上获得最大3.03倍、平均2.65倍加速。

Conclusion: 成功实现了首个基于NVSHMEM的多GPU并发队列系统，证明了其在实际应用中的有效性和可扩展性，为多GPU并行计算提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents the design and implementation of a multi-GPU concurrent queue system using NVIDIA's NVSHMEM. The Bellman-Ford algorithm is used as a case study to evaluate the performance of the proposed concurrent FIFO queue, with this multi-GPU implementation being the first known instance of its kind. Experimental results demonstrate that the multi-GPU queue implementation achieves a maximum speedup of 3.92x and an average speedup of 3.04x over the singleGPU baseline on four NVIDIA A100 GPUs. When applied to the Bellman-Ford Single-Source Shortest Path (SSSP) algorithm, the multi-GPU system achieves a maximum speedup of 3.03x and an average speedup of 2.65x compared to the single-GPU implementation, tested on 10 graphs of different kinds taken from the SuiteSparse Matrix Collection.

</details>


### [22] [PRISM: Predictive Runtime In-place Scaling and Model Selection for Edge Microservices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21910)
*Uwe Gropengießer,Thomas Reuter,Dominik Schön,Osama Abboud,Xun Xiao,Max Mühlhäuser*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: PRISM是一个预测引导的运行时框架，通过联合选择模型变体和CPU分配，在满足延迟要求的同时显著降低边缘AI微服务的能耗，在ALPR管道中实现了高达36%的能耗节省。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态CPU配置存在资源浪费问题，因为推理成本在不同输入、模型变体和运行时条件之间存在显著差异。延迟敏感的边缘AI服务需要在严格的截止时间、输出质量和有限的计算及能耗预算之间取得平衡。

Method: 提出了PRISM框架，利用容器级能耗监控和轻量级回归模型，在满足截止时间、资源和离线模型级QoR约束的前提下，对每个流水线阶段进行就地自适应，联合选择模型变体和CPU分配，以最小化预测的CPU封装能耗。

Result: 在包含52,000多个请求的自动车牌识别(ALPR)管道上评估PRISM。在检测阶段，与最强静态配置相比，PRISM在保持相似成功率且使用不到一半平均CPU分配的情况下，能耗降低36%。在识别阶段，以更低的平均CPU分配达到接近静态最优的性能。

Conclusion: 预测性就地自适应是使时间敏感的AI微服务管道在边缘更加节能的实用机制，证明了动态资源管理的有效性。

Abstract: Latency-sensitive edge AI services must balance strict deadlines, output quality, and limited compute and energy budgets. However, static CPU provisioning wastes resources because inference cost varies substantially across inputs, model variants, and runtime conditions. We present PRISM, a prediction-guided runtime framework that jointly selects model variants and CPU allocations for containerized edge microservices. Using container-level energy monitoring and lightweight regression models, PRISM adapts each pipeline stage in place and minimizes predicted CPU-package energy under deadline, resource, and offline model-level Quality of Result (QoR) constraints. We evaluate PRISM on more than 52,000 requests in an Automatic License Plate Recognition (ALPR) pipeline with detection and recognition stages. For detection, PRISM reduces energy consumption by 36 % compared to the strongest static configuration while preserving a comparable success rate and using less than half of the average CPU allocation. For recognition, it reaches near-static-best performance with lower average CPU allocation. These results show that predictive in-place adaptation is a practical mechanism for making time-sensitive AI microservice pipelines more energy-efficient at the edge.

</details>


### [23] [Systematization of Knowledge: Formal Verification of Consensus Protocols](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21935)
*Nikita Bondarev,Kirill Ziborov,Yury Yanovich*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文系统化分析了20多个已验证的区块链共识协议，建立了验证方法的统一分类体系，揭示了活性验证不足、规范与实现脱节、可扩展性受限等关键差距，并提供了工具选择建议和研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链共识协议的形式化验证至关重要，因为细微的漏洞可能导致不可逆转的经济损失和网络故障。然而，现有验证方法文献在工具、协议族和属性类别方面呈现碎片化，阻碍了累积性进展，需要建立统一的知识体系来指导研究和实践。

Method: 系统化分析了超过20个已验证的共识协议，涵盖从崩溃容错的Raft到拜占庭容错的HotStuff、基于DAG的FairDAG和权益证明的Beacon Chain等。引入了从非形式化推理到机器检查代码证明的验证成熟度量表，并构建了协议-属性-方法矩阵，将协议映射到已验证的安全性、活性和经济属性。

Result: 建立了验证方法的统一分类体系和验证成熟度量表，识别出三个持续存在的差距：活性验证仍然不成熟，尽管其对进展保证很重要；规范与实现的脱节削弱了实际保证；可扩展性限制将验证局限于小型网络。

Conclusion: 本文为研究人员和从业者提供了工具选择和证明工程的实用建议，制定了面向可扩展、经济感知验证的研究路线图，旨在指导构建更严格验证的共识系统，推动区块链共识协议验证领域的累积性发展。

Abstract: Formal verification is increasingly critical for blockchain consensus protocols, where subtle bugs can cause irreversible financial loss and network failure. Yet the literature on verification methods is fragmented across tools, protocol families, and property classes, hindering cumulative progress. This Systematization of Knowledge paper analyzes over 20 verified consensus protocols--from crash-fault-tolerant Raft to Byzantine-fault-tolerant HotStuff, DAG-based FairDAG, and proof-of-stake Beacon Chain--to establish a unified taxonomy of verification approaches. We introduce a verification maturity scale ranging from informal reasoning to machine-checked code proofs, and present a Protocol--Property--Method matrix mapping protocols to verified safety, liveness, and economic properties. Our analysis reveals persistent gaps: liveness verification remains underdeveloped despite its importance for progress guarantees; specification-implementation disconnects undermine real-world assurance; and scalability limits restrict verification to small networks. We provide practical recommendations for tool selection and proof engineering, and outline a research roadmap toward scalable, economically-aware verification. This work aims to guide both researchers and practitioners in building more rigorously verified consensus systems.

</details>


### [24] [Scalable Exact Path Selection via Structure-Aware Search for Virtual Payment Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22276)
*Jiangnan Luo,Zhebei Shen,Yuan Zhang,Sheng Zhong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于四叉树搜索的结构感知精确求解器，用于在支付通道网络中选择最优的虚拟支付通道路径。通过利用单调性和距离高原特性，该方法在保持最优性的同时大幅剪枝搜索空间，实现了比现有方法快2-5个数量级的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟支付通道能够实现支付通道网络中的高效链下交易，但其性能依赖于选择高质量的底层路径。现有方法要么依赖简化指标导致效果不佳，要么计算成本过高难以实用，因此需要一种既能保证最优性又能高效计算的路径选择方法。

Method: 在广义单调度量框架下，提出了一种基于四叉树搜索的结构感知精确求解器。该方法利用单调性和距离高原特性对容量约束的搜索空间进行大规模剪枝，同时保证最优性，从而显著减少最短路径计算次数。同时提出了一个集成经济成本和安全风险的组合度量，支持跨应用场景的灵活权衡。

Result: 在合成图和真实世界闪电网络拓扑（最大12,552个节点）上的实验表明，该方法比现有工作实现了2-5个数量级的加速，延迟始终保持在100毫秒以下，展现出卓越的实用性能。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了虚拟支付通道路径选择中的效率和最优性权衡问题，在保证解的最优性的同时实现了显著的性能提升，为支付通道网络的实际部署提供了可行的解决方案。组合度量的引入使得系统能够灵活适应不同应用场景的需求。

Abstract: Virtual Payment Channels (VPCs) enable efficient off-chain transactions in Payment Channel Networks (PCNs), but their performance depends on selecting high-quality underlying paths. Existing approaches either rely on simplified metrics or incur high computational cost.
  We study VPC path selection under generalized monotone metrics and propose a structure-aware exact solver based on quadtree search. By exploiting monotonicity and distance plateau properties, our method prunes large regions of the capacity-constrained search space while preserving optimality, significantly reducing the number of shortest-path computations.
  We further instantiate the framework with a composite metric that integrates economic cost and security risk, enabling flexible trade-offs across application scenarios. Experiments on synthetic graphs and real-world Lightning Network topologies (up to 12,552 nodes) show 2--5 orders of magnitude speedup over prior work, with consistent sub-100ms latency.

</details>


### [25] [Non-Leaking Concurrent Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22288)
*Hagit Attiya,Rotem Oshman,Noa Schiller,Corentin Travers*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于认知逻辑的框架，用于分析并发实现中的信息泄露问题，并研究了无泄露实现的可行性与局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发对象的抽象规范虽然限定了操作的返回值，但实现中的内部协调机制可能会泄露额外的信息（如其他操作的参数或存在性），因此需要一种系统的方法来识别和防止此类信息泄露。

Method: 使用认知逻辑（epistemic logic）构建分析框架，通过比较进程在抽象规范下与具体实现中可能获得的知识来检测信息泄露，并以抽象对象本身作为允许观察的参考基准。

Result: 成功实现了多值寄存器和有界最大寄存器的完全无泄露无等待实现；证明了无界最大寄存器无法基于有限状态对象实现完全无泄露的无等待版本；获得了栈、队列和近似一致性的参数无泄露（argument-non-leaking）实现。

Conclusion: 无泄露保证通常可以与正确性和进展要求兼容，但也存在固有的局限性；不同强度的无泄露属性可以根据具体数据结构选择性地实现。

Abstract: Abstract specifications of concurrent objects determine which values operations may return, but they also implicitly constrain which information operations may know, for example the arguments of other operations that do not affect their outcome, or even whether such operations occurred. Concrete implementations, while correct with respect to the abstract specification, may nonetheless expose additional information through their internal coordination mechanisms.
  We introduce a framework for reasoning about information leakage in concurrent implementations. The framework uses epistemic logic to compare what a process may know under an abstract specification with what it may know in a concrete implementation, using the abstract object itself as the reference for permissible observations. This yields several notions of non-leaking implementations.
  Using this framework, we investigate both the possibilities and limitations of non-leaking implementations. We present fully-non-leaking wait-free implementations of multi-valued registers and bounded max registers, but show that a fully-non-leaking unbounded max register cannot be implemented in a wait-free manner from finite-state base objects. We then consider a weaker guarantee, obtaining argument-non-leaking implementations of stacks, queues, and approximate agreement. These results demonstrate that non-leakage guarantees are often compatible with correctness and progress requirements, while also indicating their limitations.

</details>


### [26] [Understanding the Synchronization Tax in GPU Scale-Up Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22503)
*Arjun Devraj,Lindsey Bowen,Rachee Singh*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 研究发现GPU规模扩展域中存在严重的同步开销（占集合通信时间50%以上），主要由GEMM内核执行时间跨rank差异导致，该开销随域规模增长，从根本上限制了带宽扩展的收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习基础设施中，GPU规模扩展域的设计呈现互联带宽和域规模指数增长的趋势。本文指出这两个趋势之间存在矛盾：尽管在相同硬件上执行相同内核，但GPU到达集合屏障的时间存在数百到数千微秒的差异，这种等待时间（同步税）严重影响了通信效率。

Method: 1) 在4个语言模型和3种最新GPU架构上分析数十万次集合操作；2) 设计基于图的算法分析per-rank内核追踪数据；3) 应用极值理论建模内核执行时间变异；4) 将模型融入增强的Hockney通信成本模型进行理论分析。

Result: 1) 8-GPU规模扩展域中同步税消耗超过50%的集合通信时间；2) 跨rank的GEMM内核执行时间差异占该开销的78%；3) 同步税随域规模增长；4) 同步税从根本上限制了带宽扩展的回报，颠覆了关于互联带宽应如何随域规模扩展的传统认知。

Conclusion: GPU规模扩展域中的同步开销是一个根本性限制因素，主要由计算内核执行时间变异引起。这一发现挑战了传统的带宽扩展策略，表明需要重新思考互联带宽与域规模之间的扩展关系。

Abstract: GPU scale-up domains have become the building block of modern machine learning infrastructure, and their design follows a clear trajectory of exponential growth in both interconnect bandwidth and domain size. This paper argues that these two trends are in tension. Through a study of several hundred thousand collective operations across four language models and three recent GPU architectures, we find that GPUs within a scale-up domain arrive at collective barriers hundreds to thousands of microseconds apart, despite executing identical kernels on identical hardware over a uniform fabric. We call this waiting time the synchronization tax and show that it can consume over 50% of collective communication time in an 8-GPU scale-up domain. To understand the sources of this tax, we design a graph-based algorithm that operates on per-rank kernel traces, revealing that cross-rank variation in GEMM kernel execution times accounts for 78% of this overhead. We apply extreme value theory to model this variation and demonstrate that the synchronization tax grows with domain size. Folding this model into an augmented Hockney communication cost model, we show that the synchronization tax fundamentally limits the return on bandwidth scaling and inverts prevailing beliefs about how interconnect bandwidth should scale with domain size.

</details>


### [27] [Model-Consistent Byzantine-Resilient Decentralized Federated Learning for Collaborative Missions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22552)
*Yue Li,Sudip Bhujel,Cameron Lira,Ning Wang,Yang Xiao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了DFL-C，一种新型抗拜占庭的去中心化联邦学习架构，通过集成ACS共识协议实现全局模型一致性，并采用双域信任评分机制抵御数据域拜占庭攻击，在保持模型准确性的同时显著提升了安全性和一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有去中心化联邦学习（DFL）方案无法保证全局模型一致性，这在需要决策统一性和安全性的关键任务场景中是严重问题。缺乏一致性还加剧了对拜占庭对手的脆弱性，攻击者利用去中心化网络拓扑和弱同步性实施等价攻击和模型投毒攻击。

Method: DFL-C的核心方法包括两部分：(1) 将异步公共子集(ACS)共识协议集成到DFL工作流中，确保所有节点聚合统一的模型更新集以建立全局模型一致性；(2) 实现双域信任评分机制，提供对数据域拜占庭操控（包括模型投毒攻击）的韧性，该机制补充共识协议并显著减少其运行时间。

Result: 实验结果表明DFL-C在拜占庭行为下能够保持模型准确性并实现全局模型一致性，且共识开销适中。与最先进的DFL方案BALANCE相比，DFL-C在面对无目标模型投毒攻击时具有更好的模型准确性，在后门攻击下具有相当的韧性，且在非IID场景下优势更加明显。

Conclusion: DFL-C成功解决了去中心化联邦学习中的全局模型一致性问题，同时提供了有效的拜占庭容错能力，为关键任务场景下的协作训练提供了安全可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Decentralized federated learning (DFL) is a promising paradigm for autonomous nodes to collaboratively train AI models without relying on a central server. However, existing DFL solutions do not guarantee global model consistency, a critical requirement for collaborative mission-critical scenarios where model divergence undermines decision uniformity and safety. This lack of consistency also amplifies vulnerability to Byzantine adversaries, who exploit the decentralized network topology and weak synchrony to perform equivocation and model poisoning attacks against individual victims.
  This paper introduces DFL-C, a novel Byzantine-resilient DFL architecture that enables decentralized nodes to perform collaborative training with global model consistency. At its core, DFL-C integrates an asynchronous common subset (ACS) consensus protocol into the DFL workflow to ensure all nodes aggregate a uniform set of model updates to establish global model consistency, despite individual Byzantine equivocation. DFL-C further implements a dual-domain trust scoring mechanism to provide resilience against data-domain Byzantine manipulations including model poisoning attacks. This mechanism complements the consensus protocol, significantly reducing the latter's runtime. Our experimental results demonstrate that DFL-C maintains model accuracy while achieving global model consistency under Byzantine behaviors with moderate consensus overhead. Notably, when compared with the state-of-the-art DFL solution BALANCE (Fang et al.) that does not provide model consistency, DFL-C achieves better model accuracy against untargeted model poisoning attacks and comparable resilience against backdoor attacks, with the advantage widened under non-IID scenarios.

</details>


### [28] [NeuroPrefetcher: Storage-Aware Sparse LLM Inference via Delta Prefetching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22643)
*Nobel Dhar,Md Romyull Islam,Xuechen Zhang,Gongjin Sun,Sahidul Islam,Bobin Deng,Kun Suo*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对模型超过内存容量的边缘设备LLM推理场景，提出NeuroPrefetcher系统，利用MLP层的时序局部性（82-85%神经元连续token间保持活跃）实现预测式增量预取，在受限内存下相比llama.cpp实现7.9-12.0倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM边缘部署方法（量化、小模型、卸载）仍假设模型可压缩或分区以适应内存预算。本文针对更困难的"模型超内存"场景，即模型在执行过程中始终大于可用内存，存储成为关键路径上的权重来源。

Method: 利用自回归解码中MLP活动的强时序局部性（约82-85%活跃神经元在连续token间保持活跃），设计预测式增量预取机制。使用占用基础模型2.86%参数的GPU常驻预测器，在第0层后一次性预测所有下游MLP层的稀疏活动，对比GPU缓存后仅对新增行发起NVMe读取，将被动OS缺页调度转变为主动、模型感知的权重移动。

Result: 在真实统一内存边缘硬件上，NeuroPrefetcher在受限内存预算下相比llama.cpp实现7.9-12.0倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过显式的模型感知权重预取，可有效解决边缘设备上模型超内存的LLM推理问题，显著优于传统的反应式操作系统分页机制。

Abstract: Deploying large language models on edge devices is increasingly limited by a widening gap between model size and available memory. Existing approaches such as quantization, smaller models, and offloading can raise the effective memory limit, but they still assume that the model can be compressed or partitioned to fit within some budget. We target the harder model-exceeds-memory setting, in which the model remains larger than resident memory throughout execution and storage becomes an active source of weights on the critical path. We observe that MLP activity during autoregressive decoding has strong temporal locality: approximately 82-85% of active neurons persist from one token to the next. This means that most sparse weights needed for the current token are already resident, and only the newly needed rows must be fetched from storage. We present NeuroPrefetcher, a storage-backed LLM inference system that exploits this property through predictive delta prefetching. After layer 0, a single GPU-resident predictor, occupying 2.86% of base model parameters, predicts sparse activity for all downstream MLP layers in one forward pass. The runtime compares these predictions against resident GPU buffers and issues application-scheduled NVMe reads only for incoming delta rows, replacing reactive operating-system demand paging with explicit, model-aware weight movement. On real unified-memory edge hardware, NeuroPrefetcher achieves 7.9-12.0x speedup over llama.cpp across constrained memory budgets.

</details>


### [29] [Exploring spectral element methods on the Tenstorrent RISC-V accelerator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22964)
*Daniyal Arshad,Nick Brown*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本研究将HPC mini应用Nekbone的AX内核移植到Tenstorrent Wormhole RISC-V加速器上，通过优化数据转置瓶颈，在128个Tensix核心上实现242.97 GFLOPS性能，超越24核Xeon Platinum CPU且功耗降低约7倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着商用RISC-V硬件的普及，其在高性能计算（HPC）领域的应用受到关注，PCIe加速卡提供了实用的近期采用路径。本研究旨在探索将广泛应用于HPC的Nekbone mini应用（源自获得Gordon Bell奖的Nek5000谱元求解器）移植到广泛可用的Tenstorrent Wormhole RISC-V加速器上。

Method: 将Nekbone的AX内核（用于评估泊松算子）移植到Wormhole加速器，描述算法到Tensix核心的映射过程。针对初始性能分析中发现的主机端数据转置瓶颈（用于z方向梯度计算），研究了两种优化策略以提升性能。

Result: 在128个Tensix核心上处理100,000个元素时达到242.97 GFLOPS性能，超越了24核Xeon Platinum CPU的性能表现，同时功耗降低约7倍。优化策略显著改善了由数据转置引起的性能瓶颈。

Conclusion: RISC-V加速器在高性能计算领域具有显著潜力，通过针对性优化可以克服架构特定的性能瓶颈，实现优于传统CPU的性能功耗比。这为RISC-V硬件在HPC领域的采用提供了有力证据。

Abstract: The growing availability of commodity RISC-V hardware has sparked interest in its use for High Performance Computing (HPC), with PCIe accelerator cards offering a practical near-term pathway to adoption. The Tenstorrent Wormhole is one example, with dedicated vector and matrix units across 128 Tensix cores, and is widely available. In this paper, we explore porting the AX kernel of Nekbone, a widely used HPC mini-application derived from the Gordon Bell Prize-winning Nek5000 spectral element solver, onto the Wormhole accelerator. This kernel evaluates the Poisson operator, and we describe the mapping of the algorithm onto the Tensix. The initial performance results reveal that the host-side data transposition, required for the z-direction gradient computation, is a severe bottleneck. Consequently, we investigated two optimisation strategies that yield dramatic improvements, achieving 242.97 GFLOPS for 100000 elements across 128 Tensix cores, outperforming a 24-core Xeon Platinum CPU and drawing approximately 7 times less power.

</details>


### [30] [Introducing the Self-Stabilizing SLEEPING Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23044)
*Tistou Fages,Colette Johnen,Mikaël Rabie*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文引入了自稳定睡眠模型，扩展了睡眠局部模型到自稳定场景，提出了能量高效的算法和转换器，在收敛和稳定阶段显著降低唤醒复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统自稳定算法要求节点持续保持活跃状态以检测系统问题，这导致了不必要的能量消耗。为了在保证自稳定性的同时实现能量效率，需要扩展睡眠局部模型到自稳定场景，使节点能够在不需要时进入睡眠状态以节省能量。

Method: 提出了自稳定睡眠模型，定义了三个复杂度指标：达到合法配置所需的同步轮数、达到该配置所需节点唤醒次数、以及达到稳定后节点需要唤醒的频率。开发了针对$(Δ+1)$-着色、最大独立集和最大匹配问题的能量高效算法，采用新颖的临时睡眠技术在收敛阶段降低唤醒复杂度。同时设计了两个转换器，将静默自稳定算法适配到睡眠设置中。

Result: 成功开发了针对$(Δ+1)$-着色、最大独立集和最大匹配问题的能量高效自稳定算法，在收敛阶段显著降低了唤醒复杂度。提出了两个有效的转换器：第一个简单且适用于低复杂度算法，第二个更复杂但能更高效地转换慢速自稳定算法。

Conclusion: 通过引入自稳定睡眠模型和相应的算法及转换器，成功实现了自稳定分布式系统的能量效率优化，为在保证系统稳定性的同时降低能量消耗提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: The SLEEPING LOCAL model introduces a new complexity parameter, the awake complexity, to make distributed algorithms energy-efficient. In the synchronous LOCAL model, nodes can now decide to be awake or asleep in each round. In a round, only awake nodes can communicate to share information, which consumes energy. The awake complexity is the maximum number of times a node is activated to produce an output. In particular, it often comes at the cost of the total number of rounds required to solve a problem, compared with algorithms in which every node is awake in every round.
  In this article, we adapt the notion of awaken rounds to the context of self-stabilization, introducing the Self-Stabilizing SLEEPING model. Nodes are no longer required to remain awake at all times. However, in self-stabilization, nodes must be activated infinitely often to detect any issue in the system's current state. In this model, the complexities are:
  * How many synchronous rounds are needed to reach a legitimate configuration?
  * How many times does a node need to be awake to reach this configuration?
  * How often does a node need to be awake once this configuration is reached?
  The goal is to minimize those three metrics, and we can expect different trade-offs.
  We present energy-efficient algorithms to solve the problems of finding a $(Δ+1)$-coloring, a Maximal Independent Set, and a Maximal Matching, thanks to new ad hoc sleeping techniques that reduce the awake complexity (i.e., energy consumption) during the convergence phase.
  We also propose two transformers that adapt silent self-stabilizing algorithms to the SLEEPING setup. The first transformer is pretty simple and deals with low-complexity algorithms. The second is more elaborate and is more energy-efficient when it transforms slow self-stabilizing algorithms.

</details>


### [31] [Effects of Hybrid CPU and Cache Architectures on Parallel HPC and Cloud Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23165)
*Nanda Velugoti,Joseph Manzano,Nathan Tallent,Kyle Hale*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了混合CPU架构（如Intel Alderlake）对高性能并行计算工作负载的影响，发现负载不均衡的应用在禁用线程亲和性时扩展性更好，且混合缓存架构对共享数据应用影响有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着Intel Alderlake架构的发布，混合CPU架构进入主流桌面计算领域。这种向异构CPU架构的转变对现有并行工作负载的性能和功耗具有多方面影响，需要深入研究混合核心和缓存架构对高性能并行工作负载的具体影响机制。

Method: 系统分析混合核心和缓存架构对高度并行HPC工作负载性能的影响；定性和定量描述并行工作负载的线程扩展行为；探索混合缓存架构对并行共享数据HPC应用的影响；通过实验验证线程亲和性和负载均衡对性能的影响。

Result: 发现两个关键结果：1）对于存在工作负载不均衡（即应用中线程执行不同工作量）的并行应用，当禁用线程亲和性时，在混合核心上具有更好的扩展性；2）混合缓存架构对并行共享数据应用影响很小，仅对某些包含锁的工作负载存在影响。

Conclusion: 本研究为未来工作奠定基础，未来研究将扩展此工作以建模并行工作负载和混合CPU架构，从而在执行时间、内存使用和功耗消耗方面提升性能。研究结果为混合架构下的并行应用优化提供了重要指导。

Abstract: Hybrid CPU architectures have entered the mainstream desktop computing with the announcement of Intel's Alderlake architecture. Such a transition to heterogeneous CPU architecture has various performance and power implications on existing parallel workloads. In this paper we study the effects and impact of hybrid core and cache architecture on the performance of highly parallel HPC workloads. We also illustrate interesting thread scaling behavior for parallel workloads and describes the reason for such behavior both qualitatively and quantitatively. We also explore the impact of hybrid cache architecture on parallel shared data HPC applications. Finally, we illustrate that 1) parallel applications with work imbalance (i.e., threads in application perform different amount of work) scale better across hybrid cores when thread affinity is disabled and 2) hybrid cache architecture has very little impact on parallel shared data applications except for some workloads with locks. This work lays the foundation for our future work which focuses on extending this work to model parallel workloads and hybrid CPU architectures to improve their performance in terms of execution time, memory usage and power consumption.

</details>

### [32] [KVBoost: Chunk-Level Key-Value Cache Reuse with Deviation-Guided Recomputation for Efficient Large Language Model Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21362)
*Srihari Unnikrishnan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: KVBoost通过分块级KV缓存复用和双哈希机制，实现了任意位置内容的缓存复用，在保持准确率的同时将首个token生成时间减少4.49倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前缀缓存系统要求提示词共享连续前缀，限制了共享内容出现在任意位置时的有效性；Transformer类大语言模型在预填充阶段需要为每个请求重新计算KV张量，导致高延迟。

Method: 提出KVBoost系统：采用双哈希键方案分离位置标识（前缀哈希）和内容标识（内容哈希）；引入选择性重编码和CacheBlend重编码两种修复策略解决注意力边界误差；集成非对称KV量化、自适应块边界分割和重要性加权驱逐机制。

Result: 在Qwen2.5-3B模型和1000个bug定位样本上测试，首个token生成时间从639.1ms降至142.4ms（减少4.49倍）；比前缀缓存方法性能提升16%；准确率保持99.2%（对比基线99.1%）。

Conclusion: KVBoost提供了一个实用的、内存受限的推理加速层，兼容基于RoPE的模型且无需架构修改，实现了任意位置内容的有效KV缓存复用。

Abstract: Transformer-based large language models (LLMs) incur high prefill latency because key-value (KV) tensors must be recomputed for each request. Existing prefix-caching systems reduce this cost but require prompts to share a leading contiguous prefix, limiting effectiveness when shared content appears at arbitrary positions. We present KVBoost, a chunk-level KV cache reuse system for HuggingFace-compatible decoder models that enables reuse regardless of content position. KVBoost introduces a dual-hash keying scheme that separates positional identity (prefix hash) from content identity (content hash), supporting both exact and approximate cache matches. To address attention boundary errors from independently cached chunks, KVBoost employs two repair strategies: SelectiveRecompute, which re-encodes boundary regions, and CacheBlendRecompute, which identifies and recomputes high-deviation tokens after a probe pass. The system further incorporates asymmetric KV quantization (int8/int4), adaptive chunk boundary splitting, and importance-weighted eviction under a fixed memory budget. Evaluated on Qwen/Qwen2.5-3B over 1,000 bug-localization samples, KVBoost achieves a 4.49x reduction in time-to-first-token (142.4 ms vs.\ 639.1 ms) and outperforms prefix caching by 16%, with no loss in accuracy (99.2% vs.\ 99.1%). KVBoost provides a practical, memory-bounded inference acceleration layer compatible with RoPE-based models without architectural modification.

</details>


### [33] [AIREP: A Protocol for Per-Decision Evidence in AI Runtime Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21363)
*Ali Toygar Abak*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AIREP协议，用于记录自动化AI运行时的治理决策。该协议将每次治理决策（释放、阻止、推迟、修订或升级）记录为可离线验证的签名对象，通过SHA-256哈希链确保记录不可篡改，并采用机制中立性测试保持格式通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI系统在关键决策中的作用日益增强，需要一种可信、可验证的机制来记录AI运行时的治理决策，以便在不依赖运行时本身的情况下进行事后审计和验证，从而增强AI系统的透明度和问责性。

Method: AIREP将治理决策编码为封闭动词集合中的一个，通过哈希引用输入、输出和证据，声明证据覆盖范围及缺失部分。记录形成SHA-256哈希链，绑定位置信息，使篡改和缺口可通过重计算检测。供应商、模型和域特定内容被限制在可选命名空间，通过机械中立性测试保持共享格式的通用性。提供了参考实现和双语言一致性套件。

Result: 成功设计并实现了AIREP协议及参考实现，包括双语言一致性验证工具。该格式能够有效记录AI运行时的治理决策，支持离线验证，具备防篡改能力，同时保持了跨实现的通用性和中立性。

Conclusion: AIREP格式可供任何记录治理决策的AI运行时采用。研究还揭示了若干实现问题，如跨实现的规范形式对齐、新鲜度证明和多运行时链等，为后续研究和改进指明了方向。

Abstract: A protocol is presented for recording the governance decisions of automated AI runtimes. When a runtime releases, blocks, defers, redacts, or escalates an individual output, AIREP records that decision as a single signed object that any party can check offline, independent of the runtime that produced it. A record carries the decision as one of a closed set of verbs under a stated policy basis, references its input, output, and evidence by hash rather than by value, and declares both what its evidence covers and what it does not. Records form a SHA-256 hash chain that binds each record to its position, so that tampering and gaps are detectable by recomputation. Vendor-, model-, and domain-specific content is confined to a single optional namespace, and a mechanical neutrality test keeps the shared format free of it. A reference implementation and a two-language conformance kit are described. Some implementation issues are considered, and problems such as alignment of the canonical form across implementations, freshness witnesses, and multi-runtime chains are exposed. The format is offered for adoption by any AI runtime that records governance decisions.

</details>

*Xihao Xie,Beichen Hu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文综述了生成式AI中因使用AI合成数据训练而导致的模型崩溃现象，填补了该领域系统回顾的空白，总结了不同应用场景下的崩溃现象及缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI的快速发展促使从业者使用AI合成数据训练下一代模型，缓解了数据供给压力，但引入了新问题：在模型与数据的自消耗循环中，模型最终崩溃，引发了生成式AI的可信度担忧。近年来已有研究探索此现象，但尚缺乏系统性回顾。

Method: 本文通过全面梳理和综述近年来的相关研究，对模型崩溃（Model Collapse, MC）现象进行系统性总结，涵盖不同应用场景中的表现以及缓解MC的对策。

Result: 提供了模型崩溃现象的最新概览，归纳了MC在不同应用场景下的表现形式，总结了缓解MC的潜在方案与措施，并梳理了该领域的研究进展。

Conclusion: 本文填补了模型崩溃现象系统性回顾的空白，强调了当前面临的挑战与未来的研究机遇，为后续研究提供了参考基础。

Abstract: Driven by massive amounts of web-scale data, generative AI (GenAI) has achieved remarkable progress, enabling various applications in diverse sectors. The advances of GenAI have actuated practitioners to use AI-synthesized data for training next-generation AI models. Undeniably, using synthetic data has alleviated the increasing stringent demand for data supply. Unfortunately, it also introduces a new critical issue: in a self-consuming cycle between model and data, the model ultimately collapse, raising more trustworthiness concerns to GenAI. In recent years, increasingly more studies have investigated the phenomenon of model collapse (MC) and explored potential solutions to mitigate it. However, the review of the phenomenon of MC still remains blank. To fill this gap, this paper provides an up-to-date overview of these studies for consolidating and reviewing the progress of MC in different application scenarios and countermeasures for mitigating MC. We also highlight challenges and future research opportunities.

</details>

Abstract: This report summarizes the work conducted on the Game of Hidden Rules (GOHR), focusing on reinforcement learning agents trained to infer hidden rules from trial-and-error feedback, representation design, rule difficulty analysis, transfer learning, generalization, and pseudo-bot-assisted human learning analysis. The report focuses on the Transformer-based A2C framework, Feature-Centric and Object-Centric representations, experimental findings, and classification of human learning data.

</details>

*Ruotong Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Xurui Liu,Haidong Xue,Dong Liang,Jigao Fu,Wu YanBiao,Yuanyi Zhen,Fengli Xu,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了LitReview Arena，一个面向文献综述质量的专家对抗式评估平台，发现当前最强系统在整体效用上仅23.0%胜率击败人类撰写，同时提出了专家校准的评估器LitJudge，将LLM评估与人类专家的对齐度从Spearman's rho=0.467提升至0.78。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献综述对科学进步至关重要，但自动生成的综述质量评估存在困难，因为研究效用的许多方面依赖于专家判断而非简单的引用重叠等指标，现有评估方法无法准确衡量文献综述的实际价值。

Method: 1. 提出LitReview Arena平台，采用对抗式评估协议；
2. 匹配具有AI论文写作经验的领域专家到其专长主题；
3. 专家对匿名化草稿进行五维度文献综述特有标准的维度级评判；
4. 收集约3k专家判断，每个包含五个维度结果；
5. 基于收集的偏好数据训练专家校准评估器LitJudge。

Result: 1. 收集约3k专家判断数据集；
2. 最强系统在整体效用上仅23.0%的决定性比赛胜率击败人类草稿；
3. 代理式LLM（如Sonar Deep Research）相比基础语言模型性能提升超过60%；
4. 现有LLM-as-a-judge方法与人类专家存在显著错位（Spearman's rho=0.467），尤其在综合性强标准如论文结构和研究建议方面；
5. LitJudge将评估对齐度提升至Spearman's rho=0.78，达到专家间一致性水平。

Conclusion: 当前自动文献综述系统在整体效用上仍远落后于人类专家水平，现有LLM评估方法与人类判断存在显著差距，通过专家校准的评估器可以有效提升评估准确性，公开了代码和数据以促进该领域研究。

Abstract: Literature reviews are essential to scientific progress, but rigorously evaluating automatically generated reviews remains difficult because many aspects of research utility depend on expert judgment rather than reference-overlap metrics. We introduce LitReview Arena, a battle-style evaluation platform with a structured protocol tailored to literature review quality: domain experts with AI paper-writing experience compare anonymized drafts, are matched to topics within their expertise, and provide dimension-wise outcomes over five literature-review-specific criteria. From this protocol, we collect approximately 3k expert judgments, each containing five dimension-wise outcomes, and show that even the strongest current systems win only 23.0% of decisive matches against human drafts on overall utility, while agentic LLMs such as Sonar Deep Research substantially outperform base language models by over 60%. We further find that existing LLM-as-a-judge methods are substantially misaligned with human experts (Spearman's rho=0.467), especially on synthesis-heavy criteria such as paper structure and research suggestions. Using the collected preference data, we provide an expert-calibrated evaluator, LitJudge, which improves alignment to Spearman's rho=0.78, comparable to inter-expert consistency; code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/VanellopeAsher/LitReview-Arena.

</details>


### [37] [SchemaRouter: Field-Aware Tool Routing for Efficient Heterogeneous Agentic RAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21375)
*Yong-eun Cho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SchemaRouter是一种轻量级路由层，通过模式图表示工具和字段，在材料科学基准测试中以减少89%的检索令牌实现了与全量检索相当的准确率，同时显著降低延迟并提供可验证的数据溯源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异构智能体RAG系统面临两个昂贵的失败模式：过度获取（增加负载大小、令牌使用和延迟）和获取不足（遗漏回答查询所需的字段）。将所有工具描述暴露给LLM智能体或仅通过向量相似性选择工具都会导致这些问题。

Method: SchemaRouter将工具、端点、参数、响应字段、领域概念、单位、溯源和许可证策略表示为模式图。小型LLM提取意图、概念和源约束，而字段选择通过对图的意图组投影和带有别名的概念-字段匹配确定性地完成，生成可执行的工具计划。

Result: 在110个材料科学查询的基准测试中，SchemaRouter达到0.71的答案准确率（与fetch-everything相当），检索上下文令牌仅227个（对比fetch-everything的2,066个），端到端延迟比prompt-all降低2.7倍。工具精确率达0.93，参数有效性1.0，在62%的答案中提供了溯源和许可证信息（基线方法接近0%）。

Conclusion: SchemaRouter在保持竞争性准确率的同时，提高了效率、实现了与模式大小无关的扩展，并提供了可验证的溯源/许可证支撑的答案。最小化选择字段数量反而会降低准确率，而保留召回率的投影策略能够恢复最佳准确率。

Abstract: Heterogeneous agentic retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) systems increasingly orchestrate external APIs, internal databases, vector stores, and graph stores. Exposing all tool descriptions to an LLM agent, or selecting tools only by vector similarity, causes two costly failures: over-fetching, which increases payload size, token use, and latency, and under-fetching, which omits fields needed to answer the query.
  We present SchemaRouter, a lightweight routing layer that represents tools, endpoints, parameters, response fields, domain concepts, units, provenance, and license policies as a schema graph. Given a query, SchemaRouter emits an executable tool plan specifying which tools to call and which fields to retrieve. A small LLM extracts intent, concepts, and source constraints, while field selection is deterministic over the graph through intent-group projection and concept-field matching with an alias layer.
  On a materials-science benchmark of 110 queries, SchemaRouter achieves answer accuracy of 0.71, matching fetch-everything within overlapping confidence intervals and exceeding prompt-all's 0.66, though their intervals overlap. It uses 227 retrieved-context tokens versus 2,066 for fetch-everything and achieves 2.7x lower end-to-end latency than prompt-all. It also obtains the best tool-exact rate of 0.93 and parameter validity of 1.0. SchemaRouter grounds provenance and license information in 62 percent of answers, compared with approximately 0 percent for all baselines.
  We also find that minimizing selected-field count is counterproductive: it reduces answer accuracy to 0.56 with negligible token savings, while recall-preserving projection restores top accuracy. SchemaRouter improves efficiency, schema-size-independent scaling, and verifiable provenance/license-grounded answering at competitive accuracy.

</details>


### [38] [RIACT: A Responsible AI System for Personalized Study Habit Tracking and Early Burnout Signal Detection in University Students](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21379)
*Ria Sidhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了RIACT系统，这是一个结合结构化学习记录和混合AI架构的Web应用，旨在通过可解释的规则和个性化推荐实现学生职业倦怠的早期预警。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高等教育中学生职业倦怠率高达12%-70%且显著高于工作人群，但目前只能在学业下滑后回顾性识别。主要原因是学生缺乏对自己学习行为的结构化可见性，现有生产力工具仅记录活动而缺乏解读能力。

Method: 开发了RIACT（记录、洞察、分析、指导、追踪）Web应用系统。学生记录学习时段的位置和时间；系统通过计入休息时间计算净专注时长，基于周际行为比较的透明确定性规则检测倦怠信号，并使用受限于固定输出模式的大语言模型来语境化模式并生成个性化建议。系统嵌入负责任AI原则：警告由可审计规则而非模型判断控制，输出框架为观察而非诊断，数据收集仅限于自主记录的行为字段。

Result: 构建了一个具有早期倦怠信号检测功能的Web应用系统，该系统通过可解释、可审计的规则机制实现了对学生学习行为的结构化记录和智能化分析，同时遵循负责任AI设计原则。

Conclusion: 本文描述了系统设计原理，将其置于学生职业倦怠和教育可解释AI文献背景中，并提出了一个验证框架，用于验证系统行为信号与既定倦怠测量工具的一致性。

Abstract: Student burnout is highly prevalent in higher education, with reported rates ranging from 12% to over 70% and consistently exceeding those of the working population - yet it is typically identified only retrospectively, after academic decline has already occurred. A contributing factor is that students have little structured visibility into their own study behaviour, and existing productivity tools record activity without interpreting it. This paper presents RIACT (Record, Insight, Analyze, Coach, Track), a web-based application that combines structured study session logging with a hybrid AI architecture to surface personalized insights and early burnout signals. Students log sessions by location and time; the system computes net focus time by accounting for breaks, detects burnout signals through transparent, deterministic rules operating on week-over-week behavioural comparisons, and uses a large language model - constrained to a fixed output schema - to contextualize patterns and generate personalized recommendations. The design embeds responsible AI principles throughout: warnings are governed by auditable rules rather than model judgement, all output is framed as an observation rather than a diagnosis and data collection is limited to self-logged behavioural fields. We describe the system's design rationale, situate it within the literature on student burnout and explainable AI in education and propose an evaluation framework for validating its behavioural signals against established burnout instruments.

</details>
