Abstract: Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) have revolutionized reasoning in LLMs, and the increasing public availability of reasoning traces creates valuable opportunities to study model behavior not only at the surface level but also at the granularity of individual reasoning steps. However, understanding the types of thinking employed during reasoning - which offers critical insights into models' reasoning patterns and enables actionable applications - remains underexplored. To address this gap, we introduce a framework for automatic annotation of reasoning steps through the lens of Bloom's Taxonomy, which classifies thinking into six cognitive levels, such as Remembering, Applying and Evaluating. Using this framework, we perform a large-scale analysis across models and datasets, revealing both similarities and differences in thinking patterns across models and tasks. Moreover, we demonstrate that thinking-type information derived from reasoning traces correlates with correctness, paving the way for improved reasoning. Our findings establish a fine-grained framework for analyzing thinking patterns in LRMs and provide actionable insights for enhancing reasoning quality.

</details>


### [176] [What is mathematics now, and what should it be?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23218)
*Jeremy Avigad*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文倡导对AI与数学的关系持更广阔、乐观的视角，超越现有神经定理证明器的成功。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经定理证明器的进展虽显著，但掩盖了AI在数学领域更广阔的愿景以及数学家与AI互动的深层可能性。

Method: 通过论述性分析，重新审视AI在数学中的应用现状与发展方向。

Result: 提出了一个更具包容性和前瞻性的观点，强调AI在数学领域的潜力远超当前的定理证明范畴。

Conclusion: 呼吁学术界拓展对AI辅助数学研究的想象，重视数学家与AI系统的深度协作，推动数学与AI的共同发展。

Abstract: Advances in neural theorem provers have been impressive, but the successes obscure a broader vision of what AI can do for mathematics and how mathematicians can engage with AI. This essay advances a more expansive and optimistic point of view.

</details>


### [177] [Is Next-Chunk Reasoning RL Really Better than SFT? Revisiting Training Strategies under no-CoT Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23256)
*Yinhao Tang,Youqing Fang,Yanan Sun,Jiangning Liu,Ziyi Wang,Xun Zhao,Weiming Zhang,Bin Liu,Kuikun Liu,Wenwei Zhang,Kai Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现简单的混合监督微调(Mixed SFT)在利用无CoT数据时，性能优于下一块推理强化学习，且计算成本降低60倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究提出使用下一块推理强化学习来利用无CoT数据（如解题步骤和教科书推导），但现有评估主要与传统SFT基线比较，尚不清楚性能提升是来自RL本身还是更有效地暴露模型于无CoT数据。需要对照研究来验证RL方法的实际效果。

Method: 进行对照研究，比较下一块推理强化学习与一个简单但被忽视的替代方案：混合SFT。混合SFT是单阶段监督微调，联合训练无CoT数据和长CoT数据。在领域内数学推理和领域外推理任务上进行评估。

Result: 混合SFT在RLVR后性能上限明显高于下一块推理强化学习，同时训练计算量减少60倍以上。该优势在领域内数学推理和领域外推理任务中保持一致。此外发现更高的预RLVR准确率不一定转化为更高的后RLVR准确率。

Conclusion: 简单的混合SFT方法在利用无CoT数据方面优于复杂的RL方法。需要在完整的后训练管道背景下评估无CoT训练策略，而非仅关注预训练阶段的性能指标。

Abstract: Recent work proposes next-chunk reasoning RL for leveraging no-CoT data---corpora such as worked solutions and textbook derivations that contain reasoning-rich content but lack explicit chain-of-thought annotations. The method trains a model to generate implicit reasoning traces and rewards them by their ability to predict the next chunk of text. While promising, existing evaluations primarily compare against conventional SFT baselines, leaving open whether the gains come from the RL formulation itself or from more effectively exposing the model to no-CoT data. We address this question with a controlled study of next-chunk reasoning RL and a simple but previously overlooked alternative: Mixed SFT, a single supervised fine-tuning stage that jointly trains on no-CoT and long-CoT data. Despite its simplicity, Mixed SFT achieves a clearly higher post-RLVR performance ceiling than next-chunk reasoning RL while requiring over 60 times less training compute. The advantage is consistent across in-domain mathematical reasoning and out-of-domain reasoning tasks. Moreover, we show that higher pre-RLVR accuracy does not necessarily translate into higher post-RLVR accuracy, highlighting the need to evaluate no-CoT training strategies in the context of the full post-training pipeline.

</details>


### [178] [Automated Construction of FAIR Digital Object Knowledge Graphs from Flat Cultural Heritage Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23263)
*Zeyd Boukhers,Lingxiao Kong,Xenophon Zabulis,Georgios Toubekis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个利用大语言模型将欧洲数字图书馆（Europeana）的扁平化记录转换为符合FAIR数字对象（FDO）规范的知识图谱的管道，通过自动分类元数据值并链接至受控词表，实现了86%的元数据槽位链接和58.5%的新增值解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FAIR数字对象框架要求元数据属性值尽可能表示为持久标识符（PID），以构建完全机器可操作且每个引用均可解析的图谱。然而，欧洲数字图书馆数据模型设计于FDO规范之前，其大部分元数据值为纯文本，虽满足人工浏览需求，但无法支持自动化代理跨记录或集合的追踪与操作。

Method: 提出一个将扁平化Europeana记录转换为符合FDO规范、以CIDOC-CRM结构化的知识图谱的管道。该管道将每个遗产实体建模为独立的FDO，配备PID、类型、配置文件和元数据层。核心技术挑战在于自动化区分必须转换为PID引用的值（可解析实体）与可保留为字面量的值（终端叶节点如注释、测量值和日期）。研究采用大语言模型对每个元数据值进行分类，将其路由到受控词表（Getty AAT、Wikidata、VIAF、PeriodO），并链接至共享实体FDO。

Result: 在来自五个Europeana提供商的637条考古记录上评估，管道链接了86%的元数据槽位，解析了58.5%的Europeana此前未富化的值。该管道成功合并了字节完全匹配方法无法识别的跨语言表面形式，其中33个此类合并中有17个经人工审查为正确。图谱连通性方面与字符串匹配方法无显著差异，但FDO图谱的显著特征是每个节点均有类型且可解析。

Conclusion: 该管道成功实现了从扁平化Europeana记录到FDO合规知识图谱的转换，通过LLM驱动的元数据分类与实体链接，显著提升了元数据的机器可操作性和跨记录可追踪性，验证了LLM在文化遗产数据FDO化改造中的有效性。

Abstract: The FAIR Digital Object (FDO) framework mandates that metadata attribute values be expressed as persistent identifiers (PIDs) wherever possible, to produce a fully machine-actionable graph in which every reference is resolvable. The Europeana Data Model was designed long before the FDO specification, and it stores most metadata values as plain text. This serves human browsing well enough, but gives an automated agent nothing to follow across records or collections. We present a pipeline that transforms flat Europeana records into an FDO-compliant knowledge graph structured with CIDOC-CRM. Following the FDO specification, we model every heritage entity as a discrete FDO with its own PID, type, profile, and metadata layer. The core technical challenge is automating the FDO-prescribed distinction between values that must become PID references (resolvable entities) and those that may remain literals (terminal leaves such as notes, measurements, and dates). We address this with a large language model that classifies each metadata value, routes it to a controlled vocabulary (Getty AAT, Wikidata, VIAF, PeriodO), and links it to a shared entity FDO. We evaluate using 637 archaeological records from five Europeana providers, processing each with the LLM. The pipeline links 86% of metadata slots, resolving 58.5% of values Europeana had not already enriched. It also merges cross-lingual surface forms that byte-identical matching keeps apart, where 17 of 33 such merges are correct on manual review. Graph connectivity does not separate this from string matching; what distinguishes the FDO graph is that every node is typed and resolvable.

</details>


### [179] [Hidden in the Request: Explaining Unethical LLM Compliance through Token Relevance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23264)
*Or Biton,Tomer Krichli,Itai Allouche,Joseph Keshet*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现LLM在直接请求协助的场景下更容易出现伦理对齐失败，原因是模型存在归因偏差——过度关注良性任务框架词而忽视道德风险信号词。通过LRP引导的解码方法可有效改善这一问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM的对齐目标（有用性和无害性）存在内在冲突，导致模型在某些情况下无法表现出预期的伦理行为。为了理解这些对齐失败的内在机制，研究需要系统性地调查模型在何种场景下会违反伦理准则，以及背后的原因。

Method: 1. 提出探测方法论，通过三种结构模态呈现不道德场景：客观分类任务、主观第一人称陈述、直接请求协助
2. 使用逐层相关性传播(LRP)技术进行归因分析
3. 提出两种LRP引导的解码方法，使生成过程更关注道德风险信号词(cue-tokens)

Result: 1. 模型在请求协助形式下性能显著下降
2. 发现归因偏差：模型更关注良性任务框架词(如"Can you help me...")，而非道德风险信号词(如"without getting caught")
3. LRP引导的解码干预方法能有效促进更安全的响应，验证了cue-token归因在合规失败中的作用

Conclusion: LLM的伦理对齐失败可归因于对道德风险信号词的归因不足。通过技术干预引导模型更关注这些关键token，可以有效提升模型的安全性，为解决LLM对齐问题提供了新的技术路径和理论见解。

Abstract: Although Large Language Models (LLMs) are aligned to optimize for both helpfulness and harmlessness, these dual objectives may conflict, inevitably leading to alignment failures. This work systematically investigates instances where LLMs fail to exhibit ethical behavior. To understand the underlying mechanics of these vulnerabilities, we introduce a probing methodology that presents unethical scenarios to LLMs in three distinct structural modalities: objective classification tasks, subjective first-person statements, and direct requests for assistance. We find that model performance degrades in the request-for-assistance-based form. Using Layer-wise Relevance Propagation (LRP), we trace this discrepancy to an attribution bias: the model places greater emphasis on benign task-framing tokens (e.g., "Can you help me...") than on tokens signaling the underlying unethical behavior (e.g., "without getting caught"), which we term cue-tokens. We hypothesize that this under-attribution contributes to harmful compliance. To test this, we introduce two LRP-guided decoding methods that steer generation toward trajectories more relevant to cue tokens. Empirical evaluations show that these interventions promote safer responses, supporting cue-token attribution's role in compliance failures.

</details>


### [180] [Apodex 1.1: Scaling Agentic Intelligence for Complex Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23283)
*Apodex Team,B. An,B. Li,B. Wang,B. Zhang,B. L. Wang,C. Feng,C. Wei,C. Xue,C. Zhang,D. Ng,D. Ye,E. Min,F. Chen,F. Liu,F. Yang,F. Ye,H. Xu,H. Yang,H. Ye,H. Zhang,H. Zhao,J. Li,J. Lin,J. Xia,K. Jin,K. Wang,K. Yang,L. Bing,L. Lei,L. Su,Le. Wang,Lu. Wang,N. Wang,Q. Ren,Q. Yang,R. Li,S. Bai,S. Du,S. Li,S. Lin,S. Nie,S. Wang,S. Zhang,S. Z. Wang,Ta. Q. Fang,Ti. Q. Fang,W. Fang,W. Li,W. Zhang,X. Chen,X. Li,X. Tang,X. Wang,X. Xu,X. Zhang,X. Q. Wang,X. Y. Wang,Y. Deng,Y. Gao,Y. Hu,Y. Li,Y. Sui,Y. Wang,Y. Xiao,Y. Zhang,Z. Chen,Z. Cheng,Z. Feng,Z. Liang,Z. Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Apodex 1.1通过环境扩展和智能体协调扩展两个维度，开发出具有持续、可验证进展能力的通用语言模型，在复杂专业工作中达到领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用语言模型虽然能够推理和综合知识，但复杂工作还需要与文件、信息源和可执行代码进行持续交互，同时需要状态维护、故障恢复和可验证交付。这种"工作能力"（sustained, verifiable progress toward a real-world objective）是现有系统所缺乏的。

Method: 1. 环境扩展(Environment Scaling)：扩展可执行文件、搜索和代码环境的多样性和可验证性
2. 智能体协调扩展(Agentic Coordination Scaling)：训练智能体分解长期任务、委派并行工作、整合异步结果和重规划
3. 共享执行框架和AgentOS：跨工具和智能体维护任务状态和来源
4. 将环境轨迹和协调轨迹转化为可靠行为的训练方法

Result: 在复杂专业工作、金融、科学研究、数学、编程和搜索等多个领域达到领先性能水平，尽管使用的模型参数量（35B）远小于许多前沿系统。Apodex 1.1 Mini在本地可部署形式下仍保持强大的工作能力。

Conclusion: 该研究将智能体智能建立在有用、可验证的长期工作基础上，推进了构建"Heavy-Duty Solver"的目标，能够处理雄心勃勃的长期任务。

Abstract: General-purpose language models can reason and synthesize knowledge, but complex work also requires sustained interaction with files, information sources, and executable code, together with state maintenance, failure recovery, and verifiable delivery. We call this \emph{working capability}: sustained, verifiable progress toward a real-world objective. Apodex 1.1 develops this capability along two complementary dimensions. \emph{Environment Scaling} expands the diversity and verifiability of executable file, search, and code environments, while \emph{Agentic Coordination Scaling} trains agents to decompose long-horizon tasks, delegate parallel work, integrate asynchronous results, and replan. A shared execution harness and AgentOS maintain task state and provenance across tools and agents, and training turns environment trajectories and coordination traces into reliable behavior. Across complex professional work, finance, scientific research, mathematics, coding, and search, Apodex 1.1 reaches the leading performance band despite using a substantially smaller model than many frontier systems. The 35B-parameter Apodex 1.1 Mini further retains strong working capability in a locally deployable form. These results ground agentic intelligence in useful, verifiable work completed over time and advance our goal of building a \emph{Heavy-Duty Solver} for ambitious, long-running tasks.

</details>


### [181] [EviSafe: Evidence-Grounded Safety Evaluation for Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23313)
*Xuetong Li,Gaofeng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了EviSafe框架和EviSafeBench基准，通过自然响应、证据报告和反事实响应三探针协议，评估视觉语言模型是否基于正确的多模态理由做出安全决策，发现现有模型的安全行为并不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的视觉语言模型安全基准仅评估最终响应结果（拒绝、警告或遵从），无法判断模型是否基于正确的多模态理由做出安全决策。表面安全的行为可能源于关键词触发的拒绝、遗漏视觉危险或对良性敏感输入的过度拒绝，这种结果层面的评估视角存在局限性。

Method: 提出EviSafe框架，包含三项核心评估：自然用户行为、文本和视觉证据的显式基础、安全关键证据的反事实变化的行为敏感性。构建EviSafeBench基准，包含1,181个金标准图像-文本场景和2,452个目标反事实变体，覆盖8个安全域和8个风险源类型。采用三探针协议：自然响应、证据报告和反事实响应提示，使用证据感知评判器进行评分。

Result: 在11个评估的VLM中，自然严重性准确率范围为27.6%至52.8%，宽松诊断一致性为6.1%至29.3%，不安全到安全的反事实转换成功率为30.4%至58.4%。这些结果差距表明评估的VLMs无法可靠地基于正确的多模态理由保持安全。

Conclusion: 当前视觉语言模型的安全行为并不可靠，缺乏基于正确多模态推理的安全决策能力，这促使需要超越简单拒绝计数的评估方法，采用更细粒度的证据基础评估框架。

Abstract: Vision-language model safety benchmarks typically evaluate only final responses: whether a model refuses, warns, or complies. This outcome-level view cannot tell whether a model is safe for the right multimodal reason. Safelooking behavior may reflect keyword-triggered refusal, missed visual hazards, or over-refusal of benign-sensitive inputs. We introduce EviSafe, an evidence-grounded framework for VLM safety that jointly evaluates natural user-facing behavior, explicit grounding in textual and visual evidence, and behavioral sensitivity to counterfactual changes in safety-critical evidence. EviSafeBench instantiates the framework as a controlled benchmark with 1,181 gold image-text scenarios and 2,452 targeted counterfactual variants across eight safety domains and eight risk-source types. Each scenario includes a gold safety decision, evidence annotations, a safe-response policy, and counterfactual interventions. The three-probe protocol queries models with natural-response, evidencereporting, and counterfactual-response prompts, then scores them using an evidence-aware judge. Across eleven evaluated VLMs, natural severity accuracy ranges from 27.6% to 52.8%, relaxed diagnostic consistency from 6.1% to 29.3%, and unsafe-to-safe counterfactual transition success from 30.4% to 58.4%. These gaps show that the evaluated VLMs are not reliably safe for the right multimodal reason and motivate evaluation beyond refusal counts.

</details>


### [182] [Agent-G$^2$: Gaussian Guidance for Agentic Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23318)
*Zixuan Wang,Yanrui Miao,Zhengxi Lu,Teng Pan,Yiwen Qiu,Hongxing Li,Peng Qiu,Ruiqing Zhang,Yongliang Shen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agent-G²框架，用高斯分布建模提示深度，无需额外探测回滚即可自适应估计最优指导深度，在ALFWorld上超越现有基线2.3-7.4分，回滚成本降低至原来的三分之一以下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于提示的强化学习方法将指导深度（expert trajectory保留长度）视为确定性标量：调度方法对所有样本使用同一值，忽略任务异质性；逐样本探测虽能估计个体深度但需额外回滚成本。作者发现有用指导深度分布呈高斯带状而非集中在单一点，激发了对深度进行概率建模的动机。

Method: Agent-G²框架将指导深度视为从高斯分布中采样的随机变量：中心由全局基线和按集群难度组合估计，散布追踪集群内方差。参数在线估计利用策略优化过程中已收集的回滚数据，无需额外探测回滚或训练深度预测器。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop上使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B-Instruct评估：Agent-G²在ALFWorld上分别超越最强提示基线、无提示基线和Aux-RL基线2.3/3.9/7.4分，同时回滚成本降至逐样本探测方法的三分之一以下。

Conclusion: 通过高斯分布建模指导深度并在线估计其参数，Agent-G²有效解决了现有方法的深度选择困境，在提升性能的同时显著降低计算成本，为长视界智能体任务的稀疏奖励问题提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Hint-based reinforcement learning addresses reward sparsity in long-horizon agentic tasks by retaining a prefix of an expert trajectory before each rollout, letting the policy explore from a state closer to success. Its effectiveness hinges on the guidance depth: how much of the trajectory to keep. Existing methods treat this depth as a deterministic scalar. Scheduled approaches share one value across samples and ignore per-task heterogeneity; per-sample probing estimates it separately at the cost of extra rollouts. We find that useful guidance occupies a band of depths whose informativeness profile is approximately Gaussian around the band center, rather than concentrating at a single optimal point. We propose Agent-G$^2$, a Gaussian guidance framework that draws the depth per task from a Gaussian whose center and spread are estimated online from rollouts already collected for policy optimization, requiring no probe rollouts or learned depth predictor. The center combines a global baseline with per-cluster difficulty, and the spread tracks within-cluster variance. We evaluate Agent-G$^2$ on ALFWorld and WebShop on Qwen2.5-1.5B / 7B-Instruct. Agent-G$^2$ outperforms the strongest hint-based, hint-free, and Aux-RL baselines on ALFWorld by 2.3 / 3.9 / 7.4 points at under one-third the rollout cost of per-sample probing.

</details>


### [183] [Walking on the DARKSIDE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23370)
*Aldo Gangemi,Emanuele Bottazzi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DARKSIDE方法，通过追踪话语排除路径和认证轴，增强LLM对伪造权威和误导机制的检测能力，有效识别看似合理的无意义输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLMs）只能识别模式但缺乏对排斥路径的追踪能力，容易被伪造权威、错误应用机制或隐蔽类比所欺骗。POLANYI++等现有方法生成扩展知识图谱(XKG)时，会将看似合理的错误假设同合法三元组一起具体化，难以被自动推理器检测。

Method: 提出DARKSIDE连贯性审计方法，作为POLANYI++的上层引导层。该方法将话语排除路径形式化为显式数据结构，引入认证轴（Warranted、Unattested、Misattributed、Fabricated四类）对命名指称进行分类，设置升级规则：当伪造率为正或未支持率超阈值时，将委托风险评估推至UNSAFE。

Result: 在BSBench基准测试上（包含软件工程、金融、医疗、物理和法律领域的100个对抗性语料），以Claude Sonnet 4.6为独立裁判，DARKSIDE作为Gemini 3的引导层进行评估。实验证明：当LLM前向传递被本体介导的负路径装置包裹时，结构化模式-路径鸿沟可被部分填补。

Conclusion: 扩展知识图谱(XKG)作为缺失的记忆模块，认证轴作为认识论防火墙，能够有效识别和阻止看似合理的无意义输入进入知识图谱。该架构验证了本体介导的负路径装置对于增强LLM连贯性判断的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) recognise patterns but do not natively track the path of exclusions that a coherent discourse demands. When an input rests on a fabricated authority, a misapplied mechanism, or a surreptitious analogy, an unsteered LLM tends to engage with it as if it were grounded, and to reify the misstep into any structured output it generates. Logic-Augmented Generation (LAG) with POLANYI++, an LLM-steering method that uses heuristics, ontologies and problem solving methods for tacit knowledge extraction, produces an Extended Knowledge Graph (XKG) in OWL2, but inherits the same vulnerability: a sophisticated nonsensical input is reified into the graph alongside the legitimate triples, and is hardly detectable by automated reasoners since the XKG is generated jointly with the wrong assumptions. We introduce DARKSIDE, a coherence auditing method on top of POLANYI++. It formalises the trail as an explicit data structure of accumulated exclusions over discourse time, complemented by a warrant axis that classifies each named referent as Warranted, Unattested, Misattributed or Fabricated, with an escalation rule that pushes the DelegationRiskAssessment to UNSAFE when the fabricated rate is positive or the unsupported rate exceeds a threshold. We evaluate DARKSIDE as a steering layer over a Gemini 3 on BSBench, a 100-item adversarial corpus of sophisticated-sounding nonsense across software engineering, finance, healthcare, physics and law, with Claude Sonnet 4.6 as an independent judge. The empirical evidence supports an architectural claim: when an LLM forward pass is wrapped in an ontology-mediated negative-trail apparatus, the structural pattern-vs-path gap can be partially scaffolded. The XKG functions as the missing memory, and the warrant axis as an epistemic firewall.

</details>


### [184] [Modalities Should Talk to Each Other: Dual-Stream Multimodal Learning for Long-Horizon Influenza Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23373)
*Seyed Mohammad Hossein Hashemi,Mohsen Hooshmand,Parvin Razzaghi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出双流注意力(DSA)框架，融合数值和文本数据预测12周后流感样病例(ILI)，相比最佳基线误差降低37-67%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共卫生监测数据集通常包含数值流行病学信号和文本信息（如新闻标题），但文本数据存在噪声大、结构松散、与近期趋势间接相关以及相对于数值信号滞后等问题。如何有效融合这两种模态进行长期ILI预测是一个关键挑战，需要精心设计的融合机制。

Method: 提出双流注意力(DSA)框架：1) 使用基于Transformer的数值编码器和领域自适应的标题编码器分别编码数值和文本模态；2) 通过双向跨模态注意力(CMA)机制实现两种模态的相互条件化（文本引导数值信号解读，反之亦然）；3) 将CMA输出传递给因果时序模型进行预测。模型基于36周多模态历史数据预测12周后的ILI活动。

Result: 在Time-MMD数据集上，DSA中位测试MSE为0.416，显著优于iTransformer(0.668)、TaTS(0.607)和GPT4MTS(0.851)，平均误差分别降低54.95%、37.29%和67.23%，配对Cohen's d分别为0.555、0.337和0.345。在100%的Bootstrap抽样中排名第一，且最坏窗口误差显著低于所有基线。在外部地理数据集上同样排名第一。消融实验表明双向注意力优于单向，且优势不依赖文本编码器选择或语言模型微调。

Conclusion: DSA通过双向跨模态注意力有效融合数值和文本模态，在长期ILI预测任务上取得显著优势。扰动保真度分析显示学习到的CMA在目标掩码下具有功能性信息，且文本到数值方向的效果更强，证明了文本信息对数值信号解读的重要引导作用。

Abstract: Forecasting long-range influenza-like illness (ILI) matters for public health readiness. Publicly available surveillance datasets typically pair numeric epidemiological signals with textual information that is noisy, loosely structured, only indirectly related to near-term trends, and often lagged relative to the numeric signal. Fusing the two therefore requires careful design. We propose Dual-Stream Attention (DSA), a multimodal deep learning framework that forecasts 12-week-ahead ILI activity from a 36-week multimodal history by letting the numerical and textual streams condition each other. Using the Time-MMD health-domain dataset, DSA separately encodes the two modalities with a Transformer-based numerical encoder and a domain-adapted headline encoder, then couples them through a bidirectional Cross-Modal Attention (CMA) mechanism: the text (news headlines) conditions the interpretation of the numeric signal and vice versa. The CMA output then passes to a causal temporal model for forecasting. Evaluated across ten random seeds, DSA achieves a median test MSE of 0.416, versus 0.668, 0.607, and 0.851 for iTransformer, TaTS, and GPT4MTS, corresponding to mean-error reductions of 54.95%, 37.29%, and 67.23%, with paired Cohen's d of 0.555, 0.337, and 0.345, respectively, and ranks first in 100% of bootstrap draws. It also has substantially lower worst-window error than all baselines. On an external-geography dataset, DSA again ranks first among nine evaluated baselines. Ablations show the advantage does not depend on text-encoder choice or language-model fine-tuning, and that bidirectional attention outperforms either direction alone. Finally, perturbation-based faithfulness analysis shows the learned CMA is functionally informative under targeted masking, with a stronger effect in the text-to-numerical direction.

</details>


### [185] [MediSkill-Evo: Process-Constrained Self-Evolution for Evidence-Grounded Clinical Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23397)
*Ruoyu Wu,Shenfu Xie,Yinqian Sun,Haibo Tong,Feifei Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MediSkill-Evo是一个临床智能体，通过四个类型化的技能库（临床技能、过程规则、符号模式和测量程序）在无需骨干网络微调的情况下演化过程知识，显著提升了诊断准确性和治疗意图覆盖率，同时减少了关键失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式临床智能体需要在部分可观察性下收集决定性证据并将其转化为基于证据的行动。仅凭正确的最终诊断无法证明智能体遵守了证据和护理过程约束，因此需要一种能够在不进行骨干网络微调的情况下演化受控过程知识的系统。

Method: 提出MediSkill-Evo，将经验分离为四个类型化技能库；引入来源追踪、支持、重放和控制器定义的安全检查来管理发布；设计过程约束偏好工具，将证据绑定到其来源，拒绝控制器无效的候选者，并使用安全优先的临床过程评判器对行动进行排序。

Result: 在300个保留的Qwen遭遇中，诊断准确率从61.33%提升至69.00%，治疗意图覆盖率从33.62%提升至66.44%，关键失败率从31.00%降低至16.33%；在180个硬隔离条件下，患者行为压力下目标恢复率达93.61%，时间证据达100.00%，分诊红旗达92.22%。

Conclusion: 结果为固定评估套件上的完整系统提供了描述性的端到端证据，但不是针对单个技能库的因果证据或自动评判器的临床验证。

Abstract: Interactive clinical agents must gather decisive evidence and convert it into grounded actions under partial observability. A correct final diagnosis alone does not show that an agent respected evidence and care-process constraints. We introduce MediSkill-Evo, a clinical agent that evolves governed process knowledge without backbone fine-tuning. It separates experience into four typed banks for clinical skills, process rules, symbolic schemas, and measurement procedures. Provenance, support, replay, and controller-defined safety checks govern publication to a frozen test-time snapshot. A Process-Constrained Preference Harness binds evidence to its source, rejects controller-invalid candidates, and ranks actions with a safety-prioritized Clinical Process Critic. We evaluate complete agent systems across two backbone endpoints and six controlled stress dimensions under the same Doctor-turn limit. On 300 held-out Qwen encounters, MediSkill-Evo improves diagnosis accuracy from 61.33 percent to 69.00 percent and treatment-intent coverage from 33.62 percent to 66.44 percent, while reducing automatically scored critical failures from 31.00 percent to 16.33 percent relative to AgentClinic. On 180 hard-isolation conditions derived from 30 cases, target recovery reaches 93.61 percent under patient-behavior pressure, 100.00 percent for temporal evidence, and 92.22 percent for triage red flags. An exploratory 100-case MedSAM comparison evaluates request-gated tool-interface feasibility. These results provide descriptive end-to-end evidence for the complete system on fixed evaluation suites, not causal evidence for an individual bank or clinical validation of the automatic judge.

</details>


### [186] [SkillAlchemy: Open-World Agent Skill Creation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23417)
*Hengjun Wang,Shuyue Wei,Boyi Liu,Jun Yang,Yongxin Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出了SkillAlchemy框架，用于从开放世界材料中自动创建语言代理技能。实验显示该框架相比无技能执行提升19.9个百分点，相比最强自动化基线提升8.6个百分点，性能接近人工策划的技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的语言代理技能创建主要依赖人工编写、模型先验或执行轨迹，这些来源在面对陌生任务时往往不可用。因此需要研究从开放世界材料中创建技能，以解决技能简报中行为相关需求缺失的问题。

Method: 提出SkillAlchemy框架，采用以准入为中心的方法：通过对比证据识别隐含需求，基于证据支持的适用范围接纳候选程序，并将接纳的内容编译成语法引导的技能包。

Result: 在SkillsBench v1.1的87个任务上进行了广泛实验，SkillAlchemy相比无技能执行提升通过率19.9个百分点，相比最强自动化基线提升8.6个百分点，同时达到了与人工策划技能相当的性能。

Conclusion: SkillAlchemy成功实现了从开放世界材料中自动创建可靠的代理技能，为解决陌生任务的技能创建问题提供了有效方案，性能表现接近人工策划水平。

Abstract: Agent skills are reusable procedural artifacts that extend language agents with specialized workflows, tool conventions, and domain behaviors at inference time. However, creating reliable skills still depends largely on human authorship, model priors, or execution traces. These sources are often unavailable for unfamiliar tasks, suggesting the need to create skills from open-world materials. In this paper, we study open-world skill creation: given an underspecified skill brief and a source-access specification, a creator must discover behavior-relevant requirements omitted by the brief and determine how broadly each source-derived procedure is justified. We propose SkillAlchemy, an admission-centered framework for source-grounded skill creation. SkillAlchemy identifies implicit requirements through contrastive evidence, admits candidate procedures based on evidence-supported scope, and compiles the admitted content into a grammar-guided skill package. Extensive experiments across 87 SkillsBench v1.1 tasks demonstrate that SkillAlchemy improves pass rate over no-skill execution by 19.9 percentage points and the strongest automated baseline by 8.6 percentage points, while achieving performance comparable to human-curated skills.

</details>


### [187] [Characterizing Necessary Losers to Explain Tournaments Losers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23446)
*Contet Clément,Umberto Grandi,Jérôme Mengin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究锦标赛规则中候选者落选的形式化解释问题，定义了破坏性最小支持作为子锦标赛，其中候选者无论如何都会落选。针对六种常见锦标赛规则，刻画了必要失败者和可能获胜者的条件，确定了最小破坏性支持的大小，并提供了多项式时间算法（Borda规则除外）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在锦标赛投票中，落选候选者往往需要解释为何失败。现有方法缺乏形式化的解释框架，无法系统地识别导致候选者落选的关键子锦标赛。本文旨在填补这一空白，为"为什么失败者输掉锦标赛"这一关键问题提供可验证的形式化解释。

Method: 定义破坏性最小支持：任何满足在补全剩余比赛后候选者必然落选的最小子锦标赛。将这些解释对应到可解释人工智能中的溯因解释框架。针对maximin、无覆盖集及其加权变体、顶级循环、Copeland和Borda六种锦标赛规则，分别刻画必要失败者和可能获胜者的条件，分析最小支持规模，设计多项式时间算法进行计算。

Result: 对六种锦标赛规则完整刻画了必要失败者和可能获胜者的判定条件；确定了各规则下最小破坏性支持的大小界限；为除Borda外的五种规则提供了多项式时间计算算法；Borda规则的计算复杂度疑似NP完全。

Conclusion: 本研究为锦标赛投票建立了系统的形式化解释框架，使得"为何失败"这一问题可被精确回答。五种规则的高效算法使解释方法具有实用性，而Borda规则的复杂性揭示了不同投票机制在可解释性上的本质差异。该框架为决策系统提供了可验证的问责机制。

Abstract: We study the problem of formally explaining why a candidate was not selected by a given tournament rule, by identifying sub-tournaments in which the candidate loses independently of how the rest of the tournament is completed. We define destructive minimal supports as any minimal sub-tournaments satisfying this property, which in formal explainable artificial intelligence correspond to abductive explanations for the question "Why does the loser lose the tournament?". For six common tournament solutions (maximin, uncovered set and its weighted variant, top-cycle, Copeland, and Borda) we provide characterizations of when a candidate is either a necessary loser or a possible winner, we determine the size of the smallest destructive minimal supports, complemented by polynomial-time algorithms for their computation except for the case of the Borda rule which is suspected to be NP-complete.

</details>


### [188] [StrategyBench: Evaluating Explicit Strategy Induction in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23475)
*Jinghan Tan,Yuanzheng Wang,Lu Chen,Zijun Chen,Yuqian Wang,Maosong Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出StrategyBench基准，用于评估大语言模型从示例中归纳任务策略并应用于新实例的能力，发现显式策略的有效性在不同任务类别和执行条件下差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统few-shot上下文学习直接使用少量示例而不显式抽象任务规则，导致对示例构建高度敏感；而人类学习者通常先从示例中总结规则再应用，本研究旨在评估模型的这种策略归纳能力。

Method: 从BIG-Bench中选择策略可诱导任务构建StrategyBench基准，定义策略质量和下游效用两个评估维度，从任务变化、模型配置和适应设置三个视角分析策略归纳能力。

Result: 实验表明显式策略的有效性在不同任务类别间差异显著，且依赖于策略生成和执行条件；覆盖了类别差异、生成器-执行器选择、示例设计和SFT适应等多方面发现。

Conclusion: 模型从示例中显式归纳任务策略的能力具有重要研究价值，该能力在不同条件下表现不一，StrategyBench为后续研究提供了系统评估框架。

Abstract: As large language models are increasingly used in data-scarce and evolving task scenarios, few-shot in-context learning (ICL) has become a key paradigm for task adaptation. However, direct ICL often uses a small set of examples without explicitly abstracting task rules, making it sensitive to example construction. In contrast, human learners often reduce such sensitivity by first summarizing task rules from examples and then applying them to new instances. To evaluate this ability, we propose StrategyBench, which selects strategy-inducible tasks from BIG-Bench, constructs reference strategies, and defines evaluation metrics along two dimensions: strategy quality and downstream utility. We further analyze strategy induction from three perspectives: task variation, model configuration, and adaptation setting, covering category-wise differences, generator-executor choices, demonstration design, and SFT-based adaptation. Experiments show that explicit strategy utility differs substantially across task categories and depends on both strategy generation and execution conditions. The benchmark is released at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/StrategyBench-D53C.

</details>


### [189] [Multi-Modal Semantic Expansion with Constrained LLM Reranking for Conversational Music Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23484)
*Naman Garg,Sarika Jain,George Fazekas*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文介绍了ACM RecSys 2026 TalkPlayData挑战赛的解决方案，采用三阶段多模态会话音乐推荐系统，通过融合多种检索信号和个性化响应生成，在Blind B上取得0.3213的综合得分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对会话式音乐推荐任务，需要处理多模态信息（音频、视觉、文本）并结合个性化用户偏好，在实时对话系统中实现精准的音乐推荐。

Method: 三阶段管道架构：(1)多模态检索阶段融合7种密集嵌入空间（CF-BPR协同过滤、Qwen3文本/歌词/属性、CLAP音频、SigLIP视觉）以及BM25词法检索和艺术家匹配，通过加权倒数排名融合(RRF)集成；(2)轻量级重排序（历史过滤、流行度平滑、目录多样性惩罚）；(3)基于GPT-4o-mini的个性化多样化响应生成。RRF权重通过差分进化算法优化。

Result: RRF权重优化使MRR提升19.5%。无约束LLM引导的艺术家注入导致nDCG下降18.9%，而保守注入策略表现最佳。提交系统在Blind B测试集上获得综合得分0.3213。

Conclusion: 多模态检索信号融合和优化的RRF权重对会话式音乐推荐效果显著，LLM引导的内容注入需要谨慎控制以避免性能下降，成本和复杂度约束限制了更优配置的部署。

Abstract: We present Team Semiintelligencn's solution for the ACM RecSys 2026 TalkPlayData Challenge, addressing conversational music recommendation through a multi-modal and personalized conversational recommender system. Our submitted system employs a three-stage pipeline: (1) multi-modal retrieval constructing decay-weighted centroids across seven dense embedding spaces - track- and user-level CF-BPR, Qwen3 (metadata, lyrics, attributes), CLAP audio, and SigLIP visual - supplemented by BM25 lexical retrieval and an artist substring-match signal, all fused via weighted Reciprocal Rank Fusion (RRF) with optimized signal weights; (2) lightweight reranking (history filtering, popularity smoothing, and catalog diversity penalization); and (3) persona-diversified response generation using GPT-4o-mini. Beyond this submitted configuration, we report development-time experiments with additional components - constrained LLM-guided artist injection, album continuation signals, XGBoost LambdaMART, and a superior GPT-4.1 response prompt - that were not deployed to Blind B due to cost and complexity constraints. We optimize RRF weights on a 500-session development split via differential evolution, improving MRR by +19.5%. On Blind A, we observe that unconstrained LLM-guided injection across 54 sessions causes catastrophic nDCG regression (-18.9%), while conservative injection on only 9 sessions yields the best observed Blind A nDCG - a finding we present as a Blind A observation warranting further validation. The submitted system achieves a Blind B composite score of 0.3213.

</details>


### [190] [SRPO: Self-Reflective Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23493)
*Jialong Liu,Yuling Shi,Ning Yang,Xiaodong Gu,Zuchao Li*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出SRPO框架，使LLM能够通过自我反思将稀疏奖励转化为密集训练信号，在数学推理和智能体任务上取得SOTA性能，且数据效率极高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类学习中的自我反思机制能有效进行信用分配，将稀疏的结果反馈转化为可操作的指导。然而，这种能力在大语言模型后训练中的潜力尚未被充分探索。现有方法依赖外部评论者、奖励模型或更大的教师模型，且常面临稀疏奖励的挑战。

Method: 提出自我反思策略优化(SRPO)框架：(1)使LLM分析自身完成的轨迹；(2)将错误合成为简洁的"反思补丁"；(3)在学生策略的在线采样上使用反思条件的教师分数作为密集的token级训练信号。该方法无需外部评论者、奖励模型或更大的教师模型，即可将稀疏的终端监督转化为密集的token级学习信号。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B基座模型上实现：(1)AIME'24达到73.3%，仅需缩放监督微调8%的训练FLOPs；(2)WebShop成功率64.7%；(3)ALFWorld成功率76.8%；(4)SWE-Bench-Lite成功率31.2%。在数学推理和长视野智能体基准上均达到SOTA性能，数据效率显著提升。

Conclusion: SRPO成功将人类自我反思机制内化到LLM后训练中，通过自我生成的反思补丁实现高效信用分配。该方法证明了LLM能够在无需外部监督的情况下，通过自反思实现显著的性能提升和训练效率优化，为LLM后训练开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Self-reflection is a powerful mechanism for credit assignment in human learning, converting sparse outcome feedback into actionable guidance. However, its potential for post-training Large Language Models (LLMs) remains underexplored. We propose Self-Reflective Policy Optimization (SRPO), a framework that internalizes this capability. SRPO enables LLMs to analyze their own completed trajectories, synthesize errors into concise "reflection patches," and use reflection-conditioned teacher scores on student on-policy rollouts as dense token-level training signals. This process effectively transforms sparse terminal supervision into dense, token-level learning signals without requiring external critics, separate reward models, or larger teacher models. We demonstrate that SRPO achieves state-of-the-art performance across mathematical reasoning and long-horizon agentic benchmarks with exceptional data efficiency. Using a Qwen3-8B base model, SRPO attains 73.3% on AIME'24 using only 8% (0.08x) of the training FLOPs required by scaled supervised fine-tuning, while significantly improving success rates on WebShop (64.7%), ALFWorld (76.8%), and SWE-Bench-Lite (31.2%). Code is available at https://github.com/Galleons2029/SRPO

</details>


### [191] [Mitigating Reasoning-Induced Misalignment via Safety-Direction Penalty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23497)
*Yipeng Zhao,Qishun Yang,Shenzhe Zhu,Shu Yang,Di Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了推理诱导错位(RIM)现象：在无害推理数据上微调会导致大模型产生有害行为。研究通过表征空间分析发现推理能力与安全行为存在耦合关系，并提出安全方向惩罚(SDP)方法，在保持推理性能的同时恢复模型安全性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理诱导错位(RIM)现象对大语言模型的安全性构成严重挑战——即使仅使用数学、代码、问题求解等无害推理数据进行微调，也会诱导模型产生有害行为。现有研究将RIM归因于神经元级别的纠缠，但未识别表征空间的几何结构，也未提出训练时的解决方案。

Method: 1) 提出表征空间分析方法，提取激活空间中的两个方向：编码推理能力的方向和编码安全行为的方向；2) 分析这两个方向的耦合关系，使用CKA距离比和探测技术定位安全决策层；3) 基于分析结果设计安全方向惩罚(SDP)方法，在推理微调过程中惩罚沿安全方向的位移；4) 通过迭代扩展机制处理初始范围外仍有补偿性位移的情况。

Result: 跨架构、跨规模和跨数据集检查显示RIM并非总是出现。分析发现：提升推理能力的微调会改变安全表征，且位移越大的提示显示更大的安全降级。在Qwen2.5-3B和7B模型上，SDP成功在保持基准推理性能的同时恢复了模型安全性。

Conclusion: 本研究为RIM现象提供了完整的表征空间解释和训练时解决方案。通过识别推理与安全方向的耦合关系并定位关键层，安全方向惩罚方法能够有效缓解推理微调带来的安全风险，为大模型安全推理提供了理论指导和实用技术。

Abstract: Reasoning-Induced Misalignment, where fine-tuning on reasoning data containing no harmful content, including mathematics, code, and problem-solving with chain-of-thought traces can induce harmful behaviors of LLM, posing a serious challenge to the safety of LLM reasoning. Cross-architecture, cross-scale, and cross-dataset checks show that RIM does not always emerge. Previous work attributed RIM to neuron-level entanglement, but did not identify the geometry of the representation space underlying this entanglement or propose a training-time fix. We provide both: a representation-space analysis of RIM and the Safety-Direction Penalty (SDP), which penalizes movement along a learned safety direction during reasoning fine-tuning. The analysis extracts two activation-space directions, one encoding reasoning ability and the other safety behavior. These directions are coupled: fine-tuning that improves reasoning shifts safety representations, and prompts with larger shifts show larger safety degradation. CKA distance ratios and probes locate the safety-decision layers where this shift is most relevant. These findings guide the design of SDP: the coupling motivates penalizing displacement along the safety direction, and the layer localization sets the initial scope. When the initial scope leaves compensatory shifts beyond the penalized layers, the same diagnostics guide iterative expansion. On Qwen2.5-3B and 7B, SDP restores safety while preserving benchmark reasoning performance.

</details>


### [192] [EarthVerse: Benchmarking Scientific Agents Across Dynamic Earth Systems and Natural Hazards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23525)
*Zhiqing Cui,Xinxiang Yin,Yihong Tang,Xinglang Zhang,Yuanzhe Hu,Siru Zhong,Weidong Tang,Yuxuan Liang,Weijia Li,Ming Jin,Shirui Pan,Yuhao Kang,Dingyi Zhuang,Jinhua Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出EarthVerse基准测试，包含405个可复现任务，覆盖199个记录事件和19个灾害家族，用于评估科学智能体在处理地球系统分析任务中的端到端可靠性。对25个模型和智能体系统的评估显示，最佳平均答案单元准确率为84.65%，而最高Strict@95仅为34.81%，揭示了当前智能体在维持跨证据、尺度、单位、计算和物理解释的一致链方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地球系统分析需要从不同来源、尺度、时间和模态的观测数据中重建变化的物理过程。自然灾害使得这项工作具有重要后果，因为不完整的证据可能改变对严重程度、暴露程度和机制的估计。现有科学智能体缺乏对端到端科学可靠性的系统评估基准。

Method: 1. 构建EarthVerse基准，包含405个可复现任务，基于199个记录事件和19个灾害家族；
2. 设计包范围调查任务，要求智能体检查异构事件包、选择兼容证据、执行透明计算、协调来源差异并保留溯源；
3. 提供可执行的基本真值，将每个任务分解为细粒度答案单元，并配备任务特定评分标准；
4. 在受控工具使用协议下评估25个模型和智能体系统；
5. 通过受控研究定位证据访问、工具选择、记忆、推理、交互和科学执行中的失败点。

Result: 在所有评估系统中，最佳平均答案单元准确率为84.65%，而最高Strict@95仅为34.81%。这一差距表明当前智能体通常能够完成单个步骤，但无法在证据、尺度、单位、计算和物理解释之间维持一致的链条。研究识别了智能体在证据访问、工具选择、记忆、推理、交互和科学执行等方面的失败模式。

Conclusion: EarthVerse为测量动态地球系统中端到端科学可靠性提供了可复现的基础。评估结果揭示了当前科学智能体的重要局限性：虽然能够处理个别步骤，但缺乏在整个研究过程中维持一致性的能力。该基准测试为未来改进科学智能体的可靠性和可信度提供了标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Earth-system analysis reconstructs changing physical processes from observations that differ in source, scale, timing, and modality. Natural hazards make this work consequential because incomplete evidence can change estimates of severity, exposure, and mechanism. We introduce EarthVerse, a benchmark that evaluates scientific agents through package-scoped investigations. Its 405 reproducible tasks are grounded in 199 documented events and 19 hazard families. Agents inspect heterogeneous event packages, choose compatible evidence, execute transparent calculations, reconcile source differences, and preserve provenance in the final answer. We provide executable ground truth that decomposes each task into fine-grained answer units, together with task-specific rubrics that assess the supporting research process while allowing multiple valid paths. We evaluate 25 model and agent systems under a controlled tool-using protocol, then use controlled studies to locate failures in evidence access, tool selection, memory, reasoning, interaction, and scientific execution. Across systems, the best mean answer-unit accuracy is 84.65%, while the highest Strict@95 is only 34.81%. The gap shows that current agents often complete individual steps without maintaining a consistent chain across evidence, scales, units, calculations, and physical interpretation. EarthVerse provides a reproducible basis for measuring end-to-end scientific reliability in dynamic Earth systems.

</details>


### [193] [Correcting a learned physical invariant improves world-model rollouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23526)
*Richard Bao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 世界模型能从像素中学习物理守恒量（如能量），但在自主推演时会违反这些约束，表明学习与遵守之间存在差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究世界模型是否真正学习并保持物理动力学中的守恒约束，还是仅仅学会了解码可观测变量而忽略了底层动态规律。

Method: 在仅用钟摆视频训练的冻结DreamerV3模型上，通过无标签搜索方法寻找潜在空间中被潜在转移模型近似守恒的标量不变量，对比了保守模型与阻尼模型，并在自主推演时通过将该不变量投影回初始水平集来测试其对推演误差的影响。

Result: 该方法在独立训练的保守模型中一致恢复了类似能量的守恒量，而在匹配的阻尼模型中未发现类似不变量；自主推演时该不变量会发生漂移；将潜在状态投影回守恒量的初始水平集可减少推演误差。

Conclusion: 世界模型可以从像素中学习具有动态意义的不变量，但存在一个具体的失效模式：即使学习到了物理约束，模型在想象推演时仍可能违反该约束。这区分了动态上有意义的不变量与仅仅可解码的相关量。

Abstract: World models can predict video without learning dynamics that they reliably preserve. We test whether a frozen DreamerV3 trained only on pendulum video learns a scalar that its own latent transition treats as approximately conserved. A label-free search recovers the same energy-like invariant across independently trained conservative models, while the same procedure finds no comparable invariant in matched damped models. During autonomous rollouts, this quantity drifts. Projecting the latent state back toward its initial level set reduces rollout error in all three conservative models, whereas matched random constraints usually increase it. These results distinguish a dynamically meaningful invariant from a merely decodable correlate and reveal a concrete failure mode: a world model can learn a physical constraint from pixels yet violate that constraint when it imagines forward.

</details>


### [194] [How AI Assistance Affects Human Skill Development: A Study of Learning with Logic Puzzles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23543)
*Shang Wu,Catarina G Belem,Shuyuan Fu,Mark Steyvers,Padhraic Smyth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AI辅助虽然能短期内提升任务表现，但会因替代独立思考而削弱技能习得，且这种长期负面影响会被低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究AI辅助在提升短期任务表现与损害长期技能发展之间的权衡关系，探究AI辅助的使用频率及其对技能习得的影响。

Method: 采用对照实验设计逻辑拼图任务，参与者分为AI辅助前、中、后三个阶段；通过实验操控AI请求成本来调节使用频率；使用贝叶斯潜在能力模型分离初始能力、AI后能力及技能变化，量化独立推理与技能发展的关联。

Result: 低AI请求成本导致更高的使用频率；AI辅助阶段请求帮助的参与者在辅助移除后表现更差；基于AI辅助期表现会高估后续无辅助表现；独立解决问题努力程度越高，潜在能力提升越大。

Conclusion: AI辅助通过替代独立推理削弱了技能发展，短期收益伴随长期认知成本；需在AI系统设计中平衡即时辅助与技能培养。

Abstract: While AI assistance can improve human task performance in the short term, it may also undermine the development of skills in the longer term. We examine this tension in a controlled logic-puzzle experiment involving on-demand AI assistance, where participants complete tasks before, during, and after AI is available. By experimentally varying AI request costs, we find that lower-cost assistance induces more frequent AI use. We also find that participants who request AI assistance during the AI-access phase perform worse at the task after assistance is removed, and their subsequent unassisted performance is overestimated when predicted from earlier AI-assisted performance. We use a Bayesian latent ability model to separate initial ability, post-AI ability, and participant-specific skill change, while estimating how independent reasoning during the AI-access phase relates to skill development. The results show that greater independent problem-solving effort is associated with larger gains in latent ability, consistent with the interpretation that skill development is weaker when AI assistance substitutes for independent reasoning.

</details>


### [195] [Prime Agent: A Self-Improving RLM Harness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23552)
*Seth Karten,Alex L. Zhang,Kevin Thomas,Sebastian Müller,Elie Bakouch,Daniel Auras,Mika Senghaas,Fares Obeid,Konstantin Dunas,Johannes Hagemann,Sami Jaghouar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Prime Agent是一个开源的长期任务评估框架，通过持久化IPython REPL、递归子智能体协调和持续化工具，显著提升了语言模型在长期任务中的性能，在ARC-AGI-3测试中将最佳成绩从30%提升至95.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型是顺序处理器，但长期任务需要外部信息和超出模型权重与活动上下文的计算能力。现有框架存在资源管理、状态恢复和多智能体协调等局限性，需要构建一个低摩擦、高表达力的框架来真实测量模型的最大潜在能力。

Method: 采用持久化IPython REPL实现递归语言模型抽象，支持程序化上下文处理和测试时计算；使用Continual Harness保存历史、记忆、技能和子智能体配置；实现递归子智能体间的直接通信；提供Agents View用于会话监控管理；标准化执行、恢复、验证和资源核算流程。

Result: 在ARC-AGI-3 RHAE Best@1指标上从30%提升至95.5%；在长期上下文编程、GPU内核生成、模拟器构建和自主nanoGPT速通等任务上达到或超过现有框架；在Factorio任务中实现持续技术进步和并行化工作流程。

Conclusion: Prime Agent成功构建了一个将框架故障与模型故障分离的框架，让策略构建完全由模型决定，能够更准确地测量模型的真实最大能力，并在多个挑战性基准测试中显著超越现有方案。

Abstract: Language models are sequential processors, but long-horizon agency requires external information and computation beyond model weights and active context. Prime Agent is an open-source harness for long-horizon evaluation and coding-agent workflows. A persistent IPython REPL follows the Recursive Language Model abstraction for programmatic context processing and test-time compute, while Continual Harness preserves histories, memories, skills, prompts, and subagent specifications across trajectories. Recursive subagents coordinate through direct agent-to-agent communication, and the Agents View lets humans inspect and manage daemon-backed sessions. Prime Agent standardizes execution, recovery, verification, and resource accounting while leaving strategy construction to the model. This low-friction, expressive membrane prevents harness failures from becoming model failures and pushes measurement toward the model's true maximal underlying capability. Prime Agent raises ARC-AGI-3 RHAE Best@1 from 30% to 95.5% and matches or exceeds native and popular harnesses across long-context coding, GPU-kernel generation, emulator construction, and autonomous nanoGPT speedruns. On Factorio, we find refinement allows for continuous technology progression and dedicated subagents enable parallelized work. Code is available at https://github.com/PrimeIntellect-ai/prime-agent.

</details>


### [196] [ReWorld: An Interactive World Model with Long-Horizon Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23565)
*Zhifei Chen,Luozhou Wang,Guibao Shen,Dongyu Yan,Shuai Yang,Tianshuo Xu,Yihua Du,Wei Wang,Tianyi Gui,Lianghua Huang,Yingcong Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ReWorld提出了一种交互式世界模型，通过分离训练时的控制与记忆需求并在推理时进行约束，实现了在固定内存预算下保持长时记忆的能力，同时支持实时流式生成高质量视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式世界模型面临结构性矛盾：控制需要短时视野以实现实时响应，而记忆需要无界视野以记住历史信息。现有方法难以同时满足这两个需求——滑动窗口会过早丢弃关键信息，而全KV注意力会耗尽内存。

Method: 1. 训练阶段：混合注意力头窗口设计，大部分头关注近期信息，少量全局头关注完整历史；随机头路由防止特定能力绑定到特定头；随机块丢弃使稀疏历史成为分布内数据。2. 推理阶段：固定预算的有界KV缓存配合姿态索引的地标库，检索当前姿态附近的地标。3. 数据引擎：度量尺度对齐的八源数据（虚幻渲染、游戏、真实视频），统一物理动作尺度。4. 蒸馏压缩：通过LoRA适配器的分布匹配蒸馏，将采样压缩至四步。

Result: 在三维评估协议（动作跟随、长时记忆、视频质量）下，相比六个最新交互式世界模型：达到最佳控制保真度（旋转误差11.95°）和最佳相机运动一致性；最佳生成质量；在64秒、384个潜变量的往返轨迹上，固定12块缓存仍能重建起始视图，而滑动窗口早已丢失证据，全KV注意力则内存溢出。支持704x1280分辨率的实时流式生成，覆盖照片级真实、游戏风格和风格化世界。

Conclusion: ReWorld成功解决了交互式世界模型中控制与记忆的结构性矛盾，通过分离训练时的注意力窗口和推理时的固定内存预算，实现了长时记忆与实时生成的统一。单一骨干网络可同时服务高保真多步模式和实时交互模式，为交互式世界模型提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: An interactive world model must follow the user's actions, remember the places it has shown, and stream in real time. The tension is structural: control wants a short horizon, memory wants an unbounded one. ReWorld separates the two during training and bounds them at inference. Mixed per-head attention windows confine most heads to the recent past while a small set of global heads attends over the entire history, and random head routing keeps either capability from binding to particular heads; random chunk dropping makes sparse histories in-distribution. At inference the whole past lives under a fixed budget: a bounded KV cache backed by a pose-indexed landmark bank, from which the model retrieves the landmarks nearest the current pose. A metric-scale-aligned data engine places eight sources -- Unreal-rendered fly-throughs, game roaming, and real-world footage -- on one physical action scale, so the same key press moves the camera the same distance in every source, and palindrome trajectories supply the revisit evidence that memory training needs. Distribution-matching distillation confined to a LoRA adapter then compresses sampling to four steps: one backbone serves both a high-fidelity multi-step mode and a real-time interactive one, streaming 704x1280 video across photorealistic, game-style, and stylized worlds. Under a three-axis protocol covering action following, long-horizon recall, and video quality, against six recent interactive world models it attains the best control fidelity ($11.95^\circ$ rotation error and the best camera-motion consistency) and the best generation quality; and on minute-long out-and-back rollouts ($64$\,s, $384$ latents), its fixed 12-chunk cache still regenerates the starting view -- at rollout lengths where a sliding window has long evicted the evidence and full-KV attention runs out of memory.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [197] [Age-Optimal Target Wake Time: Provably Good Wake Schedules for Energy-Constrained Wi-Fi Status Updating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21596)
*Haoyu Wang,Bo Sheng,Xiaoqian Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文设计了一种针对信息新鲜度优化的TWT调度器，在满足每个站点能耗约束的前提下最小化加权平均信息年龄（AoI），并证明其具有常数因子近似保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有TWT调度器主要优化能耗或吞吐量，仅将信息新鲜度视为约束条件且无性能保证。随着物联网等应用对信息时效性要求的提高，需要专门针对信息新鲜度设计TWT调度方案。

Method: 1. 推导TWT在每服务周期块衰落下的更新精确AoI模型，并通过802.11ax仿真验证（平均误差~1%）；
2. 证明非抢占式TWT在调度密度1时可能不可行，确定小优先最佳适应打包的成功条件；
3. 设计Harmonic-Greedy调度器，结合凸松弛、锚优化的2的幂次舍入和均匀分布保护机制；
4. 在ns-3中实现完整系统，包括TWT唤醒/休眠机制和调度器。

Result: 1. 理论上证明了算法是常数因子近似：在温和粒度假设下为4/ln2≈5.77倍，无条件下为6/ln2≈8.66倍；
2. 仿真结果表明该调度器是唯一在所有情况下都接近松弛下界的方案：与能耗贪婪基线相比，在能耗约束主导时持平，在调度密度约束主导且需按AoI权重或信道质量重新分配时优势达4-36%。

Conclusion: 该研究首次为TWT调度问题提供了针对信息新鲜度的系统化解决方案，兼具理论保证和实际可行性，填补了现有调度器在信息时效性优化方面的空白。

Abstract: Target Wake Time (TWT), introduced in IEEE 802.11ax, lets an access point schedule exactly when each station wakes, transmits, and dozes. Existing TWT schedulers optimize energy or throughput, treating information freshness at best as a constraint and offering no performance guarantees. We design the wake schedule itself for freshness: minimize the weighted average Age of Information (AoI) over stations subject to per-station energy budgets, where the decision variables are the TWT triples (wake interval, offset, service period duration). We derive a renewal-exact AoI model for TWT under per-SP block fading and validate it against packet-level 802.11ax simulation with ~1% mean error. We show that, unlike preemptive scheduling, non-preemptive TWT packing can be infeasible at schedule density 1, and identify the granularity condition under which a small-first best-fit packer provably succeeds. Around this we build Harmonic-Greedy, a scheduler combining a convex relaxation, anchor-optimized power-of-two rounding, and a best-of-uniform safeguard, and prove it is a constant-factor approximation: 4/ln 2 ~= 5.77 under a mild granularity assumption and 6/ln 2 ~= 8.66 unconditionally. We implement the complete system in ns-3 -- a TWT wake/doze mechanism integrated with the power-save architecture, plus the scheduler -- and show that it is the only scheduler that stays near a relaxation lower bound across all regimes: against a strong energy-greedy baseline it ties when per-station energy floors already pin the periods, and wins by 4-36% exactly where the schedule density is binding and must be redistributed by AoI weight or channel quality rather than by energy budget -- the regime our analysis identifies.

</details>


### [198] [Building A CSFQ-Inspired Transport for Switched CXL Memory Pooling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21731)
*Zerui Guo,Emily Shriver,Ming Liu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对交换式CXL内存池化系统中的性能干扰问题，本文提出了MemChannel传输层，通过发送端驱动的传输协议实现端到端带宽管理，有效解决了并发内存流之间的性能隔离问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新兴的交换式CXL内存池化系统虽然前景广阔，但由于主机核心与远程DIMM之间的并发内存流共享数据路径但缺乏性能控制，导致严重的性能干扰。本文基于XConn Apollo CXL交换机系统性地刻画了内存池化设备，识别出三个核心问题：主机内部争用、交换结构内拥塞以及主机-远程DIMM交互管理缺失。

Method: 提出MemChannel传输层，提供mchannel抽象来管理竞争内存流之间的端到端交换结构带宽。核心思想是构建一个受核心无状态公平排队(CSFQ)启发的发送端驱动、交换结构感知的传输协议，根据估算的核心到CXL-DIMM带宽可用性向每个mchannel准入适量的CXL请求。针对CXL的特殊性，引入了基于时间的速率控制、主机侧准入控制、跨主机记账、新型拥塞信号、基于流体模型的速率估计以及基于时延的链路容量调整机制。

Result: 从零构建了MemChannel系统，支持未经修改的应用程序。在交换式内存池化环境下的评估表明，该方法在性能隔离、可扩展性和多租户三个维度均表现出显著有效性。

Conclusion: MemChannel成功地为交换式CXL内存池化系统提供了有效的传输层解决方案，通过创新的带宽管理和流量控制机制，显著改善了系统的性能隔离能力和多租户支持，为CXL内存池化技术的实际部署提供了重要支撑。

Abstract: Emerging switched CXL memory pooling systems, albeit promising, suffer from significant performance interference due to the shared but performance-uncontrolled data path among concurrent memory streams between a host core and a remote DIMM. We systematically characterize a memory pooling appliance based on XConn's Apollo CXL switch and identify three issues: intra-host contention, in-fabric congestion, and unmanaged host-remote DIMM interaction.
  This paper presents a new transport layer, MemChannel, which provides the mchannel abstraction to manage end-to-end fabric bandwidth among competing memory flows and enable application-specific traffic for switched CXL memory pooling. Our key idea is to build a sender-driven, fabric-informed transport protocol, inspired by Core-Stateless Fair Queueing (CSFQ), that admits just the right amount of CXL requests to each mchannel based on the estimated core-to-CXL-DIMM bandwidth availability. To address CXL-induced idiosyncrasies, MemChannel introduces time-based rate control, host-side admission control, cross-host bookkeeping, new congestion signals, rate estimation based on the fluid model, and delay-based link-capacity adjustment. We build MemChannel from scratch and support unmodified applications. Evaluations over switched memory pooling demonstrate its effectiveness from performance-isolation, scalability, and multi-tenancy perspectives.

</details>


### [199] [Reliability-Aware Scheduling for Digital Twin Maintenance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21866)
*Milica Jankov,Carlo Fischione*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 针对工业物联网中资源受限且物理过程动态变化场景，本文提出基于集成分歧指标(EDI)的可靠性感知调度器R-VoU，通过优先调度高价值观测有效降低数字孪生估计误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业物联网系统中，当上行链路资源有限且物理过程在数字孪生模型训练后发生变化时，传统调度策略难以保持数字孪生的准确性。仅依赖最新观测无法保证DT精度，因为已训练模型可能与底层过程不再匹配，亟需新的调度策略来解决此问题。

Method: 定义集成分歧指标(EDI)作为不确定性度量，利用独立训练的DT预测器间的估计差异计算。基于EDI提出R-VoU调度器，优先调度能通过降低不确定性和预测误差来最大程度改进DT的观测，并利用接收观测与当前预测的差异在线调整DT模型。

Result: 在过程制造数据上的实验表明，在有限通信预算下，R-VoU在所有仅使用调度决策前可用信息的调度器中实现了最低的综合成本和DT估计误差。

Conclusion: 所提出的R-VoU调度器有效解决了资源受限且物理过程变化场景下的数字孪生维护问题，通过可靠性感知的调度决策显著提升了系统性能。

Abstract: In Industrial Internet of Things systems, learning-enabled Digital Twins (DTs) support remote monitoring by using data reported by distributed devices to maintain digital representations of physical processes. When uplink resources are limited, a base station cannot collect new observations from every device at every communication slot and must decide which devices should transmit. This decision becomes challenging when the physical process changes after the DT models have already been trained. In such cases, recent observations alone may not keep the DT accurate, because the learned model may no longer match the underlying process. This paper studies how to schedule observation requests so that the DT maintained at the base station remains close to the true physical process. We define the Ensemble Disagreement Indicator (EDI) as an uncertainty measure computed at the base station from the spread among estimates produced by independently trained DT predictors. Building on EDI, we propose R-VoU, a reliability-aware value-of-update scheduler that prioritizes the observations expected to most improve the DT by reducing a cost based on uncertainty and predicted DT error. The DT is further adjusted online using the difference between received observations and current predictions. Experiments on process manufacturing data show that, under limited communication budgets, R-VoU achieves the lowest combined cost and DT estimation error among the compared schedulers that use only information available before each scheduling decision.

</details>


### [200] [Pruned Traffic Trees: Native Semantic Compression with a Protocol-Structured Model Family for Encrypted Traffic Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21874)
*Yuantu Luo,Jun Tao,Xiangyu Xu,Linxiao Yu,Kangying Li*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出PTT模型家族，通过将协议结构作为压缩单元，在加密流量分类任务中实现了性能与效率的优异平衡，参数减少80%、计算量降低98%且速度提升8倍以上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习在加密流量分类中表现优异，但其计算成本限制了在路由器等资源受限设备上的部署。现有压缩方法主要针对权重、通道等，未显式确定应保留哪些协议字段和结构上下文。

Method: 提出三级协议结构化模型PTT：PTT-Full从完整协议树图(PTG)学习协议结构化表示和字段显著性，采用流级自监督学习和协议感知稀疏执行；通过学习到的显著性构建蒸馏PTG用于PTT-Distilled；PTT-Lite通过结构对齐迁移和流级logits蒸馏减少宽度。

Result: 在CSTNET-TLS1.3和CipherSpectrum数据集上，PTT-Full Macro-F1分别达0.9519和0.9416；PTT-Lite保持0.9325和0.9136的性能，同时参数减少80.3%和61.3%，有效GFLOPs降低98.85%和98.78%，CPU推理速度提升8.75倍和8.46倍。

Conclusion: 将协议结构本身作为压缩对象，能够在加密流量分类中实现有效的性能-效率权衡，为轻量级部署提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep learning has achieved strong performance in encrypted traffic classification (ETC), yet its computational cost limits deployment on resource-constrained network devices such as routers and middleboxes. Existing compression methods mainly operate on weights, channels, hidden representations, or predictions, but do not explicitly determine which protocol fields and structural contexts should remain. We propose Pruned Traffic Trees (PTT), a three-level protocol-structured model family that treats native protocol structures as compression units. PTT-Full learns protocol-structured representations and field salience from complete Protocol Tree Graphs (PTGs), with flow-level self-supervised learning and protocol-presence-aware sparse execution. The learned salience and TopK+$k$ closure construct Distilled PTGs (PTG-Ds) for PTT-Distilled, while PTT-Lite inherits this topology and reduces width through structure-aligned transfer and flow-level logits distillation. Under flow-disjoint and Strong Information Information (SII)-masked settings, PTT-Full achieves Macro-F1 scores of 0.9519 and 0.9416 on CSTNET-TLS1.3 and CipherSpectrum, while PTT-Lite retains 0.9325 and 0.9136 with 80.3\% and 61.3\% fewer parameters, 98.85\% and 98.78\% lower effective GFLOPs, and 8.75$\times$ and 8.46$\times$ CPU inference speedups. These results demonstrate that treating protocol structure itself as the compression object enables effective performance-efficiency trade-offs for lightweight ETC.

</details>


### [201] [Fleet-Scale Pod Deployment with VPC-Native Networking in Managed Kubernetes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22210)
*Sri Saran Balaji Vellore Rajakumar,Jayanth Varavani,Murat Parlakisik,Pavani Panakanti*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文对Amazon EKS的三种VPC原生Pod部署模式进行了大规模测量研究。研究发现，ENI预分配和IPv4前缀委托模式将地址配置移出Pod创建关键路径，在400节点部署80,000个Pod时仅需约495秒，而传统secondary-IP模式需要7,403秒。前缀委托达到约162 pods/s的部署速率，与最快的overlay基线相当，同时节省了IP地址空间（每个/28块包含16个地址但仅占用1个IPv4槽位）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 托管的Kubernetes Pod部署通常在VPC原生和overlay网络之间选择。公共EKS文档描述了prefix模式的容量和配置，但以往的CNI研究主要测量稳态吞吐量和延迟。缺乏对大规模Pod部署场景下不同VPC原生模式的操作性能测量研究，特别是部署速度和资源使用效率的对比分析。

Method: 在Amazon EKS上对三种VPC原生Pod部署模式进行操作测量研究：单独secondary-IP分配、ENI预分配和IPv4前缀委托，使用Cilium和Calico作为overlay基线。比较各模式的代表性warm-pool设置，测试规模达到400节点、80,000个Pod。研究关注Pod部署时间、部署速率、EC2配置调用次数以及网络地址使用效率。

Result: 在400节点测试中，三地址secondary-IP warm pool需要7,403秒部署80,000个Pod；ENI预分配和前缀委托模式仅需约495秒。前缀委托达到约162 pods/s部署速率，与最快的overlay基线和ENI预分配处于同一范围。前缀委托通过/28块分配（每块16个地址占用1个IPv4槽位），一个有30个槽位的接口可承载29个前缀，提供最多464个潜在Pod地址，而单独IP分配每ENI约30个槽位。前缀委托还降低了网络地址使用率，并保留了VPC直连可达性和流日志可见性。

Conclusion: IPv4前缀委托模式通过将地址配置移出Pod创建关键路径，显著提升了大规模Pod部署性能，达到与overlay网络相当的部署速率，同时在IP地址利用效率上具有优势。权衡是需要连续的子网空间用于/28分配。对于需要大规模快速Pod部署且希望保持VPC原生连接性的场景，前缀委托是更优选择。

Abstract: Managed Kubernetes pod deployment is often presented as a choice between VPC-native and overlay networking. Public EKS documentation describes prefix-mode capacity and configuration, while prior CNI studies primarily measure steady-state throughput and latency. This paper presents an operational measurement study of fleet-scale pod deployment across three VPC-native modes on Amazon EKS: individual secondary-IP allocation, ENI preallocation, and IPv4 prefix delegation, using Cilium and Calico as overlay baselines. We compare representative mode-specific warm-pool settings, so the results describe combined operating points. At 400 nodes, the tested three-address secondary-IP warm pool required 7,403 seconds to place 80,000 pods. With the tested ENI-preallocation and prefix-delegation settings, the same workload completed in approximately 495 seconds, moving address provisioning off the pod-creation critical path. In the reported 400-node runs, prefix delegation achieved approximately 162 pods/s, within the same observed range as the fastest overlay baseline and ENI-preallocation runs, while requiring fewer successful EC2 provisioning calls per pod than individual-IP allocation by allocating addresses in /28 blocks. Because each /28 contains 16 addresses and occupies one IPv4 slot, an interface with 30 slots carries 29 prefixes, representing up to 464 potential pod addresses, compared with approximately 30 IPv4 slots per ENI for individual-IP allocation when kubelet and CNI limits are configured accordingly. Prefix delegation also has lower documented network-address usage and retains direct VPC reachability and flow-log visibility but the trade-off is that /28 allocations require contiguous subnet space.

</details>


### [202] [STAR-GS: Truthful and Visibility-Aware Resource Scheduling for Ground Station as a Service](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22222)
*Zhiying Wang,Xiaojian Wang,Huayue Gu,Zhishan Guo,Ruozhou Yu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出STAR-GS机制，解决低地球轨道卫星星座地面站即服务（GSaaS）的资源调度问题，在保证真实性和可行性约束下最大化服务商收益，性能接近MILP且运行效率更高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低地球轨道卫星星座的快速增长对高效、可扩展的下行链路服务提出了更高需求。地面站即服务（GSaaS）为卫星运营商提供了按需接入模式，但商业GSaaS提供商需要在异构数据需求、重叠可见窗口、严格截止期限和策略性投标行为等复杂约束下，在多个卫星之间调度有限的地面站带宽资源。

Method: 提出STAR-GS机制，结合拍卖理论与可调度性分析，包含四个核心组件：(1)投标感知的任务准入控制；(2)最佳适配的地面站分配；(3)基于最早截止期限优先（EDF）的带宽调度；(4)关键支付定价。该机制在卫星轨道动态、带宽约束和下行链路任务截止期限约束下，联合确定任务准入、地面站分配、带宽分配和支付定价。

Result: 基于Ansys Systems Tool Kit (STK)的仿真实验表明：(1)STAR-GS持续获得比启发式基准更高的收益；(2)以显著更低的运行时间达到接近混合整数线性规划（MILP）的性能；(3)能够平滑扩展至包含多达900个任务的工作负载规模。

Conclusion: STAR-GS成功解决了GSaaS资源调度的NP-hard问题，在激励任务所有者真实报告私有估值的同时，确保被接纳的任务能够在截止期限前可行完成，实现了真实性、可行性与收益最大化的有效平衡。

Abstract: The rapid growth of Low Earth Orbit satellite constellations has created increasing demand for efficient and scalable downlink services. Ground Station as a Service (GSaaS) provides an on-demand access model for satellite operators, but commercial GSaaS providers must schedule limited ground-station bandwidth among multiple satellites with heterogeneous data demands, overlapping visibility windows, strict deadlines, and strategic bidding behaviors. This paper studies GSaaS resource scheduling from a ground-station-centric perspective, where the provider jointly determines task admission, ground-station assignment, bandwidth allocation, and payments. Under satellite orbital dynamics, bandwidth constraints, and downlink task deadlines, maximizing the provider's revenue is NP-hard. To address this challenge, we propose STAR-GS, a truthful and feasibility-aware scheduling mechanism that combines bid-aware admission control, best-fit ground-station assignment, Earliest Deadline First (EDF)-based bandwidth scheduling, and critical-payment pricing. By integrating auction theory with schedulability analysis, STAR-GS incentivizes task owners to truthfully report their private valuations while ensuring that admitted tasks can be feasibly completed before their deadlines. Simulations using Ansys Systems Tool Kit (STK) show that STAR-GS consistently achieves higher revenue than heuristic baselines, obtains near-MILP performance with substantially lower runtime, and scales smoothly to workloads containing up to 900 tasks.

</details>


### [203] [Advanced LLM-Enhanced Intent-Based 5G Network Management using Dynamic Semantic Routes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22644)
*Thomas Benton Townsend,Dimitrios Michael Manias*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于语义路由器的动态路由方法，用于在基于意图的5G+核心网中识别网络操作员的自然语言意图并提取关键细节，实现了静态和动态路由在意图解析方面的成功应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI和大语言模型在日常应用中的普及，将其与网络管理和编排实践相结合成为新兴趋势。特别是在基于意图的网络中，网络操作员希望通过自然语言控制网络，因此需要有效的意图识别和细节提取机制。

Method: 采用语义路由器（semantic router）实现动态路由，从网络操作员的自然语言提示中识别意图并提取必要的意图实现细节。通过评估多种编码器的静态路由选择性能，以及对一系列真实操作员提示的动态路由细节提取准确性进行验证。

Result: 实验结果表明，静态路由和动态路由在细节提取和模式格式化方面均表现成功，能够有效处理基于意图的5G+核心网络中的自然语言意图解析任务。

Conclusion: 该研究验证了动态路由与语义路由器在LLM增强的基于意图的网络中的可行性，为网络操作员使用自然语言进行网络控制提供了有效的技术方案。

Abstract: As the use of Artificial Intelligence (AI) and Large Language Models (LLMs) is becoming common in everyday applications, their ability to interpret natural language has increased significantly. An emerging application of AI is integration with network management and orchestration practices. An instance of this integration is LLM-enhanced intent-based networking, where network operators will control a network using natural language. This work presents the use of dynamic routes with a semantic router to identify an intent from a network operator's prompt and extract necessary details for intent fulfillment in intent-based 5G+ core networks. Furthermore, the performance of static route selection is assessed by evaluating multiple encoders and dynamic route detail extraction accuracy against a series of realistic operator prompts. The presented results show that static and dynamic routes are successful in detail extraction and schema formatting.

</details>


### [204] [Spatio-temporal Path Optimization for Stabilizer-Code-Protected Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22766)
*Yuanbo Zhang,Qianfan Wang,Yangming Zhao,Lin Chen,Deke Guo*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文针对量子网络中量子纠错保护的直接传输，提出了跨层时空路径优化框架和多种路由算法，显著降低了路由成本和网络拥塞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在嘈杂的量子网络中，如何联合选择传输路径、恢复位置和保护方案以保护脆弱量子态是一个关键问题，现有方法缺乏有效的优化策略。

Method: 提出跨层时空路径优化问题建模，针对块状稳定子码保护的直接传输，设计了固定方案和灵活方案的单流路由算法以及多流路由算法。

Result: 仿真结果表明，与Decode-Always方法相比，单流平均路由成本降低约25-30%；与Greedy-Assignment方法相比，多流吞吐量归一化拥塞降低约28-31%。

Conclusion: 该框架可作为逻辑错误和逻辑寿命约束下量子纠错感知路由的算法构建模块，为量子网络传输优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Quantum Error Correction~(QEC)-protected direct transmission is a fundamental approach to preserve fragile quantum states while they are physically forwarded across noisy quantum networks. When a logical qubit traverses multiple hops, selected QEC-capable nodes may recover the encoded state before it continues along the route. The feasibility and cost of the final transmission strategy therefore depend on how we jointly choose the path, the recovery locations, and the protection schemes. In this paper, we formulate and analyze a cross-layer spatio-temporal path optimization problem for block-style stabilizer-code-protected direct transmission. Our main results include fixed-scheme and flexible-scheme single-flow routing algorithms, as well as a multi-flow routing algorithm. The framework developed in this paper can serve as an algorithmic building block for QEC-aware routing under logical-error and logical-lifetime constraints. Simulations show that it reduces single-flow average routing cost by approximately 25--30\% over Decode-Always and lowers multi-flow throughput-normalized congestion by approximately 28--31\% over Greedy-Assignment.

</details>


### [205] [The Surprising Effectiveness of LLMs in BGP Security: Mining An Unprecedented Amount of Incidents and Boosting Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22812)
*Libin Liu,Wenzhou Yang,Li Chen,Dan Li,Xiuting Xu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文利用LLM辅助提取流程从运营商邮件列表中构建了232个验证的路由异常事件基准数据集（规模为现有数据集的11.89倍），并提出了ROUTELELM模型，在事件级准确率达到87.13%，消息级准确率达到94.65%，显著优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BGP安全对互联网基础设施至关重要，但路由异常检测研究进展受限于公开事件数据集的稀缺性（仅包含18个记录案例）。作者发现公开的运营商邮件列表（如NANOG和AusNOG）包含大量未被充分利用的真实路由异常报告，为构建更全面的数据集提供了机会。

Method: 开发LLM辅助提取流程从历史讨论线程中识别候选事件，经专家验证后构建基准数据集。基于对现有检测系统泛化能力差和通用LLM表现不稳定的观察，设计ROUTELELM模型，该模型整合了BGP语义感知的分词方法、路由域自适应技术和时序感知的路由证据检索机制。

Result: 构建了包含232个验证路由异常事件的基准数据集，规模比现有数据集大11.89倍。现有路由异常检测系统在多样化真实事件上泛化能力差。ROUTELELM模型实现87.13%的事件级准确率和94.65%的消息级准确率，分别超越最强基线55.30%和68.50%。

Conclusion: 通过开源验证的路由异常基准、微调模型和实现代码支持未来BGP安全研究。证明了LLM在路由异常检测领域的潜力，同时强调了领域适配和语义感知设计的重要性。

Abstract: Border Gateway Protocol (BGP) security is critical to Internet infrastructure, yet progress in routing anomaly detection has been limited by the scarcity of publicly available incident datasets, which contain only 18 recorded cases. We observe that public operator mailing lists, e.g., NANOG and AusNOG, contain abundant yet largely untapped reports of real-world routing anomalies. To leverage this source, we develop an LLM-assisted extraction pipeline that identifies 244 candidate incidents from historical discussion threads. After expert validation, we curate a verified benchmark containing 232 confirmed routing anomaly events, making it 11.89X larger than existing dataset.
  Using this benchmark, we show that existing routing anomaly detection systems generalize poorly to diverse real-world incidents. At the same time, we find that some general-purpose LLMs without routing-specific adaptation can identify a subset of routing anomalies, but their performance varies across models and remains insufficient for reliable routing anomaly detection. Motivated by this observation, we design ROUTELLM, an LLM-based routing anomaly detector that integrates BGP-semantic-aware tokenization, routing-domain adaptation, and time-aware routing evidence retrieval. Experimental results show that ROUTELLM achieves 87.13% event-level accuracy and 94.65% message-level accuracy, outperforming the strongest baselines by 55.30% and 68.50%, respectively. We open-source the verified routing anomaly benchmark, fine-tuned model, and implementation code to support future research on BGP security.

</details>


### [206] [Multipath Adaptive Video Streaming with Multiple Description Neural Video Codec over 5G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22891)
*Xinyue Hu,Ziyan Wu,Jiaxiang Tang,Wei Ye,Qixin Zhang,Eman Ramadan,Ali Anwar,Zhi-Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 论文提出NeuralMDC，一种与多路径流媒体协同设计的神经多描述视频编解码器，将视频块编码为独立可解码、相互可精炼的描述流，显著提升了5G动态网络下的视频传输质量和用户体验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有5G网络多路径视频传输系统依赖单编解码器或分层编解码器，其解码依赖性在快速变化的5G网络条件（如遮挡、切换、异构路径容量）下导致传输脆弱：关键视频数据的瞬时不足会直接引发卡顿并降低QoE。

Method: 提出NeuralMDC神经多描述视频编解码器，将每个视频块编码为独立可解码、相互可精炼的描述流，每个描述流覆盖完整视频块。该设计将多路径传输单元从依赖的数据包或层级转变为独立的块级流，缺失的流主要降低质量而非导致无法解码。同时开发用户空间多路径流媒体系统，将描述流映射到异构5G路径并采用简单有效的调度逻辑。

Result: 在轨迹驱动的仿真和实际5G实验中，NeuralMDC相比现有单编解码器、分层编解码器和神经流媒体系统，QoE提升26%-44%，视频质量提升最高41.8%，卡顿率保持在0.32%以下。

Conclusion: NeuralMDC通过将视频编码为独立可解码的描述流，有效解决了5G动态网络条件下多路径视频传输的脆弱性问题，显著提升了传输质量和用户体验，为5G网络中的高带宽、高分辨率视频流媒体应用提供了可靠的技术方案。

Abstract: 5G networks employ multiple radio channels to meet growing demands for bandwidth and high-resolution video streaming for emerging applications. However, existing multipath video systems are largely designed around monolithic codecs, which require sufficiently complete chunk delivery, or layered codecs, which depend on timely base-layer delivery. Under fast-varying 5G conditions with blockage, handovers, and heterogeneous path capacities, we observe that decoding dependencies in existing codecs make multipath delivery fragile: transient under-delivery of critical video data can directly trigger stalls and degrade QoE.
  This paper proposes NeuralMDC, a neural multiple-description video codec co-designed with multipath streaming for dynamic 5G networks. NeuralMDC encodes each video chunk into independently decodable and mutually refinable description streams, each spanning the full chunk. This design changes the multipath delivery unit from dependent packets or layers to independent chunk-level streams, so missing streams primarily reduce quality rather than making the chunk undecodable. Built on NeuralMDC, we develop a user-space multipath streaming system that maps description streams to heterogeneous 5G paths with simple yet effective scheduling logic. Across trace-driven emulation and operational 5G experiments, NeuralMDC improves QoE by 26%-44% over existing monolithic, layered, and neural streaming systems, improves video quality by up to 41.8%, and keeps stall ratios below 0.32%.

</details>


### [207] [Channel-Token Attention for Reliable Dynamic Spectrum Access under Bursty Primary-User Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22992)
*Krishna Acharya,Dinanath Padhya,Utsab Dahal,Ashish Kandel,Binod Sapkota*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TACAN，一种基于Transformer编码器和近端策略优化的集中式动态频谱接入策略，通过将信道和用户上下文表示为令牌，在突发主用户活动下实现高效频谱共享，相比贪婪策略和PPO+MLP显著提升了接入成功率和降低了延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动态频谱接入需要在主用户突发活动条件下协调次级用户，同时保持数据包的可靠性和延迟性能。现有方法难以在复杂的频谱环境中实现高效的信道分配和用户体验平衡。

Method: TACAN将每个信道表示为包含占用历史和自动调制分类熵的令牌，用户上下文令牌包含队列类别、延迟和用户身份信息。采用Transformer编码器作为策略网络，从占用贪婪策略热启动，使用近端策略优化（PPO）进行训练优化。冻结策略在所有时隙保持信道分配，通过留存轨迹回放区分待机分配成功与数据包接入和投递。

Result: 在20信道、60主设备、4次级用户的网络中，TACAN实现92.53%数据包接入成功率，相比贪婪策略的89.94%和PPO+MLP的83.53%有显著提升。成对增益为2.59个百分点（95%置信区间1.89-3.29），极端负载下增益达7.67个百分点。平均投递延迟从1.208降至1.123时隙，条件用户可靠性差距从9.69降至3.15个百分点。投递数据包保持到达限制，未声称吞吐量增益。

Conclusion: TACAN通过Transformer架构和令牌化表示有效解决了动态频谱接入中的核心挑战，在突发主用户干扰下实现了更可靠的次级用户接入和更低的延迟，证明了深度强化学习在复杂频谱管理场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Dynamic spectrum access must coordinate secondary users under bursty primary-user activity while preserving packet reliability and delay. We present TACAN, a centralized policy that represents each channel as a token containing occupancy history and automatic-modulation-classification entropy; a context token supplies queue class, delay and user identity. A Transformer encoder is warm-started from an occupancy-greedy policy and refined with proximal policy optimization. The frozen policies were trained to maintain a channel assignment in every slot, including when queues were empty. We therefore replay them on held-out trajectories and distinguish standby assignment success from packet-present access and packet delivery. In a 20-channel network with 60 primary devices and 4 secondary users, TACAN achieves 92.53% +/- 0.47 packet-present access success, compared with 89.94% for Greedy and 83.53% for PPO+MLP. Its paired gain over Greedy is 2.59 points (parametric 95% CI 1.89-3.29), with wins in all five seeds; the exact two-sided sign-test value is 0.0625. The gain rises from 0.57 points at normal primary-user load to 7.67 points at extreme load. TACAN also reduces mean delivery delay from 1.208 to 1.123 slots and the conditional user-reliability gap from 9.69 to 3.15 points. Delivered packets per SU-slot remain arrival-limited (30.12% versus 30.11%), so no packet-throughput gain is claimed.

</details>


### [208] [Distributed Trajectory Planning and Resource Allocation for Dynamic Multi-UAV Collaborative Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23123)
*Tiankui Zhang,Wenlong Xu,Tianyi Shi,Xiaoxia Xu,Arumugam Nallanathan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多智能体深度强化学习的多无人机分布式移动边缘计算框架，通过Stackelberg博弈建模和层次化联合优化算法，在动态无人机参与下实现了轨迹规划与资源分配的协同优化，显著提升了系统效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多无人机支持的分布式移动边缘计算场景中，无人机的能量状态和服务负载会随时间动态变化，导致协作无人机集合不断改变。现有研究未能有效解决这种动态变化下的轨迹规划与资源分配的联合优化问题，亟需一种能够适应动态环境、协调无人机与移动终端的分布式优化方案。

Method: 采用Stackelberg博弈理论对问题建模，将无人机和移动终端分别建模为领导者和跟随者。在多智能体深度强化学习框架下开发层次化联合优化算法：领导层采用MAPPO算法在集中训练-分散执行范式下联合优化无人机轨迹、任务迁移比例、关联关系和资源定价；跟随层使用两阶段迭代算法确定任务卸载比例和资源请求。无人机被建模为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程中的智能体进行训练。

Result: 仿真实验表明，所提算法在动态无人机参与下具有稳定收敛性。与无协作基准相比，通过无人机间任务迁移使无人机效率提升18.58%；与完全卸载方案相比，移动终端平均开销降低33.77%。在不同网络规模和能力条件下，通过联合优化无人机操作和资源利用率，算法性能均优于其他基准方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于Stackelberg博弈和多智能体深度强化学习的层次化联合优化算法，能够有效解决动态无人机参与下的分布式移动边缘计算问题，实现了无人机与移动终端的双赢，为实际应用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: This paper investigates a multiple uncrewed aerial vehicles (UAVs)-enabled distributed mobile edge computing (MEC) framework, where the set of collaborative UAVs dynamically varies over time due to their energy states and service loads. The joint optimization of trajectory planning and resource allocation is formulated as a Stackelberg game, where UAVs and mobile terminals (MTs) are modeled as leaders and followers, respectively. UAVs aim to maximize their benefits by balancing executed workload, energy cost, and resource allocation revenue, while MTs seek to minimize their total overhead, composed of computing delay and resource costs, through offloading and resource-request decisions. A hierarchical joint optimization algorithm is developed within a multi-agent deep reinforcement learning (MADRL) framework to coordinate UAVs and MTs in a distributed manner. At the leader level, UAVs jointly determine their trajectories, task migration ratios, MT-UAV association, and unit computing resource pricing. Each UAV is modeled as an agent in a partially observable Markov decision process, and the agents are jointly trained via multi-agent proximal policy optimization (MAPPO) under the centralized-training-and-decentralized-execution paradigm. At the follower level, MTs determine their optimal task offloading ratios and requested computing resources using a two-stage iterative algorithm. Simulation results demonstrate stable convergence under dynamic UAV participation. Compared to the no-collaboration benchmark, the proposed algorithm improves UAV efficiency by 18.58% through inter-UAV task migration and reduces average MT overhead by 33.77% over the fully offloading scheme. It also outperforms other benchmarks under varying network scales and capabilities by jointly optimizing UAV operations and resource utilization.

</details>


### [209] [5 Shades of Cooperation: Spectrum Sharing in the Upper-Mid Band](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23170)
*Alberto Ceresoli,Marco Mezzavilla,Ilario Filippini,Antonio Capone*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 通过利用大规模天线阵列的空间自由度进行跨运营商干扰抑制，可在共享频谱场景下实现比传统正交分配高2.5倍的用户速率增益，且仅需极少的运营商间元数据交换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统移动网络运营商的专用频谱稀缺且以固定正交块分配，在异构时变流量下导致频谱利用率低下。大规模天线阵列提供了新的可能性：运营商可以利用部分空间自由度服务自身用户，同时抑制对其他运营商用户的干扰，从而允许竞争网络复用相同频段。

Method: 将运营商间频谱共享建模为单一零点成形MU-MIMO预编码和干扰约束调度原语的连续协作"色度"谱系，涵盖正交分配、非协作复用、协作跨运营商保护，以及利用外系统调度决策的调度感知边界。研究在包含27个基站共享7GHz载波的真实多运营商部署的射线追踪数字孪生上进行评估。

Result: 协作方案相比非协作复用和正交分配，实现了超过2.5倍的中值每用户速率增益。关键发现是：大部分增益仅需极少的运营商间信息交换即可实现，无需紧密的联合处理。

Conclusion: 适度的、可标准化的元数据交换（而非紧密联合处理）就能释放共享频谱运营的大部分价值，这为实际部署提供了实用可行的路径，避免了复杂的跨运营商协作需求。

Abstract: Spectrum exclusively licensed to each mobile network operator (MNO) is scarce, and assigning it in fixed, frequency-orthogonal blocks leaves much of it idle under heterogeneous, time-varying traffic. Large antenna arrays offer an alternative: an operator can spend part of its spatial degrees of freedom (DoF) serving its own users and part suppressing interference toward others' users (nullforming), letting competing networks reuse the same band. This raises two questions: does trading DoF for interference suppression beat orthogonal partitioning, and how much coordination is needed to realize the gain? We cast inter-operator sharing as a continuum of cooperation "shades" of a single null-forming MU-MIMO precoding and interference-constrained scheduling primitive, spanning orthogonal partitioning, non-cooperative reuse, cooperative cross-operator protection, and a scheduling-aware bound exploiting foreign scheduling decisions. Evaluated on a ray-traced digital twin of a real multi-operator deployment with 27 base stations sharing a 7 GHz carrier, the cooperative shades deliver more than a 2.5$\times$ median per-user rate gain over both non-cooperative reuse and orthogonal splitting. Crucially, most of this gain requires only minimal inter-operator information exchange, indicating that a modest, standardizable metadata exchange, rather than tight joint processing, unlocks most of the value of shared-spectrum operation.

</details>


### [210] [NetConfArena: An Executable Benchmark for LLM Agents in Closed-Loop Network Configuration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23179)
*Chang Liu,Xiaohui Xie,Xinyi Chen,Yong Cui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出NetConfArena，一个用于评估大语言模型（LLM）代理在闭环网络配置中表现的基准测试平台，揭示了代理在任务规范遵循和鲁棒规划执行方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前针对网络配置的LLM代理评估基准存在缺陷，通常将配置视为静态命令生成或依赖过于简化的设置，忽略了协议复杂性和拓扑依赖性等核心挑战，无法真实反映代理在现实环境中的可靠性和失败模式。

Method: 构建NetConfArena基准平台，将代理置于模拟多设备网络环境中，提供标准化的紧凑动作接口，通过隐藏的任务特定可执行测试用例评估网络行为；采用LLM辅助、以模拟为基础的流水线，将面向人类的网络材料转换为可重用的参数化任务模板。

Result: 基于96个协议聚焦的任务模板实例化480个任务实例，评估代表性LLM代理，生成3840条执行轨迹；发现失败不仅限于命令错误，还揭示了代理在任务规范遵循和鲁棒规划执行方面的差距。
