Method: 提出了Read-Write-Relax (RWR)统一框架，将潜在注意力机制与消息传递松弛操作交替执行。从误差角度看，这两种操作构成了多重网格循环的两半：一个校正低频谱端的误差，另一个校正高频谱端的误差，通过交替执行实现全频谱误差校正。

Result: RWR成为几乎所有工业和公共基准测试比较中最准确的模型，在稀缺数据机制下具有显著的数据效率，在工程关键量上准确，并能将全场预测扩展到具有挑战性的大规模问题。

Conclusion: 通过统一全局和局部方法并利用多重网格理论指导，RWR成功突破了现有神经网格模拟方法在处理大规模工业问题时的根本局限，为工程应用提供了更准确、更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent mesh-based simulation advances have, in no small part, relied on neural surrogates of two distinct families: global models that route information through a small set of latent tokens, and local models that perform message passing across mesh edges. Consistent with both classes is the inability to perform beyond low-dimensional problems and small-scale or oversimplified meshes, the simulation regimes where industrial problems reside. Our work shows this explicitly and presents a unified formulation. In global approaches, latent-token attention acts as a spatial low-pass filter, while local message passing lacks the global reach necessary to propagate information across large mesh spaces. Viewed through the error, the two operators are the halves of a multigrid cycle: one corrects errors at the lower end of the spectrum, the other at the higher end, and neither can do the other's job. We introduce Read-Write-Relax (RWR), which interleaves latent attention with message-passing relaxation under a unified formulation. The interleaved processor lowers error across the entire spectrum, making RWR the most accurate model in nearly every comparison across our industrial and public benchmarks. It is also markedly data-efficient in the scarce-data regimes, accurate on the engineering quantities of interest, and scales full-field predictions to challenging, large-scale problems.

</details>


### [246] [Benchmarking Composable Compression Techniques in Mixture-of-Experts LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21693)
*Afsara Benazir,Chen Chen,Rongxiao Qu,Jiabo Huang,Jingtao Li,Lingjuan Lyu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了MoEXBench，首个系统评估MoE模型组合压缩技术（专家剪枝、权重量化、KV缓存压缩）交互影响的基准测试，揭示了单独评估无法预测组合效果、专家剪枝是主要性能下降源等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE LLMs在实际部署中面临专家参数量大、路由不平衡、长上下文KV缓存增长等挑战，通常需要组合多种压缩技术（专家剪枝、权重量化、KV缓存压缩），但现有研究孤立评估这些技术，缺乏对它们组合使用时交互影响的理解。

Method: 构建MoEXBench基准测试框架，涵盖10个MoE模型（30B-235B参数）、三种注意力架构（标准注意力、混合线性注意力、滑动窗口注意力），评估20%-50%专家剪枝率、1-16位权重量化、多种KV缓存精度设置，通过八模块评估套件联合测量压缩质量、鲁棒性、敏感性和部署效率。

Result: 发现压缩方法间存在非平凡交互：组合压缩效果无法从单独技术预测、压缩率不能可靠预测质量损失或运行时增益、专家剪枝是主要性能下降源、平均质量指标会掩盖特定工作负载和架构的失效问题。

Conclusion: MoEXBench通过发布标准化模块评分、压缩模型产物和可复现脚本，为MoE模型家族和硬件后端提供实用的精度-内存-延迟权衡比较，支持实际部署场景中的压缩策略优化。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) LLMs scale model capacity efficiently through sparse activation, but their large expert parameter footprint, routing imbalance, and long-context KV-cache growth make deployment difficult on commodity hardware. Practical deployment often requires stacking multiple compression techniques: expert pruning removes redundant experts, weight quantization lowers model memory footprint, and KV-cache compression reduces long-context memory pressure. However, these techniques are typically evaluated in isolation, leaving open how they interact when applied together in realistic deployment pipelines.
  In this work, we present MoEXBench, a systematic benchmark for evaluating composable MoE compression as an end-to-end deployment workflow. MoEXBench studies 10 MoE models ranging from 30B to 235B total parameters across standard-attention, hybrid linear-attention, and sliding window attention architectures. It evaluates 20%-50% expert pruning rates, 1 to 16 bit weight-quantization schemes, and multiple KV-cache precision settings, applied both individually and in combination.
  MoEXBench introduces an eight-module evaluation suite that jointly measures composable-compression quality, workload and architecture robustness, pruning/quantization/KV cache sensitivity, and deployment efficiency on commodity hardware. Our results reveal non-trivial interactions among compression methods: composable compression cannot be predicted from standalone techniques, compression rate alone does not reliably predict quality loss or runtime gain, expert pruning is the dominant degradation source, and average quality can hide workload and architecture-specific failures. By releasing normalized module scores, compressed artifacts, and reproducible scripts, MoEXBench enables practical accuracy-memory-latency comparison across MoE families and hardware backends.

</details>


### [247] [Posterior Information Dynamics of Diffusion Models for Linear Inverse Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21709)
*Xiangming Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散模型在线性逆问题中测量信息如何进入反向去噪过程，引入平滑似然力概念来量化信息增益，发现了其与去噪误差减少的I-MMSE恒等式关系，并揭示了算子-先验对齐对信息同化的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然被广泛用作线性逆问题的先验，但现有研究仅关注端点质量，无法揭示测量信息何时进入反向去噪过程以及如何在信号方向上分配，缺乏对信息注入动态的理解。

Method: 通过定义平滑似然力（精确后验与先验得分在各噪声水平的差值）来分析信息流动；推导其期望平方范数与后验-先验相对熵耗散的关系；建立信息增益与去噪误差减少的I-MMSE恒等式；在高噪声下分析力的能量衰减规律；在可解模型和FFHQ模型上进行实验验证。

Result: 发现平滑似然力的期望平方范数同时给出后验-先验相对熵耗散和反向路径相对熵增长；建立了信息增益与MMSE的恒等式关系；证明在高噪声下力能量按噪声核信号系数的二次方衰减；发现条件化会移除测量已解释的类别分离，将经验样本的均匀索引熵从log n降至H(I|r)；信息同化依赖于算子-先验对齐而非仅奇异值；相同谱的掩码产生不同的先验归一化零空间轨迹统计。

Conclusion: 测量信息在反向去噪过程中的分配具有复杂的动态特征，信息增益与去噪误差紧密耦合，算子-先验对齐是影响信息同化的关键因素，这些发现为理解和改进扩散模型在逆问题中的应用提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Diffusion models are widely used as priors for linear inverse problems, yet endpoint quality does not reveal when measurement information enters reverse denoising or how it is allocated across signal directions. We study this process through the smoothed likelihood force, the difference between exact posterior and prior scores at each noise level. For a fixed measurement, its expected squared norm gives both posterior--prior relative-entropy dissipation and reverse-path relative-entropy growth. Averaging over measurements yields an information--minimum mean-square error (I-MMSE) identity linking information gain to denoising-error reduction. Under finite second moments, the force energy and its ratio to prior-score energy decay quadratically in the noising kernel's signal coefficient at high noise. Solvable models show that conditioning removes class separation already explained by the measurement, reduces a uniform index entropy over \(n\) empirical samples from \(\log n\) to \(H(I\mid r)\), and makes assimilation depend on operator--prior alignment even for identical singular values. Experiments in models with tractable posteriors evaluate these predictions. In a separate illustration with a frozen FFHQ model, masks sharing the same spectrum yield different prior-normalized null-space trajectory statistics.

</details>


### [248] [Reinforcement Learning on Benign Facts Amplifies Leakage of Memorized Private Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21727)
*Renfei Zhang,Niloofar Mireshghallah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在良性事实数据上进行强化学习会显著增加模型泄露已记忆个人身份信息(PII)的风险，这种攻击不需要接触隐私数据本身。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究强化学习与可验证奖励(RLVR)在提升模型推理能力的同时，是否会产生意外的隐私泄露副作用，特别是对已记忆但潜伏的个人信息的影响。

Method: 1. 确认指令模型已记忆但潜伏的PII；2. 在不含任何PII的良性事实数据上应用强化学习训练；3. 使用针对性探测(name→email对)和非针对性自由回忆提示测试PII提取率的变化。

Result: PII提取率显著上升：在DeepSeek-V3.1上，verbatim recall@k从0.155升至0.370(增长2.4倍)；效应随模型规模增加而增强；模型的推理能力和拒绝率保持不变，表明RL选择性改变了记忆信息的可访问性而非广泛改变模型。

Conclusion: 已记忆的隐私数据可通过从未接触该数据的训练变得更容易被提取，这为攻击者提供了一条无需隐私相关训练信号、仅通过良性微调即可访问记忆数据的攻击路径。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) is deployed to make models better at reasoning tasks, but its side effect on what models will divulge is under studied. Here we show that RLVR on facts increases extraction of personally identifiable information (PII) the instruct model had already memorized. We first confirm that instruct models have already memorized PII but leave them latent, rarely surfacing one when asked. We then apply RL on benign factual data that contains no PII of any kind, and re-probe: a targeted probe over name->email pairs, and an untargeted free-recall prompt that simply asks the model to list the addresses it knows. PII extraction rises sharply under both: on DeepSeek-V3.1, verbatim recall@k increases from 0.155 to 0.370, a 2.4x gain. The effect scales with model size: across three models spanning 8B to 671B parameters, absolute leakage is largest in the biggest model. Meanwhile model's reasoning abilities and refusal rates are retained, indicating that RL selectively changes which memorized information is accessible rather than broadly altering the model. In summary, memorized private data can be made markedly more extractable by training that never touches it. This gives an adversary a route to memorized data that requires no privacy-relevant training signal and no access to the data itself -- only the ability to fine-tune on something innocuous.

</details>


### [249] [Adaptive Multilevel Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo for Rare Events Estimation in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21736)
*Zixuan Liu,Fangzheng Wu,Brian Summa,Zizhan Zheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出自适应多层Twisted SMC方法，通过渐进式中间事件学习解决大语言模型稀有安全风险的概率估计难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型中的稀有不安全行为虽然发生概率极低，但在大规模部署场景下仍具实际危害性。传统Twisted SMC方法依赖稀有事件的正样本进行扭曲函数学习，但在学习初期正样本稀缺导致估计不可靠。

Method: 提出自适应多层Twisted SMC算法，通过构建一系列逐渐稀疏的中间事件序列进行分层学习。每个层级的扭曲函数为下一层级提供更丰富的正样本，从而逐步逼近目标稀有事件，实现更精确的扭曲函数估计。

Result: 在多种任务和模型规模上的实验表明，该方法相比传统方法能产生更准确的稀有事件概率估计，有效解决了正样本匮乏条件下的学习困难。

Conclusion: 该方法为发现难以观测的不安全行为提供了可靠工具，可增强部署环境下大语言模型的安全评估与对齐能力。

Abstract: Rare unsafe behaviors in large language models can remain practically significant even when their probability is extremely small, particularly at deployment scales involving millions or billions of interactions. Twisted Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) provides a principled framework for rare-event probability estimation by learning twist functions that guide generation toward a target event. However, the standard twist learning framework relies on positive samples from the rare-event target distribution, which may be nearly absent before an informative twist has been learned, resulting in unreliable rare-event estimation. We propose Adaptive Multilevel Twisted SMC, which learns the rare-event twist through a sequence of progressively rarer intermediate events. At each level, the learned twist provides more informative positive examples for learning the next twist, ultimately leading to a more accurate final twist for the target rare event. Experiments across diverse tasks and model scales show that the proposed method produces more accurate rare-event probability estimates. By enabling more reliable discovery of hard-to-observe unsafe behaviors, our method provides a practical tool for strengthening the evaluation and safety alignment of deployed language models.

</details>


### [250] [Width-Independent Compressibility of Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21752)
*Hong-Yi Wang,Mingze Wang,Liu Ziyin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了深度多层感知器的统一压缩定理：对于任意深层宽网络，存在深度相同但宽度仅为O((log(1/ε))^{d_in})的窄网络能近似表示原函数，压缩后宽度与原始宽度无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络可以大幅压缩而不影响性能这一现象早已被观察到，但其理论基础尚未被充分理解。作者旨在为这一重要现象提供理论解释，建立深度网络的压缩性定理。

Method: 提出了针对具有解析激活函数的深度多层感知器的压缩理论。采用了一种新颖的导数匹配技术，该技术能够感知低维输入，并通过逐层重加权来保持输入-输出映射。理论构造了一个与原始网络深度相同但更窄的网络。

Result: 证明了压缩后的网络宽度与原始网络宽度惊人地无关，而仅取决于误差预算ε和有效输入维度d_in，达到O((log(1/ε))^{d_in})的量级。该结果表明网络压缩能力主要受输入维度和所需精度影响，而非网络规模。

Conclusion: 该工作为深度神经网络的可压缩性提供了理论保障，揭示了压缩宽度与输入维度的对数依赖关系，为理解神经网络过度参数化现象提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: It has long been known that well-trained neural networks can be compressed very strongly without affecting their performance, an important phenomenon that remains poorly understood. We prove a uniform compressibility theorem for deep multilayer perceptrons with analytic activations. For a deep, wide fixed teacher network, there exists a narrow (same depth) network that approximately represents the same function as the original. The reachable compressed width is strikingly independent of the original width, but is $O((\log(1/\varepsilon))^{d_{in}})$, where $\varepsilon$ is the error budget and $d_{in}$ is the effective input dimension. Our construction involves a novel derivative-matching technique which is aware of the low-dimensional input, and a layer-wise reweighting that preserves the input-output mapping.

</details>


### [251] [How Architecture and Training Affect TPC Representations Across Experiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21756)
*Tyler Wheeler,Michelle P. Kuchera,Raghuram Ramanujan,William Sieland,Ryan Krupp,Daniel Bazin,Connor L. Cross,Hoi Yan Ian Heung,Andrew J. Jones,Ruchi Mahajan,Saiprasad Ravishankar,Pranjal Singh,Benjamin Votaw,Chris Wrede*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了TPC探测器数据表示的跨实验可重用性，发现网络架构本身就能产生任务相关的结构，PointNet架构在随机初始化状态下就具有高度信息性，架构是嵌入空间中任务相关结构的主要来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习研究日益转向基础模型方法，在实验物理学中，这允许模型和学到的表示在原始实验之外被重用。然而，下游性能无法单独区分架构贡献和编码器训练贡献，需要明确评估架构在表示学习中的作用。

Method: 使用GADGET II TPC和AT-TPC四个数据集，采用Sparse ResNet和PointNet风格编码器生成512维嵌入。通过随机初始化编码器分离架构贡献；训练编码器后冻结参数，对每个下游任务训练线性或非线性探针，评估跨实验和跨探测器的表示可重用性。

Result: 架构诱导的结构在跨实验和跨探测器时保持有用性；随机初始化的PointNet表示在多个任务上高度信息性；两种架构组织嵌入空间的方式不同，但均未表现出跨探测器的大量系统性效用损失；架构是TPC嵌入中任务相关结构的主要来源。

Conclusion: 架构应作为表示学习评估和可重用探测器模型开发中的显式因素进行考虑，这为实验物理学中基础模型方法的应用提供了重要见解。

Abstract: Deep-learning efforts have increasingly shifted toward foundation model approaches. In experimental physics, this allows models and learned representations to be reused beyond the experiments in which they were developed. This work evaluates the reusability of representations across experiments and detector systems using probes on frozen encoders. These probes reveal task-relevant structure before downstream adaptation, complementing fine-tuning. Together with random-weight controls, they distinguish contributions from architecture and encoder training that downstream performance alone cannot resolve.
  Time projection chamber (TPC) data provide a useful testbed because events from TPC systems can be represented as variable-length sparse tensors, while detector geometries, event topologies, and scientific tasks can differ substantially. We investigate whether fixed-dimensional TPC event representations can be reused across classification tasks, experiments, and detector systems. Sparse ResNet and PointNet-style encoders produce 512-dimensional embeddings for four datasets from the GADGET II TPC and AT-TPC. Randomly initialized encoders isolate the contribution from architecture before supervised training. We then train each encoder on a classification task, freeze its parameters, and train a linear or nonlinear probe for each downstream task. We find that this architecture-induced structure remains useful across experiments and detector systems. The randomly initialized PointNet-style representation is highly informative on several tasks. The two architectures organize their embedding spaces differently, but neither exhibits a large, systematic loss of utility cross-detector. These results show that architecture is a major source of task-relevant structure in TPC embeddings and should be treated explicitly when assessing representation learning and developing reusable detector models.

</details>


### [252] [A Fixed-Radius Distance-Band Benchmark for Dimensionality-Reduction Fidelity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21779)
*Yoshio Takaeda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的降维评估指标——固定半径距离带Shepard rho，克服了传统recall@k等指标偏向近邻图方法（如t-SNE、UMAP）的偏差，能够更公平地评估不同类型降维方法对局部和全局结构的保持能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有降维评估指标（如recall@k、可信度、连续性）存在内在偏差：其逐点变化的半径和硬包含阈值天然偏向近邻图方法（t-SNE、UMAP），而不公平地惩罚那些保持绝对距离的方法。这种偏差导致评估结果无法真实反映各种降维方法的优劣，需要一种更公平、更全面的评估框架。

Method: 提出固定半径距离带Shepard rho指标：计算高维和2维空间中成对距离之间的Spearman相关系数，限制在累积距离带内以分别报告近邻和全局结构。该方法使用固定绝对半径评估每个点，并引入成员限制的Shepard rho来解决单点和少数群体问题。在包含已知几何结构的合成数据集（非均匀密度、密集聚类、闭环过渡、离群值、不平衡双群体数据）上，对PCA、Isomap、t-SNE、UMAP、PyMDE、PCC、DREAMS和toorPIA八种方法进行全面基准测试，并通过样本外测试验证异常检测能力。

Result: 实验揭示了三个关键发现：（i）高全局Shepard rho可以与约93倍的类内尺度坍缩共存，这种坍缩在基于排名的指标中不可见，但在基于数值的过度压缩指标中显而易见；（ii）recall@k和固定半径带指标系统性地不一致，不一致方向与偏差预测一致，验证了理论分析；（iii）成员限制的Shepard rho能够解决多点统计无法处理的单点和少数群体问题，而即使是DREAMS这类最新的局部-全局混合方法在这些方面也会静默失败。

Conclusion: 固定半径距离带Shepard rho提供了一种比传统recall@k族指标更公平、更全面的降维评估方法，能够揭示传统指标掩盖的问题（如尺度坍缩），公平对待不同设计理念的降维方法，并能解决单点和少数群体评估等传统统计量无法处理的问题。该评估框架独立于方法内部实现，所有指标可精确计算且结果可离线复现，为降维方法的选择和比较提供了更可靠的标准。

Abstract: Dimensionality-reduction (DR) methods are routinely judged by how well each point's k nearest neighbors survive the 2-D embedding (recall@k, trustworthiness, continuity). We argue this family is a biased measure of distance fidelity: its per-point variable radius and hard inclusion threshold favor neighbor-graph methods (t-SNE, UMAP) and penalize methods that preserve absolute distances. We instead score DR fidelity with a fixed-radius distance-band Shepard rho: the Spearman correlation between high-D and 2-D pairwise distances, restricted to cumulative distance bands so that near and global structure are reported separately, with every point judged on the same absolute radius. On synthetic datasets with known ground-truth geometry (non-uniform density, dense clusters, a closed-loop transition, off-subspace outliers, imbalanced two-population data) at realistic noise (SNR=1, D=768, N=1000), we benchmark eight methods -- PCA, Isomap, t-SNE, UMAP, PyMDE, PCC, DREAMS, and the closed-source toorPIA -- and show that (i) high global Shepard rho can coexist with a ~93x collapse of within-cluster scale, invisible to rank-based scores but obvious in a value-based over-compression metric; (ii) recall@k and the fixed-radius band disagree systematically, in the direction the bias predicts; (iii) a membership-restricted Shepard rho resolves single-point and minority-population questions that many-pair statistics cannot -- questions on which even DREAMS, a recent local-plus-global hybrid, fails silently. A supplementary out-of-sample (addplot) test asks whether a never-seen anomaly lands outside the normal region and whether its direction identifies its source. All metrics are computed exactly on all pairwise distances, independently of any method's internals, and every number is reproducible offline: the closed-source method's output coordinates (not its algorithm) are committed to the artifact.

</details>


### [253] [MSM-Mem: A Universal Medical Structured Multimodal Memory Framework for Medical AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21810)
*Md Asaduzzaman Jabin,Khoa Le,Lin Zhao,Tianming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MSM-Mem框架，使医学AI代理能够像临床医生一样通过积累临床经验来持续演进和提升决策能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床决策本质上是经验驱动的，医生通过综合患者病史、多模态观察和既往诊断经验来逐步完善推理；而当前基于MLLM的医疗AI代理主要作为无状态推理系统运行，每次交互独立生成决策，无法保留或内化经验知识，限制了其通过使用逐步提高推理可靠性和适应真实临床工作流程中纵向患者语境的能力。

Method: 提出医学结构化多模态记忆（MSM-Mem）框架，将异构临床经验组织为语义记忆、情景记忆和视觉记忆，并在推理过程中增量更新这些记忆，使代理能够检索先前的经验来指导当前推理，并随时间推移逐步完善决策制定。

Result: 在MoE-LLaVA骨干网络上的评估显示，性能持续提升，并且通过持续使用可以观察到进一步的性能增益。

Conclusion: MSM-Mem为医学AI代理提供了一条可行的路径，使其能够以类似于临床医生从实践中学习的方式演进其推理能力。

Abstract: Clinical decision-making is inherently experience-driven: physicians progressively refine their reasoning by synthesizing patient history, multimodal observations, and prior diagnostic experiences across interactions. In contrast, current multimodal large language model (MLLM)-based medical AI agents largely operate as stateless inference systems, generating decisions independently for each interaction without retaining or internalizing experiential knowledge. This discrepancy limits their ability to progressively improve reasoning reliability through usage and adapt to longitudinal patient contexts in real-world clinical workflows. In this study, we propose Medical Structured Multimodal Memory (MSM-Mem), an agentic memory framework that enables medical AI agents to evolve through accumulated clinical experiences. MSM-Mem organizes heterogeneous clinical experiences into semantic, episodic, and visual memory and incrementally updates them during inference, allowing the agent to retrieve prior experiences to inform current reasoning and progressively refine decision-making over time. Evaluations on MoE-LLaVA backbones demonstrate consistent performance improve- ments with further gains observed through continued usage. In general, MSM-Mem offers a viable pathway toward medical AI agents capable of evolving their reasoning competence in a manner analogous to the way clinicians learn from practice over time.

</details>


### [254] [Resilient Concurrent Causal Discovery for Topological Event Sequences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21815)
*Jiyu Tian,Junhao Dong,Mingchu Li,Lingling Fang,Liming Chen,Andreas Holzinger,Zheng Yan,Yew Soon Ong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RCCD方法，通过影响感知超边因果注意力和基于掩码的交替因果优化框架，实现了从拓扑事件序列中鲁棒地学习因果图，显著提升了并发事件因果发现的准确性和对缺失数据的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以捕捉并发事件产生的复杂因果关系，且对不完整事件序列缺乏鲁棒性，无法满足网络可靠性保障的需求。

Method: 1. 提出影响感知超边因果注意力机制，将事件持续时间融入嵌入表示，通过超边因果卷积聚合并发事件特征，并注入网络先验知识以捕捉多对一因果交互；2. 设计基于掩码的交替因果优化框架，通过自监督掩码重建强制模型基于上下文恢复被掩码的事件类型，增强预测器对缺失数据的韧性。

Result: 在模拟和真实电信网络数据集上的广泛实验表明，该方法在准确性和鲁棒性方面均显著优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RCCD方法能够有效处理拓扑事件序列中的并发因果关系和不完整数据问题，更适用于真实电信网络环境，为网络可靠性保障提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Causal discovery on topological event sequences is crucial for ensuring the reliability of networks. However, existing methods struggle to capture the complex causal relationships arising from concurrent events and lack robustness to incomplete event sequences. To address these issues, we propose a resilient concurrent causal discovery method, termed RCCD, enabling robust learning of causal graphs from topological event sequences. Specifically, we first introduce an influence-aware hyperedge causal attention mechanism, which incorporates event duration into the embedding representation, aggregates concurrent event features via hyperedge causal convolution, and injects network prior knowledge to capture the complex many-to-one causal interactions. Furthermore, we design a masked-based alternating causal optimization framework, which forces the model to recover masked event types based on context through self-supervised mask reconstruction, thereby enhancing the resilience of the predictor to missing data. To validate the effectiveness of our method, we conduct extensive experiments on both simulated and real-world telecommunication network datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed method significantly outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods in both accuracy and robustness, making it more suitable for real-world telecommunication network environments.

</details>


### [255] [A Physics-informed Neural Network Approach for Robust Buckling Load Prediction and Reliability-Based Design of Thin Truncated Conical Shells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21818)
*Devasmit Dutta,Budhaditya De,Rohan Majumder,Sudip Kumar Mishra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究提出了物理信息神经网络(PiNN)与可靠性设计(RBD)相结合的框架，用于预测薄壁截锥壳的临界屈曲载荷并标定安全一致的折减因子(KDF)，相比传统深度神经网络提高了预测精度和物理一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 薄壁截锥壳在航空航天等领域广泛应用，但其轴向压缩屈曲性能对几何缺陷、制造公差和材料变异性高度敏感。现有设计方法采用保守的折减因子(如NASA SP-8019)，未显式考虑壳体几何、制造质量、数据不确定性和目标可靠性，导致设计不够精准。

Method: 开发物理信息神经网络(PiNN)框架，结合几何/材料描述符与壳体稳定性理论、局部折减刚度法(LRSM)导出的力学特征；引入物理信息损失函数惩罚超过理论弹性屈曲载荷的预测；使用133个Mylar截锥壳轴向压缩实验数据进行训练验证；将训练好的代理模型集成到可靠性设计(RBD)框架中。

Result: 相比传统深度神经网络(DNN)，PiNN提高了预测精度，降低了平均绝对误差，增强了物理一致性；成功评估了可靠性指标并为指定目标可靠性水平标定了安全一致的折减因子(KDF)。

Conclusion: PiNN-RBD框架为缺陷敏感壳体结构的不确定性感知设计提供了高效方法，实现了更精确的屈曲载荷预测和可靠性校准。

Abstract: Thin-walled truncated conical shells are widely used in aerospace, marine, offshore, and lightweight infrastructure systems due to their high strength-to-weight ratio and geometric efficiency. Their buckling resistance under axial compression, however, is highly sensitive to geometric imperfections, manufacturing tolerances, material variability, and nonlinear instability effects. Conventional design procedures rely on conservative knockdown factors (KDFs), such as those recommended in NASA SP-8019, which do not explicitly account for shell geometry, fabrication quality, data uncertainty, or target reliability. This study develops a physics-informed neural network (PiNN) framework for predicting critical buckling loads of thin truncated conical shells and integrates the trained surrogate within a reliability-based design (RBD) formulation. The model combines geometric and material descriptors with mechanics-informed features derived from shell stability theory and the localized reduced stiffness method (LRSM). A physics-informed loss function penalizes mechanically inadmissible predictions exceeding the theoretical elastic buckling load. The framework is trained and evaluated using 133 experimental Mylar conical shell tests under axial compression. Compared with a conventional deep neural network (DNN), the PiNN improves predictive accuracy, reduces mean absolute error, and enhances physical consistency. The trained PiNN is then used to evaluate reliability indices and calibrate safety-consistent KDFs for prescribed target reliability levels. Results demonstrate that the PiNN-RBD framework provides an efficient approach for uncertainty-aware design of imperfection-sensitive shell structures.

</details>


### [256] [More Experts, Worse Dynamics: Inverse Scaling and Spectral Bias in Mixture-of-Experts State-Space Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21840)
*Chandresh Pandey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在谱状态空间模型中，专家混合（MoE）架构在算子层面的混合未能超越单专家基线，即使在完美监督下也会导致性能退化，揭示了算子插值方法的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设混合稳定算子能够适应异构或 regime-switching 时间序列，本文旨在批判性评估这一假设的有效性，聚焦于动力学挑战而非表示能力挑战。

Method: 在受控合成环境中研究三regime序列的下一步预测任务：Mackey-Glass混沌系统、稳定振荡regime和噪声主导自回归regime。进行了广泛的消融实验，包括容量缩放、oracle路由、冻结专家变体以及与输出级MoE基线的比较。

Result: 算子级混合模型持续未能超越单专家基线。增加专家数量导致逆缩放现象，路由崩溃或无法诱导有意义的特化，即使完美regime监督也无法阻止全局性能退化。相空间分析表明混沌轨迹上MSE的表观改善往往来自破坏吸引子几何结构的时间平滑，而非忠实建模动力学。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了在所研究参数化和训练协议下算子插值的潜在局限性，强调了在评估regime-switching动力系统时需要几何感知的评估方法，而非仅依赖均方误差等传统指标。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures are commonly motivated as a way to increase expressivity by decomposing complex systems into simpler local dynamics. This intuition has recently been extended to spectral state-space models, where mixing stable operators is assumed to enable adaptation to heterogeneous or regime-switching time series. We critically evaluate this assumption in a controlled synthetic setting designed to isolate dynamical rather than representational challenges. We study a next-step prediction task on sequences composed of three regimes: chaotic dynamics generated by the Mackey-Glass system, a stable oscillatory regime, and a noise-dominated autoregressive regime. Across extensive ablations including capacity scaling, oracle routing, frozen-expert variants, and comparisons to output-level MoE baselines, operator-level mixture models consistently fail to outperform a single-expert baseline. Increasing the number of experts leads to inverse scaling, routing collapses or fails to induce meaningful specialization, and even perfect regime supervision does not prevent degradation in global performance. Furthermore, we show that apparent improvements in mean squared error on chaotic trajectories can be misleading. Phase-space analysis reveals that lower error often arises from temporal smoothing that destroys the geometry of the underlying attractor rather than from faithful modeling of the dynamics. These results identify a likely limitation of operator interpolation under the studied parameterization and training protocol, and underscore the need for geometry-aware evaluation when assessing regime-switching dynamical systems.

</details>


### [257] [ChainPrune: Evaluating and Reducing Redundancy in Long Chain-of-Thought Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21860)
*Weihang Pan,Zhengxu Yu,Yuxiang Zhang,Wenzhi Li,Zhongming Jin,Binbin Lin,Xiaofei He,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ChainPrune方法，通过树结构整合和多准则路径选择优化推理路径，结合DPO偏好学习，有效减少大型推理模型的过度思考问题，降低计算开销同时保持准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大型推理模型存在过度思考行为，包括推理步骤过长、冗余步骤多、计算开销大。现有的token长度奖励策略虽然能减少token数量，但往往产生伪简洁性，冗余推理仍然存在，推理链结构效率低下。

Method: 提出ChainPrune方法：(1)将自生成的推理路径整合为树状结构；(2)通过多准则主导路径选择构建偏好数据，生成浅层推理轨迹同时保留关键推理步骤；(3)结合DPO偏好学习方法与监督损失，缓解虚假奖励抑制问题。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法显著减少了推理步骤长度和计算开销，同时能够保持甚至提升模型准确性。

Conclusion: ChainPrune通过推理路径的语义结构优化，实现了推理效率与效果的双重提升，为大型推理模型的推理优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning has significantly enhanced the multi-step problem-solving capabilities of large language models (LLMs) by introducing explicit intermediate reasoning. However, advanced Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) often exhibit overthinking behaviors, including excessively long reasoning steps, redundant steps, and high computational overhead. Existing token-length reward strategies aim to promote concise outputs, but often result in pseudo-conciseness, where token count is reduced, yet redundant reasoning persists, leading to longer and less structurally efficient chains. To address these limitations, we propose ChainPrune, a novel reasoning path semantic structural optimization method to efficiently and controllably synthesize self-generated high-quality training data. We initially consolidate self-generated reasoning paths into a tree-based structure, followed by a multi-criteria dominant path selection process for preference data construction that formulates shallow reasoning trajectories while preserving essential reasoning steps. To further enhance the quality of reasoning, we incorporate a DPO-based preference learning method combined with supervised loss, effectively mitigating false reward suppression. This innovative integration significantly enhances both the efficiency and effectiveness of our reasoning framework. Comprehensive experimental results demonstrate significant reductions in step length and computational overhead, while maintaining or even enhancing accuracy.

</details>


### [258] [BioMed-Agent-RL: A Meta Learning, All You Need for Biomedical Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21864)
*Md Asaduzzaman Jabin,Zihao Wu,Tianming Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BioMed-Agent-RL统一医学智能体框架，通过自适应编排和强化学习解决临床视觉大语言模型的病灶噪声、模态错位和幻觉等问题，在多基准测试中准确率达73%，超越GPT-5等现有模型约5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床视觉大语言模型在复杂病例中存在病灶噪声干扰、多模态对齐不准、幻觉生成和上下文定位缺失等问题；同时现有智能体系统依赖静态固定流程，缺乏复杂医学推理所需的灵活性。

Method: 构建统一医学智能体BioMed-Agent-RL，集成自适应编排、策略和奖励机制；采用临床上下文感知偏好优化(CPO)、直接偏好优化(DPO)和群体相对策略优化(GRPO)配合动态熵调节；通过多模态元学习方法，自适应调用临床定位推理器、病灶分割器和领域综合器等专家模块；迭代式强化学习训练使智能体学会合成冲突视觉线索并依赖内在推理。

Result: 在多基准测试上进行消融研究，该方法显著优于GPT-5等现有最先进模型，准确率提升约5%，达到约73%。

Conclusion: 该框架为构建真实可靠、鲁棒且具备专家水平的独立临床推理智能体系统确立了新标准，为生物医学应用提供了更可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: The current progress of Clinical Vision Large Language Models (C-VLLMs) has substantially improved digital diagnostics, still these frameworks often endure lesion noises, modality misalignment, hallucination, and missed contextual grounding in complex clinical cases. Moreover, prevailing agent systems usually depend on static and non-adaptable pipelines and lack the versatility necessary for complex medical reasoning. To resolve these difficulties, we present BioMed-Agent-RL, a unified medical agent that incorporates adaptive orchestration, policy, and reward-based reinforcement learning (RL) models for biomedical applications. To ensure reliability, it invokes clinical context-aware preference optimization (CPO), direct preference optimization (DPO), and group relative policy optimization (GRPO) with dynamic entropy regulation. This pipeline utilizes a multimodal meta-learning approach that operates as a field-specific expert and human judgment synthesizer. The agent adaptively utilizes a set of model-level expertise, such as clinical grounding and reasoner, lesion segmenter, and field-specific synthesizer, across various clinical modalities (e.g., X-ray) by utilizing an iterative and adaptive RL approach. The agent learns to seriously synthesize misleading, conflicting vision cues and trust in inherent reasoning, while specialist advice is faulty. An intensive ablation study is conducted across multiple benchmarks, and the agent significantly outperforms existing state of the art models, such as GPT-5, attaining up to ~73% accuracy (gain of ~5%) over contemporary baselines. As a result, the framework suggests a new standard for building factual, reliable, robust, and expert-like intelligent agent systems for independent clinical reasoning.

</details>


### [259] [CD-LoRA: Consistency-Driven Low-Rank Adaptation for Multi-Task Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21909)
*Qian Zha,Jinda Liu,Yuan Wu,Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CD-LoRA，一种无需路由器的多任务学习方法，通过一致性驱动对齐机制在共享低秩空间中强制任务间表示一致性，解决了现有路由机制在训练与推理阶段的不一致性问题，在多任务PEFT中表现优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于LoRA的多任务学习方法依赖复杂的路由机制来划分任务特定知识，但这种设计存在训练-推理不一致性问题：分布偏移下的随机路由决策会损害推理稳定性。通过二阶泰勒分析揭示了路由方差导致的不稳定性，激励了本文的研究。

Method: 提出Consistency-Driven Low-Rank Adaptation (CD-LoRA)方法，完全消除路由器设计，采用一致性驱动的对齐机制，在共享低秩空间中强制跨任务的表示一致性，从而在不进行显式划分的情况下培养稳健的任务无关特征。

Result: 广泛实验表明CD-LoRA持续优于最先进的多适配器基线方法，提供了更简单、无路由、更稳定的多任务PEFT解决方案。

Conclusion: CD-LoRA成功解决了现有路由方法中训练-推理不一致性及随机路由决策导致的推理不稳定性问题，为多任务PEFT提供了一个简单、稳定、高性能的替代方案。

Abstract: While Multi-Task Learning (MTL) is essential for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) to diverse domains, prevailing LoRA-based methods rely on complex routing mechanisms that partition task-specific knowledge. In this work, we reveal that such routing-based designs are prone to a training-inference discrepancy, where stochastic routing decisions under distribution shifts compromise inference stability. Driven by a second-order Taylor analysis that exposes the instability induced by routing variance, we challenge the training-inference discrepancy and propose Consistency-Driven Low-Rank Adaptation (CD-LoRA). By eliminating routers entirely, CD-LoRA employs a consistency-driven alignment mechanism to enforce representation congruence across tasks in a shared low-rank space. This paradigm fosters robust, task-agnostic features without explicit partitioning overhead. Extensive experiments show that CD-LoRA consistently outperforms state-of-the-art multi-adapter baselines, offering a simpler, router-free, and more stable solution for multi-task PEFT. The code is available at the anonymous link https://github.com/zhaqian21/CD-LoRA.

</details>


### [260] [Beyond Fixed Directions: Adaptive Representation Analysis of Reasoning and Memorization in LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21919)
*Shaheen Nabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现语言模型中推理与记忆任务虽然可以通过单一方向完美区分，但在GRPO训练后该几何表示方向会发生显著重组，信息保持但载体改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证近期提出的假设：语言模型中推理和记忆可以通过单一表示方向表征，且该方向在强化学习过程中保持稳定。研究旨在测试两个关键假设：1）推理导向型任务与事实回忆型任务组是否近似单方向可分；2）经过GRPO训练后，相应的几何结构是否保持稳定。

Method: 使用Qwen3-0.6B模型和受控的400个样本数据集，通过一维投影与完整的1024维线性探针进行对比分析，采用AUROC指标评估性能。通过余弦相似度和表示漂移测量来量化GRPO前后的几何变化。

Result: GRPO前，一维投影能匹配完整1024维线性探针，AUROC达到1.00，证明单方向可分性。GRPO后，平均方向余弦值为0.453，探针方向余弦为0.445，最终层的直接表示漂移达0.511，但探针AUROC仍保持1.00。

Conclusion: 证据支持所研究任务组的单方向可解码性假设，但挑战了固定方向稳定性假设：任务相关信息在GRPO后持续存在，但其几何实现方式发生改变，表明模型的内部表示具有动态重组特性。

Abstract: Recent work has proposed that reasoning and memorization in language models can be characterized by a single representation direction, including methods that keep this direction fixed during reinforcement learning. We test two assumptions behind this view. First, are reasoning-oriented and factual-recall task groups approximately single-direction separable? Second, does the resulting geometry remain stable after GRPO? Using Qwen3-0.6B and a controlled 400-example dataset, we find that a one-dimensional projection can match a full 1024-dimensional linear probe with AUROC = 1.00 on the studied task groups. However, after GRPO, the corresponding direction is substantially reorganized: mean-direction cosine averages 0.453, probe-direction cosine 0.445, while direct representation drift reaches 0.511 at the final layer. Probe AUROC nevertheless remains 1.00. The evidence therefore supports single-direction decodability for the studied task groups but challenges fixed-direction stability: the information persists while its geometric realization changes.

</details>


### [261] [Bi-EZP: LLM-Guided Bilevel Program Evolution for Ensemble Zero-Cost Proxy Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21927)
*Yutao Lai,Kezhao Lai,Hai-Lin Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Bi-EZP的双层框架，利用大语言模型生成聚合程序，并使用CMA-ES优化参数，实现了自动化集成零成本代理的构建。该方法通过将程序发现与数值校准解耦，有效提升了神经架构搜索中的候选网络排序性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零成本代理（zero-cost proxies）能够从初始化时计算的统计量中评估候选网络，从而避免重复训练。然而，不同代理捕捉不同特性，往往在不同搜索空间产生不一致的排序。集成代理可以结合互补信号，但自动化发现需要优化离散聚合结构和连续系数，导致结构质量与参数校准难以分离。

Method: 提出Bi-EZP双层框架：上层使用大语言模型生成可执行的聚合程序（基于四个互补的基础代理，带程序特定参数边界）；下层使用协方差矩阵自适应进化策略（CMA-ES）在内部训练集上优化每个固定程序的连续参数。通过Kendall排序相关性在独立验证集上评估校准后的程序，使进化选择能够推广到校准数据之外的结构。

Result: 在NATS-Bench和Network Design Spaces上的实验评估了跨异构搜索空间的排序性能，DARTS实验评估了下游架构搜索效果。结果表明，将程序发现与数值校准分离为自动化集成零成本代理构建提供了有效方法。

Conclusion: 将程序结构发现与数值参数校准解耦是一种有效的自动化集成零成本代理构建方法，能够提升神经架构搜索中候选网络排序的泛化性能，为解决不同代理在不同搜索空间表现不一致的问题提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Zero-cost proxies enable neural architecture search (NAS) to rank candidate networks from statistics computed at initialization, avoiding repeated training. However, different proxies capture different properties and often produce inconsistent rankings across search spaces. Ensemble proxies can combine complementary signals, but automated discovery must optimize both discrete aggregation structures and their continuous coefficients, making structural quality difficult to separate from parameter calibration. We propose Bi-EZP, a bilevel framework that decouples these decisions. At the upper level, a large language model generates executable aggregation programs over four complementary base proxies with program-specific parameter bounds. At the lower level, covariance matrix adaptation evolution strategy (CMA-ES) optimizes the continuous parameters of each fixed program on an inner training split. The calibrated programs are then evaluated using Kendall's rank correlation on a disjoint validation split, enabling evolutionary selection to favor structures that generalize beyond their calibration data. Experiments on NATS-Bench and Network Design Spaces evaluate ranking performance across heterogeneous search spaces, and DARTS experiments assess downstream architecture search. Results show that separating program discovery from numerical calibration provides an effective approach to automated ensemble zero-cost proxy construction. The source code is available at: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/Bi-EZP-318D

</details>


### [262] [Gated Decoupled Compositional Bandits: A Unified Theory of Contextual Bandits with Supervised-Calibrated Action Scaling and Pre-Execution Gating](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21993)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了门控解耦组合强盗算法(GDCB)家族，通过三个结构创新——动作组合、独立监督学习缩放器、预执行门控——将非平稳bandit问题转化为近似平稳问题，在六个工业系统(短期租赁定价、临床给药、信贷审批等)中证明了受监管领域的约束机制(审批门、合规规则、安全屏障)是实现快速部署的推动力而非障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有bandit算法分类(LinUCB、LinTS、HierTS、因子化bandit、神经上下文bandit、RLHF)无法涵盖某些工业场景的特殊结构需求。在受监管的高风险领域中，审批门控、合规规则、安全屏障等约束通常被视为部署摩擦，但作者认为这些机制实际上可能促进而非阻碍快速部署。需要一种新的算法框架来统一建模这类具有动作组合、独立参数学习和预执行约束的系统。

Method: GDCB包含三个结构创新：(i)动作是名义臂(由离散或分层bandit选择)与上下文相关缩放器的组合；(ii)缩放器参数在独立监督循环中学习，不与臂选择联合优化；(iii)每个动作通过预执行门控，可修改或否决组合动作。证明了四个结构定理来刻画其统计行为，并将六个工业系统统一表述为GDCB实例，仅组合算子、缩放器族和门控不同。

Result: 核心结果为解耦方差缩减定理：校准良好的缩放器消除了臂到奖励映射中由上下文引起的方差，将非平稳bandit问题转化为近似平稳问题。门控诱导等价定理表明在平稳门控下，任何先验策略收集的历史数据无需重要性采样校正即可作为有效预热初始化，将P-HITL结果从人类审批推广到任意门控。在真实生产数据上验证了短期租赁动态定价实例。

Conclusion: 六个工业重要系统(短期租赁动态定价、临床药物剂量、信贷发放、电网需求响应、内容审核、LLM工具使用代理)都是GDCB实例。在受监管高风险领域，通常被视为部署摩擦的约束(审批门、合规规则、安全屏障)是实现快速部署的机制，而非障碍。GDCB框架为这类系统提供了统一的理论基础和实用指导。

Abstract: We introduce Gated Decoupled Compositional Bandits (GDCB), a family of contextual bandit algorithms with three structural innovations that jointly fall outside the taxonomy of LinUCB, LinTS, HierTS, factored bandits, neural contextual bandits, and RLHF. In a GDCB system: (i) the action delivered to the environment is the composition of a nominal arm, drawn by a discrete or hierarchical bandit, with a context-dependent scaler; (ii) the scaler parameter is learned in a separate supervised loop, not jointly with arm selection; and (iii) every action passes through a pre-execution gate that may modify or veto the composed action before it reaches the environment. We formalise this class of algorithms, prove four structural theorems characterising its statistical behaviour, and show that six industrially significant systems -- short-term rental dynamic pricing, clinical drug dosing, credit origination, grid demand response, content moderation, and LLM tool-use agents -- are all instances of GDCB, differing only in the composition operator, scaler family, and gate. The central result is the Decoupling Variance Reduction theorem: a well-calibrated scaler removes context-induced variance from the arm-to-reward mapping, turning a non-stationary bandit problem into an approximately stationary one. The Gate-Induced Equivalence theorem shows that under a stationary gate, historical data collected under any prior policy is a valid warm-up initialiser without importance-sampling correction, generalising the companion P-HITL result (arXiv:2606.02595) from human approval to arbitrary gates. In regulated, high-stakes domains, constraints usually treated as deployment frictions -- approval gates, compliance rules, safety shields -- are the mechanism that makes fast deployment possible, not an obstacle to it. The companion paper validates instance 1 (STR dynamic pricing) on real production data.

</details>


### [263] [Variance Driven Exploration: A Provable and Efficient Methodology for Pure Exploration in Highly Stochastic Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21995)
*Khang Luong,Nam Nguyen,Hoang Ta,Hung The Tran,Tuan Dam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Variance Driven Exploration (VarDE)方法，通过最小化最终决策不确定性来分配采样资源，显著提升高随机环境下的纯探索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高随机性环境中，随机方差主导探索过程，现有方法未能有效处理方差对最终决策可靠性的影响，缺乏针对方差衰减的采样资源分配策略。

Method: 通过平滑决策函数形式化最终决策的不确定性，推导出明确捕捉各分量随机噪声对最终输出可靠性影响的分配规则，应用于最佳臂识别(BAI)、蒙特卡洛树搜索(MCTS)和最佳策略识别(BPI)三个核心纯探索问题。

Result: 理论上给出方差衰减和简单遗憾的保证；实证结果显示VarDE相比现有方法有一致且显著的改进，在高随机环境下增益尤为突出。

Conclusion: VarDE为高随机环境下的纯探索问题提供了有效的解决方案，验证了基于方差驱动的采样资源分配策略的优越性。

Abstract: We propose Variance Driven Exploration (VarDE), a principled approach for pure exploration in highly stochastic environments, where the exploration process is dominated by stochastic variance. VarDE is built on a fundamental principle: sampling effort should be allocated to minimize the uncertainty of the final decision. We formalize the uncertainty of the final decision through a smooth decision function and derive allocation rules that explicitly capture how stochastic noise in individual components affects the reliability of the final output. We apply this methodology to three core problems of pure exploration -- Best Arm Identification (BAI), Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS), and Best-Policy Identification (BPI) -- with theoretical guarantees on variance decay and simple regret. Empirically, we demonstrate consistent and significant improvements of VarDE over existing methods, with especially strong gains in highly stochastic environments.

</details>


### [264] [DySCo: Dynamically consistent data-driven downscaling of extremes in climate projections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.21998)
*S. Stamatelopoulos,M. Wang,I. Lopez-Gomez,L. Zepeda-Nunez,Z. Y. Wan,R. Carver,F. Sha,T. P. Sapsis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DySCo降尺度框架，在保持与粗分辨率GCM动力一致性的同时，实现了高分辨率气候预测，解决了传统降尺度方法在极端事件因果分析中的关键挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区域气候风险评估对基础设施设计、灾害预测和保险资源配置至关重要。然而，利用全球气候模型(GCMs)估算高空间分辨率的区域风险在计算上不可行，因此需要发展降尺度方法。现有方法面临一个关键挑战：如何在提高分辨率的同时保持动力一致性，这对于基于情节的极端事件分析和风险评估至关重要。

Method: 提出了动态和统计一致的降尺度(DySCo)非侵入式框架。该方法依赖数据驱动的改进型逼近(nudging)技术，创建动态配对的训练轨迹，无需修改GCM。利用这些配对轨迹训练一个两阶段算子，实现动态和统计一致性。以CESM2大集合(LENS2)为测试对象，在时间和空间上进行降尺度至历史再分析数据。

Result: DySCo实现了与粗分辨率GCM轨迹更优的动力一致性，对GCM应用了最小化的因果修正。同时保持了与最先进无监督模型相当的顶级统计性能。

Conclusion: DySCo成功解决了降尺度方法中动力一致性的关键挑战，为因果极端事件分析和基于情节的风险评估提供了有效工具，同时保持了卓越的统计性能，为区域气候风险评估提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Regional climate risk assessment is critical for applications such as infrastructure design, disaster forecasting, and insurance resource allocation. However, estimating regional (i.e., high-spatial-resolution) risk with global climate models (GCMs) remains computationally prohibitive, which has driven the development of downscaling methods for coarse GCM outputs. Downscaling is vital for rare events, since quantifying their extreme properties requires high spatial resolution and very long GCM simulations. These methods non-intrusively increase GCM resolution while correcting statistical biases from unresolved fine-scale processes, thereby improving the accuracy of extreme event statistics with long return periods. A key challenge is preserving dynamical consistency, as freely evolving GCM trajectories are not expected to track the observational dataset used for training the correction operator. This is critical for causal extreme event analyses, where storyline-based risk assessment, i.e., extreme event catalogs, is necessary for effective planning. We address this challenge by introducing Dynamically and Statistically Consistent downscaling (DySCo), a non-intrusive framework yielding high-resolution climate projections consistent with coarse GCM dynamics. DySCo relies on a data-driven reformulation of nudging to create dynamically paired training trajectories without intrusive GCM modifications. Using these paired trajectories, we train a dynamically and statistically consistent, two-stage operator. We evaluate the method by downscaling the Community Earth System Model v2 Large Ensemble (LENS2) in time and space towards historical reanalysis. Results show DySCo achieves superior dynamical consistency with the coarse GCM trajectories, essentially applying a minimal, causal correction to the GCM, preserving top statistical performance comparable to state-of-the-art unsupervised models.

</details>


### [265] [The Communication Map of a Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22007)
*Richard Zhe Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出通信图谱方法，首次从模型权重自动识别所有潜在的通信通道，发现70-89%的注意力头对存在显著耦合或回避关系，并成功定位了影响上下文复制能力的关键子空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer组件通过共享残差流通信，现有机制可解释性研究依赖人工逐个电路映射，效率低下且难以规模化。需要一种自动化方法来系统地识别和分析模型中所有潜在的通信通道。

Method: 将Elhage等人的组合分数推广为单一耦合系数，覆盖从整个注意力头电路到单个神经元的全部18种连接类别。通过仅分析模型权重，枚举所有候选通道（GPT-2中6.3×10^8个，Pythia-6.9B中1.3×10^11个），计算耦合强度和方向性。

Result: 发现70-89%的头对耦合方向显著偏离随机分布，部分强耦合、部分主动回避。最强的头-头耦合能无监督恢复已知的归纳电路并分组为社区；消融一个社区可破坏模型上下文复制能力。识别出独特的二维流子空间，删除该子空间会在六个模型中消除归纳能力，且该子空间与激活PCA或异常值维度识别的子空间不同。

Conclusion: 通信图谱提供了一种高效、全面的机制可解释性工具，能够在大规模语言模型中自动发现功能电路和关键子空间，为模型干预和理解提供了新途径。计算成本可控（GPT-2需15秒，Pythia-6.9B需11分钟，单GPU）。

Abstract: The components of a transformer communicate by writing to and reading from a shared residual stream, and mechanistic interpretability has mapped these connections by hand, one circuit at a time. We present the communication map, which charts every potential communication channel in a language model from weights alone, generalizing the composition score of Elhage et al. (2021) into a single coupling coefficient covering all 18 connection classes, from entire attention head circuits to single neurons. The census of all candidate channels, from $6.3\times10^{8}$ in GPT-2 to $1.3\times10^{11}$ in Pythia-6.9B, finds that 70-89% of head pairs are oriented far from chance, some coupled strongly and others actively avoiding each other. The full map costs 15 seconds for GPT-2 and 11 minutes for Pythia-6.9B on one consumer GPU. Two applications demonstrate the utility of the map. In Application 1, the strongest head-to-head couplings recover the known induction circuits blind and group them into communities, and ablating one such community destroys the model's in-context copying. In Application 2, pooling every head's coupling coefficients identifies a distinct two-dimensional stream subspace, whose deletion abolishes the induction capability in six models up to Pythia-6.9B. This subspace is different from those identified by either activation PCA or outlier dimensions. We release the map, the statistical machinery, and the intervention suite.

</details>


### [266] [Spectral Pre-Filtering for Context-Adaptive Sensor Fusion: A Four-Role FFT-GDCB Integration for High-Stakes Decision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22023)
*Oleg Miroshnichenko*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于FFT的四重预过滤器，解决了上下文自适应卡尔曼滤波器在周期性结构信号中噪声协方差矩阵校准的污染问题，同时实现了信号白化、创新清洗、谱特征生成和去季节化四种功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当传感器或信号包含周期性结构（如LiDAR旋转谐波、引擎振动、地面多路径、周期性需求、给药节奏等）时，基于创新残差在线回归的自适应卡尔曼滤波器会出现噪声协方差矩阵Q和R的校准偏差，因为回归输入被污染，导致协方差模型拟合了结构性模式而非真实的状态不确定性。

Method: 设计了一种O(N log N)复杂度的FFT预过滤器，在卡尔曼更新前进行白化处理、清洗创新序列以防止协方差回归污染、生成谱上下文特征增强下游老虎机的状态选择、并在监督回归前去季节化输入特征向量；该算法被定位为GDCB（门控解耦组合老虎机）家族中的预处理层。

Result: 在火箭下降、自动驾驶跟踪、短期租赁定价、临床药物剂量、航空票价分布和广告竞价校准等六个独立领域进行了实证验证，均返回PROVES裁决；该FFT调用占总计算预算<0.1%，可作为即插即用模块无需修改现有卡尔曼滤波器、老虎机或运行时组合算子。

Conclusion: 该FFT预过滤器通过单次O(N log N)计算同时服务四个下游消费者，以极低的计算成本有效解决了周期性结构信号中的卡尔曼滤波器协方差估计问题，并在多个实际应用场景中得到验证。

Abstract: Context-adaptive Kalman filters calibrate their noise covariance matrices Q and R from innovation residuals via online regression. When the underlying sensor or signal carries periodic structure -- mechanical LiDAR rotation harmonics, engine vibration, ground multipath, weekly and annual demand cycles, dosing-interval rhythms, weekly media-buying cadence -- the regression input is contaminated and the fitted covariance models structural modes rather than genuine state uncertainty. We introduce a four-role FFT pre-filter that solves this problem at $O(N\log N)$ cost and serves three additional roles "for free": (i) it whitens coloured noise before the Kalman update, restoring the optimality assumption; (ii) it cleans innovations before covariance regression, preventing periodic contamination of $\hat{R}$ and $\hat{Q}$; (iii) it generates spectral context features that enrich the downstream bandit's regime-selection state; (iv) it deseasonalises the input feature vector before any supervised regression that produces a sensitivity coefficient (beta, dose offset, bid modifier). We position the algorithm inside the Gated Decoupled Compositional Bandits (GDCB) family, where it acts as a preprocessing layer for the supervised scaler. The single $O(N\log N)$ FFT call thereby serves four downstream consumers, fits in <0.1% of the sensor-fusion or pricing-pipeline compute budget, and is a drop-in addition with no changes to the Kalman filter, bandit, or runtime composition operator. We summarise empirical validation across six independent domains (rocket descent, autonomous-vehicle tracking, short-term rental pricing, clinical drug dosing, airline fare distribution, and ad-operations bid calibration), all returning a PROVES verdict under a pre-registered evaluation protocol.

</details>


### [267] [ARCHER: Amortized cross-specimen pose estimation for cryo-electron microscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22029)
*Nhan D. Nguyen,Bao Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ARCHER是一种零样本、跨结构的单粒子冷冻电镜姿态估计方法，在100个测试结构上实现5.0°中位角度误差，在实验粒子中实现2.5°，无需针对每个结构重新训练，同时保留了构象信号（相关性0.97）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单粒子冷冻电镜姿态估计方法需要针对每个数据集从头开始迭代优化，估计器在权重中学习存储分子结构，这种方法缺乏跨结构的泛化能力，每次都需要重新训练。作者希望证明姿态推断是一个可泛化的、与样本无关的操作，可以通过显式条件化参考体积来实现跨结构迁移。

Method: 提出了ARCHER（amortized contrastive classifier），这是一种在离散旋转网格上建模姿态后验的摊销对比分类器。该方法通过显式条件化参考体积实现跨蛋白质结构训练，能够零样本操作而无需针对每个结构重新训练。其可迁移性基于傅里叶空间信息力学原理，其中所有样本依赖性都被参考结构的功率谱和空间范围所捕获。

Result: ARCHER在100个保留测试结构上实现了5.0°的中位角度误差，在实验粒子上达到2.5°。在三维重建中，与专用估计器相比，精度差异在0.16 Å以内。下游构象信号得到保留：主导构象坐标与沉积基准的相关性达到0.97，能够如实地重建自由能盆地和可移动结构域。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了冷冻电镜姿态估计可以跨不同结构进行泛化，姿态推断是一个与样本无关的可泛化操作。ARCHER的成功表明，通过显式条件化参考体积，可以实现零样本跨结构姿态估计，为冷冻电镜领域提供了更高效、更通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Single-particle cryo-electron microscopy (cryo-EM) pose estimation is traditionally solved anew for each dataset, where iterative refinement is done from scratch while the estimator learns to store the molecule in its weights. In this work, we show that pose inference is a generalizable, specimen-agnostic operation when conditioned explicitly on a reference volume. We introduce ARCHER, an amortized contrastive classifier that models the pose posterior over a discrete rotation grid. Trained across a variety of protein structures, it operates zero-shot without retraining per structure. This transferability is grounded in Fourier-space information mechanics, where all specimen dependence is captured by the reference structure's power spectrum and spatial extent. ARCHER achieves a median angular error of 5.0° on 100 held-out test structures and 2.5° on experimental particles, matching dedicated estimators within 0.16 Å in 3D reconstruction. Crucially, downstream conformational signal is preserved. The leading conformational coordinate correlates at 0.97 with deposited benchmarks, faithfully reconstructing free-energy basins and mobile domains. These results overall demonstrate that cryo-EM pose estimation can be generalized across different structures.

</details>


### [268] [ReMAP: Self-supervised learning to unveil brain representations and vulnerability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22042)
*Jade Perdereau,Virginie Loison,Kanssa El Ayeb,Louis Gervais,Melvin Berto Strouc,Fabrice Vallée,Thomas Moreau,Jérôme Cartailler*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用自监督学习从双电极原始EEG数据中学习麻醉轨迹的几何结构，构建了一个低维表示空间，其中麻醉深度成为可读轴。模型在超过1000名患者中验证，能准确预测麻醉深度（BIS MAE=3.2），并在预测长期认知和死亡结局方面表现出色（AUROC 0.86），证明了麻醉轨迹的几何结构携带临床相关信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有术中EEG监测几乎总是简化为单一专有深度指数，将丰富的脑状态轨迹坍缩为一个数值，忽略了大脑在状态间转移的方式。研究者希望探究麻醉轨迹的几何结构本身（而非仅其达到的深度）是否携带有临床意义的信息，从而更好地理解麻醉过程中大脑状态变化的动态特征。

Method: 采用基于相似性的自监督学习方法处理原始双电极额叶EEG数据（无需标签），将每条记录映射到低维空间。在该空间中，麻醉深度成为一个可读轴，而患者路径的形态编码了额外结构。研究在两个队列和两种采集系统中验证了该表示（共1000+患者），使用紧凑的68k参数模型，并与更大规模的EEG基础模型（4M-157M参数）进行比较。

Result: 模型准确预测麻醉深度（BIS平均绝对误差=3.2，R²=0.82）；在稀疏电极设置下，68k参数的紧凑模型与规模大几个数量级的EEG基础模型表现相当，证明表示方法与记录匹配比原始规模更重要。学习空间在无监督下沿独立于深度的梯度组织年龄。该空间与额叶α波、慢-δ波和爆发抑制等可解释的麻醉特征对齐。在独立纵向随访队列中，早期轨迹的几何结构能区分30个月认知和死亡结局，与年龄互补（AUROC 0.86）。

Conclusion: 大脑在麻醉过程中描绘的路径是潜在脆弱性的标签高效相关因子，这一发现为前瞻性验证提供了动力。研究结果表明，麻醉轨迹的几何结构携带重要的临床信息，为改进术中脑监测和预测患者预后提供了新视角。

Abstract: General anesthesia offers a rare opportunity to observe the human brain under a standardized, controlled perturbation. Yet intraoperative electroencephalography (EEG) is almost always reduced to a single proprietary depth index, collapsing a rich trajectory into one number and discarding how a brain moves between states. Here we ask whether the geometry of that trajectory, not merely the depth it reaches, carries clinically meaningful information. Using similarity-based self-supervised learning on raw, two-electrode frontal EEG, with no labels, we place each recording within a low-dimensional space in which anesthetic depth becomes one readable axis while the shape of a patient's path encodes additional structure. We validate the representation across two cohorts and two acquisition systems totaling more than 1,000 patients. Depth of anesthesia is predicted accurately (BIS mean absolute error = 3.2, R2 = 0.82), and in the sparse-montage setting our compact ( 68k parameter) model remains competitive with EEG foundation models orders of magnitude larger (4M-157M parameters), indicating that matching the representation to the recording dominates raw scale. The learned space organizes age along its own gradient, independent from depth, without supervision. The same space also aligns with interpretable anesthetic signatures like frontal alpha, slow-delta, and burst suppression, linking this data-driven representation to established neurophysiology. On an independent cohort with longitudinal follow-up, the geometry of the early trajectory separates 30- month cognitive and mortality outcomes complementary to age (AUROC 0.86). These results suggest that the path a brain traces through anesthesia is a label-efficient correlate of latent vulnerability, motivating prospective validation.

</details>


### [269] [Personalized and Aspiration-Oriented Career Path Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22056)
*Kuleshwar Sahu,Girish Keshav Palshikar,Rajiv Srivastava*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的方法，通过结合职业路径相似性(CPS)和期望相似性(AS)为员工推荐个性化的职业发展路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现职业期望对员工和组织成长至关重要，需要一种个性化的方法帮助员工找到既能发挥个人优势又能满足职业期望的发展路径。

Method: 提出两种计算职业路径相似性的方法：(a)基于领域知识驱动(DKD)的特征提取和汇总方法；(b)基于无监督表示学习与对齐(URLA)的方法，使用LSTM神经网络学习职业事件名称的嵌入向量，并通过加权余弦距离的最优Levenshtein对齐来计算相似性。同时定义了期望与职业路径之间的匹配方法来计算AS，最终结合CPS和AS对候选职业路径进行排名。

Result: URLA方法在DCG(折损累积增益)指标上优于DKD方法，且两种方法的排名结果具有一致性。URLA方法更具优势，因为它不需要领域知识来建模相似性，并且通过对齐方法包含了时间维度。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法能够有效推荐个性化职业路径，URLA方法由于不需要领域知识且能捕捉时间特征，在职业路径推荐任务中表现更好，为员工职业发展提供了有效的决策支持。

Abstract: Fulfilling career aspirations is important for growth of employee and organization. We propose a data driven methodology to recommend personalized career path for a given aspirant's career path and aspirations. The pro-posed method uses the career path similarity (CPS) between aspirant's career and candidate career path, and 'aspirational similarity' (AS) between aspiration and candidate career paths to find suitable career path. CPS ensures personalized recommendation while AS ensures aspiration fulfillment. We defined two methods to compute the CPS between career paths which are (a) domain knowledge driven (DKD) and, (b) unsupervised representation learning and alignment (URLA) based, along with different AS measures. The DKD based similarity is defined in the terms of features extracted and summarized over career paths. In the URLA, we use the sequence of event names present in the career paths of the employees to learn the embedding for each event name. In URLA we use learned embedding vector of the career path event names and as-sociated event attributes (skill cluster and domain) to find the best alignment between two career paths. We hypothesized that relative position of event names in the sequence represents semantics of event name and that can be learned. We use LSTM neural network to learn the embedding vector of each career event name. We also define the matching method to compute the AS be-tween aspiration and career path in both proposed methods. We combine CPS and AS to rank available 'candidate career paths' of employees to find the suitable one. We get better DCG value in URLA as compare to DKD. We also showed that ranking are coherent using both the methods. URLA method is better since it does not require domain knowledge to model the similarity and includes temporal aspect by optimal Levenshtein alignment using weighted cosine distance.

</details>


### [270] [Improving Energy Efficiency of Oil Platforms Through Optimal Loading of Diesel Generators Using Machine Learning and Search Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22076)
*Khivishta Boodhoo,Josh Plumbly,Nicholas Watson*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用机器学习和搜索算法优化苏格兰海上石油平台的柴油发电机负载组合，实现日均27%的柴油节省（约24,000升/天）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上油气平台面临能源利用效率低、系统故障、可达性和环境影响等挑战，而现有研究多关注提高石油产量，忽视了平台自身的能源消耗优化问题。

Method: 收集苏格兰某海上平台18个月的运行数据，聚焦四台柴油发电机；通过探索性数据分析、异常值检测，构建多种回归模型（多元线性回归、人工神经网络、极端随机树、XGBoost和随机森林）预测不同功率负载下的柴油消耗，并运用搜索算法识别最小化柴油消耗的发电机功率负载组合。

Result: 多元线性回归和人工神经网络在预测性能上表现最佳；优化的功率负载组合相比最差组合，平均每日可节省27%的柴油消耗，相当于约24,000升/天。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于机器学习的优化方法在海上石油平台能源效率提升方面具有显著潜力，为平台的可持续运营提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: Rising energy demand, fossil fuel depletion and climate change highlight the need for more efficient energy production and consumption. Offshore oil and gas platforms face challenges related to inefficient energy use, system failures, accessibility and environmental impact. Machine learning (ML) offers opportunities to improve the safety, sustainability and efficiency of these systems; however, previous research has largely focused on increasing oil production rather than reducing energy consumption on platforms. This study investigates the use of ML and search algorithms to improve diesel efficiency on an offshore oil platform. Data collected over 18 months from a platform in Scotland were analysed, focusing on four diesel generators as the primary diesel-consuming equipment. Following exploratory data analysis and outlier detection, regression models were developed to predict daily diesel consumption for different generator power loads. Multiple Linear Regression and Artificial Neural Networks achieved the best predictive performance compared with Extra Trees Regression, Extreme Gradient Boosting and Random Forest. Search algorithms were then used to identify combinations of generator power loads that minimised daily diesel consumption. The results showed an average diesel saving of 27% per day compared with the worst daily power-load combinations, equivalent to approximately 24,000 litres/day. These findings demonstrate significant opportunities for improving energy efficiency on offshore oil platforms using ML-based optimisation.

</details>


### [271] [Counterfactual Quotient Models: Learning What Actions Change, Not What the World Does](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22092)
*Junlin Chen,Ruijie Wang,Jianxin Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出反事实商模型，通过消除与动作无关的共享成分，仅学习动作相关的效果，提高了强化学习模型的效率和决策准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有强化学习模型预测完整的未来状态或特征占用，而动作选择实际上只取决于候选动作后果的差异，导致模型将大量统计和表示能力浪费在与当前动作无关的高维现象上。

Method: 提出反事实商模型，将仅在共享成分上不同的动作条件未来视为等价，通过规范中心化表示去除公共成分，从同步反事实rollout中直接学习动作相关效果，使共享随机动态在函数逼近前就相互抵消。

Result: 建立了该表示的决策充分性、可识别性、共模不变性、逼近行为和遗憾性质；在物理环境中的受控实验证明，直接效果学习抑制了动作无关变化，支持未见过的奖励查询，并改善了相对于预测绝对未来模型的动作排序。

Conclusion: 反事实商模型通过只关注动作相关的效果差异，有效避免了建模与决策无关的高维现象，在理论上具备完善的性质，在实践中表现出更高的效率和更好的决策能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement-learning models commonly predict complete future states, observations, or feature occupancies, even though action selection depends only on differences between the consequences of candidate actions. As a result, these models may devote substantial statistical and representational capacity to high-dimensional phenomena that evolve independently of the agent's current choice. We introduce the Counterfactual Quotient Model, which treats action-conditioned futures as equivalent when they differ only by a component shared across actions. Its canonical centered representation removes this common component while preserving every pairwise action comparison expressible by the modeled reward family. The implemented model learns these action-dependent effects directly from synchronized counterfactual rollouts, so shared stochastic dynamics cancel before function approximation rather than after complete futures have been predicted. We establish the decision sufficiency, identifiability, common-mode invariance, approximation behavior, and regret properties of the resulting representation. Controlled experiments in physics-based environments provide initial evidence for these properties: direct effect learning suppresses action-independent variation, supports previously unseen reward queries, and improves action ranking relative to models trained to predict absolute futures.

</details>


### [272] [Beyond Fresh Starts: Stateful Inference for Streaming ASR in Conversational Voice Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22101)
*Sameep Chattopadhyay,Alexander Erdmann,Mari Ostendorf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对流式语音识别系统在对话中因长停顿和反向通道等现象导致的开头错误率问题，提出了两种保留跨语句上下文的状态管理策略，在开头错误率上实现了15-21%的相对降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代语音代理系统依赖流式语音识别模型，受实时处理的内存限制，会受长停顿和反向通道等对话现象的不利影响。现有解决方案通过每轮重置状态来缓解，但这会丢失关键上下文，损害语句开头的识别性能。

Method: 提出了两种状态管理策略，旨在保留跨语句的上下文信息，减少语句开头错误。在两个最先进的流式模型和两个口语对话基准上进行了实验验证。

Result: 最佳方法在语句开头的词错误率（WER）上实现了平均15-21%的相对降低。

Conclusion: 所提出的状态管理策略有效解决了流式语音识别系统在对话场景中开头识别性能下降的问题，证明了保留跨语句上下文的重要性。

Abstract: Modern voice-agent systems rely on streaming speech recognition models that operate under stringent latency constraints. This study shows that, due to the limited memory constraints of real-time processing, these systems are adversely impacted by conversational phenomena such as long silences and backchannels. While many agentic pipelines mitigate this by resetting state at each turn, this approach discards vital context and impairs performance at turn onsets. We propose two state-management strategies that preserve cross-utterance context to reduce onset errors. In experiments with two state-of-the-art streaming models on two spoken dialogue benchmarks, our best method yields an average of 15-21% relative WER reduction at utterance onsets.

</details>


### [273] [Symbolic Neural ODEs: Learning interpretable models from time-series data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22112)
*Nibodh Boddupalli,Jeff Moehlis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一种结合多步预测训练和稀疏正则化的机器学习框架，能够从时间序列数据中识别出稀疏、可解释且具有良好泛化能力的动力系统模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于单步回归的动力系统识别方法存在模型稳定性差、泛化能力不足的问题，特别是在长期预测和复杂动力行为（如混沌系统）建模方面表现不佳。

Method: 采用神经网络参数化向量场，通过在有限时间窗内最小化多步预测的平均绝对误差来训练模型，训练过程中逐步增加预测时长。结合稀疏正则化技术促进模型简约性，该方法强制学习到的动力学在重复组合下保持一致性。

Result: 成功恢复了具有多种行为的动力系统（包括稳定/不稳定不动点、周期轨道和混沌吸引子）。对于混沌系统，虽然长期轨迹预测受初值敏感性限制，但模型在短期动力学和长期统计性质（均值、方差、李雅普诺夫指数）方面表现优异，同时建立了轨迹误差与统计精度之间的理论界限。

Conclusion: 多步训练方法相比单步回归能产生更稳定的动力系统模型，结合稀疏正则化可获得简洁且泛化能力强的模型，为从数据中识别动力系统提供了一种有效的新范式，并有理论支持其在统计性质预测方面的有效性。

Abstract: We present a machine learning framework for identifying sparse, interpretable models of dynamical systems directly from time-series data. Our approach parameterizes the underlying vector field using a neural architecture and trains it by minimizing a multi-step prediction loss over a finite horizon. To ensure numerical tractability, we optimize a mean absolute error objective averaged across prediction steps, and progressively increase the horizon during training. A key feature of this formulation is that it enforces consistency under repeated composition of the learned dynamics. As a result, the identified models exhibit significantly improved stability compared with approaches based on one-step regression of the vector field. When combined with sparsity-promoting regularization, this leads to parsimonious models that generalize beyond the training data. We demonstrate accurate recovery of systems exhibiting a wide range of behaviors, including stable and unstable fixed points, periodic orbits, and chaotic attractors. For chaotic systems, while long-term trajectory prediction is inherently limited by sensitivity to initial conditions, we show that multi-step training yields models with accurate short-term dynamics and strong agreement in long-time statistical properties, including mean, variance, and Lyapunov exponents. Moreover, we establish theoretical bounds linking trajectory error to statistical accuracy, providing a step toward a principled explanation for this behavior.

</details>


### [274] [CST: Collaborative Selective Transmission for Communication-Efficient Multimodal Edge Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22115)
*Hai Chi,Junrui Zhang,Rui Ning,Chonggang Wang,Robert Gazda,Huanrui Yang,Hongyi Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CST框架，通过主设备引导的查询-响应机制，仅检索与主设备表示互补的辅助信息，在三个多模态感知基准上传输不超过14.18%的辅助特征值，实现最佳或接近最佳的任务性能，端到端推理速度提升高达4.27倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有协作多模态推理方法在减少通信开销时仅优化紧凑性或任务相关性，未显式考虑主设备已有信息，导致任务相关但冗余的辅助特征仍占用带宽。需要一种方法只传输对主设备真正互补的信息。

Method: 受偏信息分解和多视图冗余假设启发，提出CST框架：(1)主设备发送支持索引而非密集特征；(2)学习样本自适应、辅助设备特定的稀疏检索支持；(3)避免检索主设备已覆盖或跨辅助设备重复的语义信息。

Result: 在三个真实多模态感知基准上，CST传输不超过14.18%的辅助特征值，达到最佳或接近最佳任务性能；在五节点NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano测试台（5-100 Mbps）上，相比Transmit-All实现最高4.27倍端到端推理加速。

Conclusion: CST通过显式建模主设备已有信息，有效识别并仅传输互补性辅助信息，显著降低通信开销和端到端延迟，同时保持优异的任务性能，验证了该方法在实际边缘感知系统中的有效性。

Abstract: Collaborative multimodal inference improves edge perception by combining observations from distributed sensing devices, but transmitting high-dimensional helper representations incurs substantial communication overhead and can lead to high end-to-end latency. Existing communication-efficient methods reduce payloads through compression, semantic coding, or feature selection, yet typically optimize compactness or task relevance without explicitly accounting for information already represented at the main device. Consequently, task-relevant but redundant helper features may still consume bandwidth. We present Collaborative Selective Transmission (CST), a main-directed query--response framework that retrieves only helper information complementary to the current main representation. Inspired by Partial Information Decomposition and the Multiview Redundancy Assumption, CST learns sample-adaptive, helper-specific sparse retrieval supports while discouraging retrieval of semantics already covered by the main device or duplicated across helpers. During inference, the main device transmits only support indices, and each helper returns the corresponding latent values, avoiding dense helper-feature exchange. Across three real-world multimodal sensing benchmarks, CST transmits no more than 14.18% of helper feature values while achieving best or near-best task performance among the evaluated methods. Experiments on a five-node NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano testbed across 5--100 Mbps demonstrate up to a $4.27\times$ speedup over Transmit-All in end-to-end inference, confirming practical end-to-end latency reductions.

</details>


### [275] [TANGO: Token-Aggregated Nonlinear Gating Operators for Natural and Formal Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22117)
*Joshua Nunley*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TANGO和WANGO两种新型架构，通过单一跨token门控残差更新替代标准Transformer的两个子层。TANGO在多个基准测试中取得最佳验证NLL，WANGO在保持线性计算复杂度的同时超越Recurrent Transformer++。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Transformer块将自注意力的跨token交互与位置独立的非线性前馈网络分离。本研究旨在简化这一架构，将两个子层统一为一个跨token门控残差更新操作，以提升模型性能和计算效率。

Method: TANGO模型：每个源token生成SwiGLU门控向量，通过查询-键相似度确定源门控的加权平均，结果门控对投影后的目标特征进行重新缩放，序列长度复杂度为二次方。WANGO模型：在最近窗口内保持未归一化分数，对更早的源使用正特征图前缀统计，实现固定窗口和特征维度下的线性序列长度复杂度。所有模型约44.3M非嵌入参数，进行三次匹配训练。

Result: TANGO在FineWeb-Edu、Lean和DeepMind Mathematics上获得最低平均验证负对数似然，尽管前向传播操作计数最大。WANGO在线性计算复杂度架构中获得最低FineWeb-Edu NLL，在几乎相同的前向传播乘加计数下超越Recurrent Transformer++。

Conclusion: TANGO和WANGO证明了将跨token交互与非线性门控统一的有效性。TANGO提供了最佳性能，WANGO在保持线性复杂度的同时提供了优异的性能-效率权衡，两者均优于传统的分离式Transformer架构。

Abstract: A standard Transformer block separates cross-token interaction in self-attention from a nonlinear feed-forward network applied independently at each position. We introduce the TANGO model (Token-Aggregated Nonlinear Gating Operators), which replaces these two sublayers with one cross-token gated residual update. Each source token produces a SwiGLU gate vector. Query-key similarities determine a weighted average of source gates for each destination, and the resulting gate rescales projected destination features. TANGO assigns a separate weight to every causally visible source and is quadratic in sequence length. The WANGO model (Windowed Aggregation of Nonlinear Gating Operators) retains the same unnormalized scores within a recent window and uses positive feature-map prefix statistics for older sources, giving linear sequence-length complexity for fixed window and feature dimensions.
  We compare TANGO and WANGO with Recurrent and Untied Transformer++, full-attention GAU, and FLASH. All models have approximately 44.3M nonembedding parameters and are trained in three matched runs. TANGO, WANGO, and Recurrent Transformer++ apply one shared block four times; the other architectures use four independent blocks. TANGO obtains the lowest mean validation negative log-likelihood on FineWeb-Edu, Lean, and DeepMind Mathematics, although it has the largest analytical forward-pass operation count. WANGO obtains the lowest mean FineWeb-Edu NLL among the architectures with computation linear in sequence length and outperforms Recurrent Transformer++ at nearly the same analytical forward-pass multiply-accumulate count.

</details>


### [276] [The Price of Decentralization in Top-$K$ Arm Identification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22120)
*Larissa Xu,Jasmine Nguyen,William Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多智能体多臂老虎机中的Top-K联合臂识别问题，设计了无通信的UCB-Intervals算法，证明在三种可观测性机制下都能达到近乎最优的性能，统计代价仅为固定常数倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协作团队需要在无法完全观测队友行为和奖励的情况下，共同识别最优的K个联合臂选项，而非简单的累积奖励。这一需求在去中心化决策场景中普遍存在，但缺乏有效的无通信协调机制。

Method: 提出UCB-Intervals消除算法，针对三种可观测性机制设计：(A)共享奖励但隐藏动作，利用共享的臂排序；(B)观测动作但私有奖励，利用可观测的偏差；(C)完全不对称，使用放大的置信半径。所有机制都无需显式通信，通过隐式协调实现目标。

Result: 通过变尺度下界证明共享奖励识别机制在仅差一个通用对数因子的情况下达到最优；完全移除通信的统计代价仅为样本复杂度的ρ²倍（完全不对称情况下为固定的4倍）。停止时间为O(∑_a log(A^M/δ)/Δ_a²)，固定预算误差为exp(-Θ(T/H_1))，其中对联合动作数A^M的依赖性已证明不可避免。

Conclusion: 将三种可观测性机制统一到一个以乘数c和共识因子ρ为指标的元保证框架中，证明了无通信协调的理论最优性边界，为去中心化协作决策提供了完整的理论基础和实用算法。

Abstract: Cooperative teams often need to agree on the best few options rather than simply accumulate reward, and they must do so while each member sees only a fragment of the team's collective experience. We study this as top-$K$ joint-arm identification in multi-agent multi-armed bandits: at every round $M$ agents simultaneously choose individual actions that compose a joint arm, and the team must ultimately return the $K$ joint arms of highest mean reward. The difficulty is that an agent may not observe the actions of others, their rewards, or either. We treat three observability regimes---(A) shared rewards with hidden actions, (B) observed actions with private rewards, and (C) full asymmetry---and design communication-free elimination algorithms (UCB-Intervals) that reconstruct implicit coordination from whatever signal each regime leaves intact: a shared arm ordering in (A), observable deviations in (B), and enlarged confidence radii under (C). We give matching analyses in both the fixed-budget and fixed-confidence objectives, then fold all three regimes into a single meta-guarantee indexed by a multiplicity $c$ and a consensus factor $ρ$. Our central result is quantitative rather than merely algorithmic: change-of-measure lower bounds show that shared-reward identification is optimal up to one universal logarithmic factor, and that the entire statistical price of removing communication is a multiplicative $ρ^2$ in sample complexity---a fixed $4\times$ penalty under full asymmetry. The resulting stopping time scales as $O\!\left(\sum_{\mathbf{a}} \frac{\log(A^M/δ)}{Δ_{\mathbf{a}}^2}\right)$ and the fixed-budget error as $\exp(-Θ(T/H_1))$, with the dependence on the joint-action count $A^M$ shown to be unavoidable.

</details>


### [277] [Decoupled Physical Modeling and Execution for Physics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22126)
*Ye Zhang,Xuehang Guo,Rui Pan,Pengfei Yu,Denghui Zhang,Manling Li,Qingyun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种显式物理建模的两阶段训练框架，通过蒸馏中间表示并使用监督微调和基于评分标准的强化学习，在多个物理推理基准上平均提升约3%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型在数学和编程问题上表现优异，但在物理问题上仍然困难，因为物理问题将物理建模过程与数学计算纠缠在一起。人类解决物理问题的方式是先构建系统表示再进行计算，而现有模型缺乏这种显式的物理建模能力。

Method: 提出统一框架蒸馏中间表示，显式编码物理建模过程；采用两阶段后训练策略：第一阶段通过监督微调建立结构化建模，第二阶段使用基于评分标准的反馈强化学习来提升建模过程质量。

Result: 在PhysReason、PhyX和SeePhys三个多模态物理基准测试上，物理建模输出相比GRPO方法平均提升约3%，证明了显式物理建模对小规模LLM物理推理的有效性。

Conclusion: 显式物理建模是提升小规模LLM物理推理能力的有效策略，通过结构化建模和两阶段训练可以显著改善模型在不同数据集上的推理性能。

Abstract: Physics reasoning requires constructing a consistent model of the underlying physical system rather than relying solely on symbolic or formula-based manipulation. Although large language models have shown strong ability in solving math and coding problems, they still struggle with physics problems, as these problems entangle the physical modeling process with mathematical calculations. Humans approach physics by first building a representation of the system before performing calculations. Inspired by this, we introduce a unified framework that distills intermediate representations that explicitly encode the physical modeling process and adopt a two-stage post-training strategy, where supervised fine-tuning establishes structured modeling, and reinforcement learning with rubric-based feedback improves the quality of the modeling process. Experiments on multiple multimodal physics benchmarks show that our approach leads to consistent improvements in reasoning performance across different models and datasets. On PhysReason, PhyX and SeePhys benchmarks, physical modeling output performs GRPO by an average ~3%, showing that explicit physical modeling is an efficient strategy of improving physics reasoning for small LLMs.

</details>


### [278] [Who Should Teach? Confidence-Aware Dual-Teacher Learning for Few-Shot Node Classification on Text-Attributed Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22127)
*Hojin Kim,Sujin Yoon,Sungsu Lim,Dongwon Lee,David Yoon Suk Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CoTeach框架，通过动态选择更可靠的教师模型（GNN或LLM）来优化文本属性图的少样本节点分类，在提升性能的同时降低LLM调用成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在文本属性图学习中统一使用LLM信息，忽略了不同节点上LLM信息的可靠性差异，且产生高昂的金钱成本；GNN和LLM分别在结构和语义信息利用上具有互补优势，不同节点适合的监督源应有所不同。

Method: 提出CoTeach（Confidence-aware dual-teacher learning）框架，针对每个节点动态评估并选择更可靠的教师模型（GNN或LLM）作为监督来源，实现双教师协同学习。

Result: CoTeach在少样本节点分类任务上持续提升性能，同时减少了不必要的LLM调用及其相关金钱成本。

Conclusion: 通过置信度感知的双教师学习策略，可有效结合GNN与LLM的互补优势，实现更优的性价比和分类性能。

Abstract: Text-Attributed Graphs (TAGs) integrate graph structures and node-associated textual attributes, and recent studies have increasingly leveraged Large Language Models (LLMs) to improve TAG learning in few-shot settings. However, existing approaches typically utilize LLM-derived information uniformly across all nodes, despite substantial variations in its reliability, while also incurring considerable monetary costs. We argue that the most appropriate source of supervision may differ across nodes, as Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) and LLMs exhibit complementary strengths in exploiting structural and semantic information, respectively. To this end, we propose CoTeach, a Confidence-aware dual-teacher learning framework that dynamically selects the more reliable teacher for each node. Experimental results demonstrate that CoTeach consistently improves few-shot node classification performance while reducing unnecessary LLM utilization and associated monetary costs.

</details>


### [279] [Blockwise Stabilized Adaptive Cubic Regularization with Subsolvers via Recurrence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22129)
*Rodion Podorozhny*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分块三次正则化牛顿优化器，解决了传统方法因完全特征分解导致的可扩展性问题，在INR任务上相比Adam实现了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三次正则化牛顿方法具有最优的$\\mathcal{O}(\\varepsilon^{-3/2})$全局收敛率和自动鞍点逃逸机制，但其子问题求解通常需要完全特征分解，限制了可处理的模型规模。

Method: 提出分块优化器：1)按张量划分参数，每块独立最小化带自适应三次常数$M_b$的三次模型；2)根据块大小选择子问题求解器：小块使用懒精确三次步骤（显式形成每块Hessian），任意大张量使用Lanczos过程构建的免矩阵Chebyshev有界Krylov子空间；3)通过全损失的单调保护机制接受或拒绝每个块步。

Result: 在FINER INR（约199k参数）和91.4M参数ViSIR INR上验证：1)ARC-$\\varphi_1$优化器达到133.5 dB PSNR，而调优后的Adam在相同扩展预算下仅达78.2 dB；2)在Adam达到峰值所需约70分钟内，ARC-$\\varphi_1$达到95.6 dB；3)分块三次步骤在每个块上保持三次模型意义上的精确性。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功保留了$\\mathcal{O}(\\varepsilon^{-3/2})$收敛率，实现了对大规模模型（包括88.5M参数解码器张量）的高效优化，显著优于Adam等主流优化器，为大规模优化提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Cubic-regularized Newton methods have the optimal $\mathcal{O}(\varepsilon^{-3/2})$ global rate and an automatic saddle-escape mechanism, but their subproblem is most often solved by a full eigendecomposition, limiting feasible model size. We introduce a blockwise optimizer that partitions parameters by tensor, minimizes an independent cubic model with an adaptive cubic constant $M_b$ per block, and accepts or rejects each block step against a monotone guard on the full loss. The subproblem solver is chosen by block size: small blocks use lazy exact cubic steps from explicitly formed per-block Hessians; arbitrarily large tensors use a matrix-free Chebyshev-bounded Krylov subspace built by the Lanczos process.
