Prior work has addressed only half of this gap: reconstructing Kaplan-Meier (KM) curves from published plots -- typically requiring manual digitization or human-in-the-loop correction -- while offering no mechanism for generating the adverse-event (AE) streams that constitute the other half of a patient record. We introduce KMGen, the first end-to-end framework that (i) fully automates KM curve extraction at accuracy competitive with human-guided tools, and (ii) generates synthetic per-patient AE trajectories from public trial registry records. The extraction stage is a fully automated agentic pipeline -- an agent generates code to extract each step in the KM curve -- achieving a mean Integrated Absolute Error (IAE) of 0.0151 on a 32-plot benchmark spanning clean, edge-case, and adversarial conditions. The IPD generation stage decouples patient archetype extraction from statistical sampling: an LLM distills the trial record into arm-specific statistics, adverse events, patient demographics, and risk multipliers. A mechanistic sampler generates patient events via clinical archetypes, bootstrap rank-correlation coupling to the empirical KM curve (preserving the marginal survival distribution exactly), and cycle-based AE scheduling with an induction/maintenance split. Across three held-out oncology trials spanning an order of magnitude in cohort size and 30 independent regenerations per trial, KMGen achieves mean integrated KM absolute difference $Δ_{\text{KM}}\,{\leq}\,0.051$, sex/ECOG JSD ${\leq}\,0.013$ on 5 of 6 demographic slots, and recovers ${\geq}\,71\%$ of the top-15 AEs by exact MedDRA term under a single fixed parameter set. The pipeline is released as open source at https://github.com/chufangao/kmgen.

</details>


### [317] [Learning Generalizable Behaviors for Terminal Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22631)
*Yihang Yao,Bo Pang,Xuan Phi Nguyen,Ding Zhao,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出River训练方案，通过提升奖励信号质量而非单纯增加环境数量/多样性，显著改善了终端智能体的RL训练效果，在多个基准测试中取得开源8B模型最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 终端智能体RL训练面临真实用户交互数据稀缺的问题，现有合成环境存在领域差距和保真度不足，导致泛化性能差。现有工作主要关注扩展合成环境的数量和多样性，而奖励信号质量和泛化机制仍缺乏探索。

Method: 提出"智能体组合泛化"假设：RL主要通过塑造高层决策行为来组合和路由预训练及SFT阶段获得的低层技能，而非从头教授新技能。基于此提出River训练方案：1) 过滤低质量环境；2) 用过程级行为正则化增强结果奖励。该方法强调验证器质量比环境数量/多样性更重要。

Result: River在四个终端智能体基准测试中取得开源RL训练8B模型最佳性能。使用不到TMax训练环境30%的环境量，在Terminal-Bench-Lite和Terminal-Bench-v2.1上，对2B到27B模型平均提升RL增益106%和30%。该方法在模型家族、规模、智能体工具链和RL目标间具有良好的泛化性。

Conclusion: 验证器质量（决定哪些行为被强化）比单纯增加环境数量或多样性更为关键。RL在终端智能体训练中主要发挥组合调度已有技能的作用，而非教授新技能。通过提升奖励质量的简单方法即可获得显著的性能提升和泛化能力。

Abstract: Terminal agents are a compelling application of large language models (LLMs), with the potential to integrate deeply into users' daily workflows. Reinforcement learning (RL) is a key technique for improving their capabilities, making scalable training environments a central challenge. Since public real-user interaction data are scarce, synthetic environments provide a practical alternative, but often suffer from domain gaps and limited fidelity, leading to poor generalization. Existing work mainly scales the quantity and diversity of synthetic environments, while reward-signal quality and the mechanisms governing generalization remain under-explored. We study how RL improves terminal agents and propose the Agentic Compositional Generalization hypothesis: rather than teaching new domain-specific skills from scratch, RL primarily shapes high-level decision-making behaviors that compose and route low-level skills acquired during pre-training and supervised fine-tuning (SFT). This account is consistent with our empirical results and suggests that verifier quality, which determines which behaviors are reinforced, is more important than simply increasing environment quantity or diversity. Motivated by this insight, we propose River, a simple training recipe that improves reward quality by filtering low-quality environments and augmenting outcome rewards with process-level behavior regularization. Using this recipe, our RL-trained agent achieves the best performance among evaluated open-source RL-trained 8B models across four terminal-agent benchmarks. River also generalizes across model families, scales, agent harnesses, and RL objectives. Using fewer than 30% of the TMax training environments, River improves RL gains by 106% and 30% on average for models ranging from 2B to 27B on Terminal-Bench-Lite and Terminal-Bench-v2.1, respectively.

</details>


### [318] [Q-Learning with Stable Infinite-Dimensional Linear Function Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22636)
*Shengbo Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个稳定的无限维线性函数逼近Q学习框架，在单个马尔可夫行为策略轨迹下实现了\widetilde O(n^{-1/2})的收敛速率，算法可自适应调整平滑性和几何结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Q学习配合线性函数逼近存在不稳定性问题，因为任意逼近架构不保证保持贝尔曼收缩性质。需要开发一个理论上稳定且统计性质可控的框架。

Method: 构建无限维框架：在紧致潜在度量空间(C(L),ρ)上定义系数场θ，通过重构算子映射到连续Q函数，通过压缩算子将贝尔曼更新映射回潜在坐标。两个算子的非扩张性诱导出收缩潜在贝尔曼映射。提出两种随机逼近算法。

Result: 证明了算法在sup-范数下的收敛界为\widetilde O(n^{-1/2})。通过压缩映射的平滑性将均匀估计误差控制与(L,ρ)的覆盖数相关而非C(L)的维数。算法对ρ的选择不可知，可自动适应平滑性和几何结构。

Conclusion: 该无限维框架提供了识别统计难度结构的强大抽象工具，在Q-测度学习（线性密度逼近）和冻结预训练网络下的输出层神经权重训练中展示了应用价值。

Abstract: Q-learning with linear function approximation can be unstable because an arbitrary approximation architecture need not preserve the Bellman contraction. We develop a stable infinite-dimensional linear function approximation framework for Q-learning from a single Markovian behavior-policy trajectory. The learning variable is a coefficient field $θ\in C(\mathbb L)$ on a compact latent metric space $(\mathbb L,ρ)$. The framework uses a reconstruction operator that maps $θ$ to a continuous Q-function and a compression operator that maps Bellman updates back to latent coordinates. Nonexpansiveness of both operators induces a contractive latent Bellman map on $C(\mathbb L)$, with a unique fixed point $θ^*$ whose reconstruction approximates the optimal Q-function up to representation error. We propose two stochastic approximation (SA) algorithms and establish their sup-norm convergence bounds with a leading term of order $\widetilde O(n^{-1/2})$. The infinite-dimensional formulation provides a powerful abstraction for identifying the structures that govern statistical difficulty. Smoothness of the compression map in $ρ$ is inherited by $θ^*$ and the SA iterates, allowing uniform estimation errors to be controlled through covering numbers of $(\mathbb L,ρ)$ rather than the dimension of $C(\mathbb L)$. Remarkably, the SA algorithms we propose are agnostic to the choice of $ρ$, and thus can automatically adapt to both the smoothness and the geometry. We further illustrate the framework through Q-measure-learning with linear density approximation and output-layer neural weight training under a frozen pretrained network.

</details>


### [319] [Mol-JEPA: A multimodal Joint Embedding Predictive Architecture for Molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22642)
*Florian Rottach,Sebastian Schieferdecker,William Rudman,Randall Balestriero,Carsten Eickhoff*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Mol-JEPA通过模态掩码学习分子世界模型，整合多种药物发现数据，在多个基准测试中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分子基础模型存在化学无效增强、模态崩溃和生化环境表示不完整等局限性。

Method: 提出Mol-JEPA框架，采用模态掩码而非分子扰动，利用分子结构、细胞表型、结合亲和力、ADMET概况、量子化学模拟等多种药物发现数据，通过潜在空间预测整合生化上下文。

Result: 在多个基准测试中，Mol-JEPA学习到的表示表现出强大的性能。

Conclusion: 通过潜在空间预测整合生化上下文能够有效提升分子表示学习的质量。

Abstract: Despite recent advances in molecular foundation models, several limitations remain, such as chemically invalid augmentations, modality collapse, and incomplete representation of biochemical environments. To address these challenges, we present \textbf{Mol-JEPA}, a scalable framework for learning molecular world models. Rather than relying on suboptimal molecular perturbations, our model uses modality masking to exploit information from molecular structures, cellular phenotypes, binding affinities, ADMET profiles, quantum chemistry simulations and other drug discovery data. Across various benchmarks, we show that the representations learned by Mol-JEPA deliver strong performance, demonstrating the value of incorporating biochemical context through latent space prediction.

</details>


### [320] [Maximum-distance nonnegative matrix factorization for unmixing highly mixed grain-size distribution data: A generalization of AnalySize](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22681)
*Qianqian Qi,Zhongming Chen,Peter G. M. van der Heijden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出最大距离非负矩阵分解方法，通过最大化端元距离有效解决高度混合粒度分布数据的分解问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NMF方法AnalySize在处理高度混合的粒度分布数据时性能不佳，因为此时没有观测样本接近真实端元，导致端元估计困难。

Method: 提出最大距离NMF方法，鼓励估计的端元尽可能不同；开发分层交替最小二乘算法进行优化；该方法可视为AnalySize的推广，与AnalySize最小化端元距离相反，本文方法最大化端元距离。

Result: 实验结果表明，所提方法能够有效分解高度混合的粒度分布数据，克服了传统方法在此类数据上的局限性。

Conclusion: 通过最大化端元距离的策略，该方法成功扩展了NMF在粒度分布数据分析中的适用范围，特别是在高度混合场景下表现出显著优势。

Abstract: Nonnegative matrix factorization (NMF) decomposes a nonnegative matrix into the product of two nonnegative matrices. This property makes NMF well suited for unmixing grain-size distribution data, which are inherently nonnegative and have row sums equal to one. Previous studies have shown that AnalySize, an NMF-based method, performs well on poorly mixed grain-size distribution data but struggles when the data is highly mixed, where no observed samples are close to the true end members. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a maximum-distance NMF that encourages the estimated end members to be as distinct as possible and develop a hierarchical alternating least squares algorithm for optimization. The proposed formulation can be regarded as a generalization of AnalySize, where AnalySize minimizes the distance among end members while the proposed method maximizes it. Experimental results demonstrate that the method effectively decomposes highly mixed grain-size distribution data.

</details>


### [321] [Spiking Neural Networks for Continuous Control: Neuromorphic Reinforcement Learning in Conventional Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22729)
*Jessica Hunter,Md Maruf Hossain Shuvo,Krishna Roy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了SANSAC算法，验证了在传统硬件上用脉冲神经网络替换传统actor网络的可行性，性能与传统SAC相当，为神经形态强化学习研究提供了基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管强化学习算法在各类任务中取得进展，但在神经形态硬件上部署强化学习用于连续控制任务的验证仍然不足。特别是，不清楚在未体现硬件优势前，用脉冲神经网络（SNN）替换传统actor网络是否会影响智能体性能。

Method: 提出脉冲Actor网络软演员-评论家（SANSAC）算法，这是SAC的最小化神经形态可行脉冲actor变体。在传统计算机上系统性地对比传统SAC与SANSAC的性能，并分析隐藏层维度的影响。

Result: SANSAC与传统SAC在性能上表现相近，同时解决了隐藏层维度的影响问题。证明了基于SNN的算法在复杂连续环境中的可行性，以及在传统计算机上与传统神经网络相比具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 基于SNN的算法在复杂连续环境中具有可行性，能够与传统神经网络在传统计算机上达到竞争性性能，为后续在连续强化学习框架中探索SNN的应用奠定了基础。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) algorithms have made strides over the past decade applying them to a wide range of problems and control tasks. However, the deployment of RL on neuromorphic hardware for continuous control tasks remains under-validated. Namely it is unclear whether replacing a conventional actor network with a spiking neural network (SNN) affects the performance of an agent before any hardware-specific benefits manifest. We provide a systematic validation of a minimal, neuromorphically viable spiking actor variant of Soft Actor-Critic (SAC) on conventional hardware, establishing a baseline for future neuromorphic RL research. In this paper, we propose the Spiking Actor Network Soft Actor Critic (SANSAC) to address the use of RL frameworks in continuous environments, designed as a framework that can be implemented on neuromorphic hardware. We compare a traditional Soft Actor Critic (SAC) network to SANSAC in a traditional computer. We demonstrate the near equivalent performance of SANSAC and SAC, while addressing the impact of hidden dimensions. Our results demonstrate the viability of SNN based algorithms in complex continuous environments, as well as competitive performance to traditional neural networks in traditional computers, providing a basis to continue exploring the use of SNNs in continuous RL frameworks.

</details>


### [322] [MOSH-WM: Mask-Grounded Soft-Hamiltonian Dynamics for Object-Centric World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22750)
*Zhekai Wang,Haoxiang Huang,Xiang Liu,Zhikang Chen,Yueqing Sun,Qi Gu,Shiji Zhou,Miao Liu,Sen Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于掩码约束的软哈密顿世界模型（\method{}），通过将位置类状态显式地依赖于实体槽拥有的图像支撑区域，显著提升了视频预测精度。在OBJ3D和CLEVRER数据集上，LPIPS分别降低25.0%和14.5%，空间MSE降低33.7%和18.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有以物体为中心的世界模型在预测未来视频时，接受动力学监督的变量通常是无约束的视觉特征，缺乏明确的物理意义和几何约束。这种不确定性导致模型在长时程预测中误差累积较快，预测质量下降。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的视频-槽编码器生成槽和掩码；2) 通过掩码支撑区域的空间矩计算正则状态Q，时间差分形成P；3) 学习的能量函数为有界增量提供软方向偏置，构建软哈密顿动力学系统；4) 外观和身份信息存储在因果视觉上下文中；5) 通过门控组合器和有界残差将传播的相位状态与视觉上下文结合，重建解码器兼容的槽。

Result: 在OBJ3D数据集上，给定6帧观测并在后续30帧评估，相比最强基线，LPIPS降低25.0%，空间MSE降低33.7%；在CLEVRER数据集上，对应降低分别为14.5%和18.7%。完整模型在30帧闭环预测中误差累积更慢。

Conclusion: 通过将状态变量显式约束为掩码支撑的几何量并引入软哈密顿动力学，可以有效减少预测误差累积，显著提升以物体为中心的世界模型在长时程视频预测任务中的性能。

Abstract: Object-centric world models forecast future videos by evolving a set of entity slots, but the variables receiving dynamics supervision are often unconstrained visual features. We introduce \method{}, a mask-grounded soft-Hamiltonian world model that makes its position-like state explicitly depend on slot-owned image support. A frozen video-slot encoder produces slots and masks; spatial moments of mask-owned support form a canonical state $Q$, temporal differences form $P$, and a learned energy supplies a soft directional bias to a bounded learned increment. Decoder-relevant appearance and identity are stored separately in a causal visual context. A gated composer and bounded residual then combine this context with the propagated phase state to reconstruct decoder-compatible slots. On OBJ3D, given six observed frames and evaluated over the following 30 frames, \method{} reduces LPIPS by 25.0\% and spatial MSE by 33.7\% relative to the strongest object-centric baseline. On CLEVRER, given six observed frames and evaluated over the following ten frames, the corresponding reductions are 14.5\% and 18.7\%. Horizon-resolved visual and object-state measurements show that the complete model accumulates error more slowly throughout the 30-frame closed-loop rollout. Project page:https://github.com/moshwm-anon/-moshwm-anon.github.io.

</details>


### [323] [LpWM: A Case for Sparse Representations in World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22764)
*Yilun Kuang,Yash Dagade,Quentin Le Lidec,Lucas Maes,Randall Balestriero,Yann LeCun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明稀疏表示比密集表示更适合建模潜在动力学：通过LpWM模型实现非负稀疏编码，在PushT任务上将规划成功率提升高达57%，同时揭示了可解释的模态分解结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有JEPA模型通过匹配高熵分布（如各向同性高斯）获得密集表示，但密集表示是否最适合建模动力学尚不明确。本文探索稀疏表示是否能使动作条件的潜在动力学更易建模，以及这种表示会涌现何种动力学结构。

Method: 1. 理论证明：非线性Lipschitz动力学可在足够高维的one-hot潜在空间中被动作条件的线性动力学任意逼近；
2. 引入LpWorldModel (LpWM)：基于JEPA框架，使用校正分布匹配正则化(RDMReg)将编码器特征匹配到校正广义高斯分布，产生非负稀疏编码；
3. 将分布式稀疏表示作为one-hot稀疏性的实用松弛。

Result: 1. 稀疏性降低了成功规划所需的预测器复杂度：在PushT任务上，稀疏LpWM在中等预测器容量下的规划成功率比密集LeWM高57%；
2. 优势超越高斯分布匹配：LpWM在多个预测器家族上均优于密集VICReg表示；
3. 学习到的稀疏表示呈现模态分解：支撑集编码离散动力学模态，特征幅值捕获模态内的连续状态。

Conclusion: 稀疏表示能够降低控制任务所需的预测器复杂度，同时揭示可解释的结构，是建模动作条件潜在动力学的更优几何结构。

Abstract: Joint-embedding predictive architectures (JEPAs) learn latent dynamics for planning and avoid representation collapse by matching features to maximum-entropy distributions such as isotropic Gaussians, yielding dense representations. However, it is unclear whether dense representations are the most favorable geometry for modeling dynamics. In this work, we ask whether a different geometry, sparse representations, can make action-conditioned latent dynamics easier to model, and what dynamical structure emerges from such representations. We first show that nonlinear Lipschitz dynamics can be approximated arbitrarily well by action-conditioned linear dynamics in a sufficiently high-dimensional one-hot latent space, with rollout error vanishing as the dimension grows. This motivates distributed sparse representations as a practical relaxation of one-hot sparsity. We introduce LpWorldModel (LpWM), a JEPA model regularized with Rectified Distribution Matching Regularization (RDMReg) to match encoder features to a Rectified Generalized Gaussian distribution, yielding non-negative sparse codes. Empirically, sparsity lowers the predictor complexity required for successful planning: on PushT, sparse LpWM outperforms dense LeWM by up to 57% in planning success at intermediate predictor capacities. This advantage also extends beyond Gaussian distribution matching, with LpWM outperforming dense VICReg representations across multiple predictor families. We further find that the learned sparse representations are mode-factored, with support encoding discrete dynamical regimes and feature magnitudes capturing continuous within-regime state. Together, these results suggest that sparse representations can reduce the predictor complexity required for control while revealing interpretable structure.

</details>


### [324] [Learning to Control Coupled-Dynamics Environments with Joint Markov Decision Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22765)
*Ege C. Kaya,Aliasghar Pourghani,Mahsa Ghasemi,Vijay Gupta,Abolfazl Hashemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文扩展了联合马尔可夫决策过程(JMDP)框架，提出了非参数分布贝尔曼最优算子，证明了在特定条件下其迭代收敛于最优联合回报分布，并推导了神经近似的目标函数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准马尔可夫决策过程(MDP)仅允许对每个动作的边际分布进行推理，而丢弃了不同反事实动作结果之间的依赖关系。JMDP框架保留了这种依赖关系。先前工作已建立形式化框架并解决了固定策略下的联合矩评估问题，但尚未开发最优控制方法。本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 定义了JMDP的非参数分布贝尔曼最优算子，利用Wasserstein距离分析收敛性质，推导了神经近似的目标函数，并针对前两阶矩建立了在较弱条件下的收敛性证明。

Result: 证明当诱导的边际MDP具有唯一最优策略时，迭代在Wasserstein距离下收敛于最优联合回报律；对于前两阶矩，在允许存在多个均值最优动作（只要其平局解决方案共享二阶矩不动点）的较弱条件下建立了收敛性。

Conclusion: 本研究为JMDP框架提供了严格的最优控制理论基础，包括收敛性保证和实际近似方法，扩展了此前仅限于策略评估的工作，为在耦合动态环境中进行风险敏感决策提供了新工具。

Abstract: Coupled-dynamics environments expose the one-step outcomes that would follow from several possible counterfactual actions under a common realization of exogenous randomness. The ordinary Markov decision process formalism allows one to reason about the marginal law of each action but discards dependence across these counterfactual outcomes. The Joint Markov decision process (JMDP) formalism preserves that dependence. Prior work established the formalism and solved the fixed-policy joint moment evaluation problem in JMDPs. This paper develops optimal-control methods. We define a nonparametric distributional Bellman optimality operator for JMDPs, and prove that when the induced marginal MDP has a unique optimal policy, its iterates converge in Wasserstein distance to the optimal joint return law. For the first two moments, we establish convergence under a weaker condition that permits several mean-optimal actions as long as their tie resolutions share a second-moment fixed point. We also derive sampled targets for neural approximation.

</details>


### [325] [Neural Operator based Multi-Field Reconstruction of Inner Solar Boundary State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22782)
*Vignesh Kumar Pandian Sathia,Reza Mansouri,Dustin J. Kempton,Pete Riley,Rafal A. Angryk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究利用局部神经算子(LocalNO)学习太阳风多场多尺度状态，从径向速度和磁场重建完整的磁流体动力学变量，为日球层建模提供边界条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 太阳风预测和日球层建模需要完整的边界条件，但实际应用中往往只能获得部分场变量（径向速度和径向磁场），缺失其他关键物理量。为获得全面的太阳风预测视图和下游磁流体动力学模拟，需要从有限观测重建完整的边界状态。

Method: 采用局部神经算子(LocalNO)方法，这是一种能够学习函数空间之间映射的算子学习方法。与传统的回归模型和自编码器不同，神经算子更适合学习物理系统中的结构化场到场变换，同时保留局部性和分辨率感知能力。该方法能够处理高度非线性、空间耦合和多尺度的映射问题。

Result: 成功实现了从径向速度和径向磁场输入，重建非径向速度分量、非径向磁场分量、径向和非径向电流密度、热力学密度和压力分量等多物理场变量。重建的完整物理场与输入量一起，可用于未来的内日球层建模流程的边界条件。

Conclusion: 局部神经算子方法有效解决了太阳磁流体动力学多场多尺度状态重建这一具有挑战性的数据驱动科学机器学习问题，为日球层建模提供了完整的边界条件预测能力，证明了神经算子在物理系统场到场变换学习中的优势。

Abstract: The Solar wind is a continuous flow of charged particles emanating from the solar surface and governed by complex, interacting magnetohydrodynamic processes. Accurate specification of inner-boundary conditions is essential for heliospheric modeling and solar-wind prediction. In many practical applications, only a subset of interacting multi-field variables is directly available, but for a comprehensive view of solar wind prediction and downstream magnetohydrodynamic simulations, a more complete boundary state is required. In this work, we study the problem of learning the multi-field multi-scale solar magnetohydrodynamic state at 30 solar radii ($R_\odot$) using operator learning. Specifically, given the radial velocity and radial magnetic field, we aim to reconstruct the non-radial velocity and magnetic field components, radial and non-radial current density, thermodynamic density, and pressure components. This mapping is highly nonlinear, spatially coupled, and multi-scale, making it a challenging task for data-driven scientific machine learning.
  To address this problem, we employ a Local Neural Operator (LocalNO) that learns mappings between input and output function spaces while retaining locality and resolution-awareness. Unlike conventional regression models and autoencoder models, neural operators are better suited for learning structured field-to-field transformations arising from physical systems. The resulting predictions along with inputs are intended to serve as boundary condition variables for future inner-heliospheric modeling pipelines.

</details>


### [326] [ReCoG: Reciprocal Co-Evolution for Multimodal Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22786)
*Rui Xue,Tianfu Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ReCoG框架，通过图结构学习与多模态表示学习的端到端交互耦合，解决现有方法将两者分离的局限性，在节点分类和链接预测任务上显著优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态图神经网络存在关键缺陷：要么仅在固定图结构上进行模态对齐，要么从单模态表示推断拓扑而忽略多模态交互。这种分离策略无法有效捕获语义关系，因为观测到的边往往存在噪声、不完整或与底层语义不一致的问题。

Method: ReCoG采用双模块协同设计：(1)多模态图细化器利用跨模态语义证据推断和修正边；(2)耦合的跨模态消息传递机制在细化图上进行模态内和模态间联合传播。两模块端到端交互，实现拓扑与表示学习的动态协同演化。

Result: 在节点分类和链接预测的多个基准测试中，ReCoG持续超越强大多模态图结构学习基线，包括图基础模型，证明了该方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明结构-语义的协同演化对多模态图学习至关重要，挑战了当前拓扑学习与表示学习分离的主流范式，为多模态图学习提供了新的研究方向。

Abstract: Multimodal graph learning requires jointly training over graph structure and heterogeneous node attributes, yet existing methods largely decouple these processes: prior multimodal graph neural networks (GNNs) focus on aligning modalities in a shared embedding space while operating on fixed or weakly adapted graph structures, and graph structure learning approaches infer topology from unimodal node representations without accounting for multimodal interactions. This separation fundamentally limits the ability of GNNs to capture semantically meaningful relationships in multimodal settings, where observed edges are often noisy, incomplete, or misaligned with underlying semantics. We propose ReCoG (Reciprocal Co-Evolution for Multimodal Graph Learning), a new learning paradigm that tightly couples graph structure learning and multimodal representation learning through end-to-end reciprocal interaction. Concretely, ReCoG integrates (i) a multimodal graph refiner that infers and corrects edges using cross-modal semantic evidence, and (ii) a coupled cross-modal message passing mechanism that performs joint intra- and inter-modality propagation over the refined graph. This unified design yields greater expressiveness than decoupled or two-stage formulations and allows dynamic interaction between topology and representation learning. Across diverse benchmarks for node classification and link prediction, ReCoG consistently outperforms strong multimodal graph structure learning baselines, including graph foundation models. Our results demonstrate that reciprocal co-evolution of structure and semantics is important for effective multimodal graph learning, challenging the prevailing separation between topology and representation learning.

</details>


### [327] [Contrastive Representation-Guided Genetic Minority Oversampling for Imbalanced Time-Series Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22804)
*Wenbin Pei,Yunrong Hao,Zhen Liu,Guan Wang,Bing Xue,Yiu-Ming Cheung,Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出FreMGP方法，通过频域对比学习引导的多树遗传编程生成高质量少数类时间序列样本，有效解决类别不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实世界的时间序列分类任务常存在严重的类别不平衡问题。现有的插值过采样和深度学习生成方法在处理复杂时序依赖和少数类样本稀缺时，生成的样本泛化能力有限且多样性不足。

Method: 提出频域表示引导的多树遗传编程过采样方法（FreMGP）。每个个体代表一组少数类合成样本，并基于对比学习开发频域类别判别表示模块，引导进化搜索生成高质量合成时间序列样本。

Result: 在不平衡时间序列数据集上的实验表明，FreMGP优于现有过采样方法，并能持续提升不同分类器（包括传统机器学习和深度学习模型）的性能。

Conclusion: FreMGP通过频域引导的进化方法有效生成高质量时间序列样本，为类别不平衡问题提供了有效的解决方案，具有良好的泛化性和广泛适用性。

Abstract: Real-world time-series classification tasks often exhibit class imbalance, which can be extremely severe in some applications. To avoid training biased classifiers on imbalanced data, sampling is one of the most popular data pre-processing techniques because of its classifier-agnostic nature. However, due to the complex temporal dependencies in original time-series data and the scarcity of minority-class samples, existing sampling methods, including interpolation-based oversampling methods and deep learning-based generative models, usually suffer from limited generalization and poor diversity when generating new time-series samples. This paper proposes a Frequency-domain representation-guided Multi-tree Genetic Programming-based oversampling approach (FreMGP) to imbalanced time-series classification, where each individual represents a set of synthetic samples for the minority class. A frequency-domain class-discriminative representation module based on contrastive learning is also developed, guiding the evolutionary search toward high-quality synthetic time-series samples. Experiments on imbalanced time-series datasets demonstrate that FreMGP outperforms existing oversampling methods and consistently improves the performance of different classifiers, including both general machine learning and deep learning models.

</details>


### [328] [Change Detection in Probability Flow ODE: Online Testing in Diffusion Latent Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22807)
*Artem Kraevskiy,Artem Prokhorov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于条件扩散模型的非参数序列变点检测方法，能够在无需参数假设的情况下检测任意分布漂移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融趋势反转识别、音视频自动分割、运动传感器方向变化检测等序列数据任务都需要检测时间有序数据中的分布漂移。现有方法面临挑战：经典似然比统计量不适用于变化前后分布均无闭式表达的场景。

Method: 利用在变化前数据上训练的条件扩散模型（冻结上下文编码器），通过概率流ODE定义确定性双射，将变化前观测映射到标准高斯潜变量。变化后观测通过同一冻结映射会偏离该参考分布。采用最大均值差异（MMD）作为检验统计量，推导其在高斯零假设下各分量的闭式表达式，并建立其作为退化U统计量的渐近分布，最后应用Shiryaev-Roberts在线检测程序进行精确阈值校准。

Result: 该方法能够检测任意分布漂移，包括协方差旋转和高阶结构突变，且无需对任一状态（变化前后）作参数假设。推导出了MMD统计量在高斯零假设下的闭式表达式及其渐近分布。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于条件扩散模型的非参数方法有效解决了变化前后分布均无闭式表达的序列变点检测问题，实现了对广泛分布漂移类型的检测能力。

Abstract: A rapidly growing range of sequential data tasks, such as identifying trend reversals in financial markets, auto-segmenting video and audio recordings, detecting changes in movement direction from motion sensors cannot be fully addressed without detection of distributional shifts in time-ordered data. We consider a sequential change-point detection problem where the conditional density switches at an unknown time, yet neither the pre- nor post-change distribution admits a closed-form. Classical likelihood-ratio statistics are inapplicable in this settings.
  A conditional diffusion model, trained on pre-change-point data with a frozen context encoder, defines a deterministic bijection via the probability flow ODE. Pre-change observations are mapped onto standard Gaussian latent variables. Post-change observations, processed through the same frozen map, deviate from this reference. We employ the Maximum Mean Discrepancy as the test statistic, derive closed-form expressions for its components under the Gaussian null, and establish its asymptotic distribution as a degenerate U-statistic. Afterwards we apply an online detection procedure of Shiryaev--Roberts to the resulting statistic with exact threshold calibration.
  The method detects arbitrary distributional shifts, including covariance rotations and higher-order structural breaks, without parametric assumptions on either regime.

</details>


### [329] [SAGE: Stability-Aware Graph-Based Ensemble Feature Selection for Explainable Postpartum Depression Risk Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22809)
*Md. Rokon Islam Emon,Syed Shariar Alam Shuvo,Shahriar Siddique Ayon,Abdullah Al Mamun,Ahnaf Atef Choudhury*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGE系统，一种结合稳定性感知图集成特征选择与遗传算法优化神经网络的方法，用于产后抑郁症（PPD）早期预测，在766名产后女性队列上实现87.96%准确率，仅需16个特征，并提供个性化风险解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 产后抑郁症对母婴健康构成重大负担，尤其在低中收入国家患病率超过19%。现有机器学习预测方法存在关键局限：（1）全局解释缺乏患者层面的临床实用性；（2）特征选择不稳定；（3）类别不平衡下泛化能力差。这些缺陷限制了临床应用的可行性和可靠性。

Method: 提出SAGE（Stability-Aware Graph-Based Ensemble）系统，包含两个核心组件：（1）稳定性感知图集成特征选择：融合信息论相关性、PCA结构和图交互，通过Bootstrap稳定性加权识别稳健且非冗余的预测因子；（2）GA-ANN架构：遗传算法优化的人工神经网络，结合GAN过采样技术解决类别不平衡问题。系统还集成了LIME局部可解释AI，实现实例级别的个性化风险评估。

Result: 在766名产后女性主队列上，SAGE仅使用16个特征即达到87.96%准确率、86.32% F1分数和0.88 AUC，显著优于基线和其他特征选择方法。识别出EPDS评分、PHQ-9评分、母亲角色感受和虐待史等心理社会经济因素为主要预测因子，人口统计学因素影响较小。LIME解释提供了图特征层面的个性化洞察。

Conclusion: SAGE为医疗资源有限环境下的PPD早期识别提供了一个可扩展、可解释且具有临床实用价值的工具。系统成功解决了现有方法的三大瓶颈，通过稳定性特征选择、类别不平衡处理和局部可解释性，实现了临床友好的精准风险预测。

Abstract: Postpartum depression (PPD) poses a major burden on maternal and child health, especially in low- and middle-income countries where prevalence exceeds 19%. Despite advancements in machine learning for PPD prediction, current approaches are limited by opaque global explanations that lack clinical usefulness at the patient level, unstable feature selection, and poor generalization under class imbalance. We propose SAGE, a Stability-Aware Graph-Based Ensemble feature selection system that incorporates both local explainable AI and a genetically optimized artificial neural network (GA-ANN). Using a primary cohort of 766 postpartum women, SAGE combines information-theoretic relevance, PCA-based structure, and graph-based interactions with bootstrap stability weighting to identify robust and non-redundant predictors. The GA-ANN architecture, optimized using a genetic algorithm and enhanced with GAN based oversampling, achieved strong performance with 87.96% accuracy, 86.32% F1 score, and 0.88 AUC using only 16 features, outperforming baseline and other feature selection methods. Psychological and socioeconomic factors such as EPDS score, PHQ-9 score, feelings about motherhood, and abuse history are the main predictors, while demographic factors have less influence. The LIME-based explanations allow instance-based insight into selected features from the graph, enabling personalized risk assessment. The findings make SAGE a scalable, interpretable, and clinical tool for early identification of PPD in health-care limited resources.

</details>


### [330] [Fairness-Aware Mixture-of-Experts via Subgroup Reweighting and Gate Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22820)
*Sunhee Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现路由偏置问题并提出了端到端混合专家(MoE)框架，通过子组重加权和门控熵正则化提升模型公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型因训练数据在敏感属性（如性别、年龄）上的不平衡，导致不同人口群体间的性能差异。现有单阶段方法难以处理异质子组分布，两阶段方法可能导致公平性目标与下游预测任务不对齐。

Method: 提出端到端混合专家(MoE)框架来修正路由诱导偏置。具体包括：(1)子组重加权以纠正数据不平衡；(2)门控熵正则化以防止路由坍缩到子组属性上，保持专家利用的平衡性和可解释性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在保持竞争性预测性能的同时提升了模型公平性。路由分布提供了子组如何在专家间分配的可解释视图。

Conclusion: 所提出的MoE框架成功解决了路由诱导偏置问题，实现了公平性与预测性能的平衡，同时提供了可解释的子组分配机制。

Abstract: Deep learning models often produce performance disparities across demographic groups, due to the training data imbalance with respect to sensitive attributes such as gender or age. To address this problem, existing work has explored fair representation learning, data re-sampling, and adversarial training, which can be broadly categorized into two main approaches. Single-stage methods typically learn a shared representation for fairness, but often struggle to handle heterogeneous subgroup distributions. Two-stage methods learn representations separately from the final prediction task, which can lead to misalignment between fairness objectives and downstream predictions. We identify routing-induced bias, a failure mode in which subgroup imbalance drives the gating network to route subgroups onto a few experts, and propose an end-to-end Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) framework that corrects it. Specifically, we apply subgroup reweighting to correct data imbalance, and introduce gate entropy regularization to prevent routing from collapsing onto subgroup attributes, keeping expert utilization both balanced and interpretable. Beyond improving fairness, the routing distribution offers an interpretable view of how subgroups are allocated across experts. Experimental results demonstrate that the proposed approach improves fairness while maintaining competitive predictive performance.

</details>


### [331] [DIME: Query-Efficient Framework for Membership Inference on Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22824)
*Tue Do,Daniel Alabi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了DIME框架，一种基于理论推导的高效扩散模型成员推理攻击方法，仅需2次查询即可超越现有30查询的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的扩散模型成员推理攻击主要是启发式的，且需要大量查询预算，缺乏理论支撑和效率优化。

Method: 基于有限训练集上最优扩散去噪器的精确表征，推导出成员信息泄露由去噪器的隐式重构误差决定。该误差分解为重构准确性的偏差项和捕获训练样本局部几何分布的拥挤项，两者均可通过模型查询高效估计。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100、STL10-U、CelebA和ImageNet数据集上，DIME在相同或更低查询成本下持续优于现有攻击方法，在1% FPR下TPR提升最高达3倍；其2查询变体可超越现有30查询基线方法。

Conclusion: DIME提供了理论驱动的、查询高效的扩散模型成员推理攻击框架，揭示了新的攻击原理，并为设计防御措施提供了依据。

Abstract: Membership inference attacks expose whether individual records were used to train a model, yet existing attacks on diffusion models are largely heuristic and can require substantial query budgets. We introduce DIME (Denoiser Ideal Membership Error), a theoretically grounded and query-efficient framework for membership inference on diffusion models. Our starting point is an exact characterization of the optimal diffusion denoiser for a finite training set, which reveals that membership leakage is governed by the denoiser's implicit reconstruction error. This error decomposes into two complementary signals: a bias term, capturing reconstruction accuracy, and a previously unexplored local crowding term, capturing the geometry of nearby training examples. Both admit efficient estimators using only model queries, yielding a practical attack with as few as two queries. Across CIFAR-10/100, STL10-U, CelebA, and ImageNet, DIME consistently outperforms prior attacks at comparable or substantially lower query cost, improving TPR at 1% FPR by up to $3\times$; remarkably, its two-query variant can outperform existing 30-query baselines. Finally, we suggest, discuss, and evaluate specific defenses to counteract such powerful membership tests.

</details>


### [332] [Hierarchy-Aware Supervised Uncertainty Estimation for Black-box LLM Taxonomic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22839)
*Shuting Xie,Nathaniel Lesperance,Graham W. Taylor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种层次感知的监督学习方法，用于估计黑盒大语言模型在分层分类推理中的不确定性，显著提升了选择性预测的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在科学决策支持中的应用日益增加，但在黑盒设置中可靠置信度估计仍然困难。特别是在长尾生物多样性监测流程中，针对分层分类推理的不确定性估计面临挑战。

Method: 利用开源工具LLM提取代理特征，训练轻量级监督估计器，采用层次感知的监督方式来预测各分类等级的正确性。提出了排名特定的多头设计（H3），以考虑分层输出结构。

Result: 在三个工具LLM上，监督估计器在单一全局拒绝阈值下的微区分度和选择性预测任务中持续优于token-likelihood基线。微AUROC从0.57提升至0.75-0.80。排名特定的多头设计取得了最佳结果。

Conclusion: 当需要统一的弃权规则时，考虑分层输出结构非常重要。层次感知的不确定性估计方法能有效提升黑盒LLM在分类任务中的可靠性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for scientific decision support, yet reliable confidence estimation remains difficult in black-box settings. We study uncertainty estimation for hierarchical taxonomic reasoning generated by a black-box LLM in a long-tailed biodiversity monitoring pipeline. Using proxy features extracted by an open-source tool LLM, we train lightweight supervised estimators with hierarchy-aware supervision to predict rank-wise correctness. Across three tool LLMs, the supervised estimators consistently outperform a token-likelihood baseline for micro discrimination and selective prediction under a single global rejection threshold, improving micro AUROC from 0.57 to 0.75--0.80. The best results are achieved by a rank-specific multi-head design (H3), suggesting that accounting for hierarchical output structure is important when a unified abstention rule is required. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/uoguelph-mlrg/hierarchy-aware-llm-uq

</details>


### [333] [RIBOSPAN: A Long-Context RNA Foundation Model for Versatile RNA Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22849)
*Ziyuan Wang,Bohao Tang,Fei Zhang,Shuo Han,Pengfei Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RIBOSPAN是一个16.1亿参数的双向RNA基础模型，支持原生10,240核苷酸上下文长度，实现了全长转录本的精确建模，在RNA表示学习和mRNA设计任务上达到最先进性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的RNA基础模型在预训练时使用的上下文长度有限，难以对全长RNA（特别是mRNA）进行单核苷酸分辨率的完整转录本建模。长RNA序列往往超过模型能够处理的上下文长度限制，这成为RNA建模的关键瓶颈。

Method: RIBOSPAN采用三种核心技术：1) 密集双向自注意力机制；2) 单核苷酸tokenization；3) 注意力隔离的序列打包技术。模型原生支持10,240 nt上下文长度预训练，并结合40%掩码的继续预训练策略来提升抗噪性能。还开发了多维度条件离散扩散框架用于全长mRNA生成和重设计，包括同义密码子扩散用于保持蛋白质的CDS优化。

Result: 1) 原生10K预训练模型在10,240 token长度上保持强重构能力；2) 长上下文基准测试显示模型保持强上下文响应性和上下文特定表示分离，扰动引起的表示变化高度局部化；3) 推理时YaRN缩放恢复了短上下文模型直接外推丢失的上下文组织；4) 在多种RNA类型上达到最先进的表示质量，在长RNA上保持明显优势；5) 同义密码子扩散实现了蛋白质保持的CDS优化。

Conclusion: RIBOSPAN为可迁移的RNA表示学习和全长转录本mRNA设计建立了强大的长上下文基础，突破了现有模型对长RNA序列建模的限制，为RNA研究和应用提供了新的技术平台。

Abstract: Full-length RNAs, particularly messenger RNAs, often exceed the context lengths used to pretrain existing RNA foundation models, limiting complete-transcript modeling at single-nucleotide resolution. We present RIBOSPAN, a 1.61-billion-parameter bidirectional RNA foundation model natively pretrained with context lengths up to 10,240 nt. RIBOSPAN combines dense bidirectional self-attention, single-nucleotide tokenization, and attention-isolated sequence packing to enable high-resolution modeling of complete long RNAs. We evaluate the model through nucleotide reconstruction, a controlled long-context representation benchmark, and frozen RNA-type representation analysis. Native 10K pretraining preserves strong reconstruction at 10,240 tokens, while continued pretraining with 40% masking improves recovery under heavy corruption while preserving representation quality. The long-context benchmark further shows that native 10K models maintain strong contextual responsiveness and context-specific representation separation while keeping perturbation-induced representation changes highly localized. Inference-time YaRN scaling recovers much of the contextual organization lost by direct extrapolation of short-context models, but induces substantially greater distal representation diffusion. Frozen-representation evaluations further demonstrate state-of-the-art RNA representation quality, with RIBOSPAN achieving the strongest overall performance across diverse RNA types and retaining a clear advantage on long RNAs. Building on the same backbone, we develop a multidimensionally conditioned discrete-diffusion framework for full-length mRNA generation and redesign, including synonymous-codon diffusion for protein-preserving CDS optimization. Together, RIBOSPAN establishes a powerful long-context foundation for transferable RNA representation learning and full-transcript mRNA design.

</details>


### [334] [Thinking at the Right Size: Amortized Distillation Across Post-Trained LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22854)
*Yan Zhou,Sara Kangaslahti,Jonathan Geuter,Nihal V. Nayak,Marco Fumero,Francesco Locatello,David Alvarez-Melis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ADAPT框架，通过一次蒸馏过程生成L×K个模型（L个尺寸×K个后训练变体），大幅降低模型家族部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型实际部署需要多种后训练变体（指令微调、推理微调、对话风格）和多个尺寸以满足不同延迟和内存需求。传统方法独立生产每个（变体，尺寸）对成本过高，现有Boomerang蒸馏虽可沿尺寸轴插值，但仍将每个后训练变体视为独立优化对象，限制模型家族覆盖范围。

Method: ADAPT框架包含两个核心组件：（1）两阶段蒸馏过程，通过预训练对齐和监督微调蒸馏构建后训练学生模型，实现生成和推理任务上的平滑尺寸-性能插值；（2）权重增量初始化，通过将基础模型的蒸馏诱导权重变化迁移到从不同后训练变体初始化的学生模型，近似跨后训练变体的蒸馏构造。

Result: 仅需单次蒸馏运行即可生成L个插值尺寸×K个后训练变体共L×K个模型；构建的插值模型连续体支持推理时自适应模型尺寸选择，改善长形式推理任务的计算-精度权衡。

Conclusion: ADAPT成功实现了沿模型家族的两个维度（尺寸和后训练变体）分摊蒸馏成本，突破了传统方法计算成本高的限制，同时支持动态模型选择，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Practical deployment of large language models (LLMs) requires families of post-trained variants---instruction-tuned, reasoning-tuned, and chat-style models---each at multiple sizes to meet diverse latency and memory budgets. Producing each (variant, size) pair independently is prohibitive, so model families typically span only a handful of coarse-grained sizes per post-trained variant. Boomerang distillation (Kangaslahti et al., 2026) reduces this cost along the size axis for base models. Through model size interpolation, it constructs models of intermediate sizes from a single teacher-student pair without additional training. However, it still treats each post-trained variant as a separate object of optimization. We introduce ADAPT---Amortized Distillation Across Post-Trained LLMs---a framework for amortizing distillation across both axes of a model family: size and post-training variant, producing $L \times K$ models for $L$ interpolated sizes across $K$ post-trained variants with a single distillation run. ADAPT combines two components. First, a two-phase distillation procedure constructs post-trained students through pre-training alignment and supervised fine-tuning distillation, enabling smooth size--performance interpolation on generation and reasoning tasks. Second, weight-delta initialization approximates this construction across post-trained variants by transferring the distillation-induced weight change from the base model to students initialized from different post-trained variants. The resulting continuum of interpolated models also enables adaptive model-size selection at inference time, improving the compute--accuracy trade-off for long-form reasoning tasks.

</details>


### [335] [Mapping the Concept Landscape: Structural Perception of Global Distributions for Transparent Data Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22858)
*Dongyue Wu,Tao Ma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出MCL框架，通过构建样本级和 Dataset 级图谱进行结构化感知，采用贪婪覆盖最大化算法实现透明且高效的数据剪枝，性能优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据剪枝方法依赖高维特征嵌入来衡量样本重要性，但这些压缩向量会掩盖细粒度语义交互，导致剪枝子集对稀有语义概念的覆盖不足。

Method: 将每个图像-标题对表示为显式的样本级图（包含实体、事件和属性），整合为数据集级图谱以刻画语义概念的全局分布并量化稀有度，基于此开发贪婪概念覆盖最大化算法，迭代选择样本以最大化高价值、低代表性概念的边际增益。

Result: 在多个基准测试上，该方法在剪枝效率方面优于最先进方法，并能为样本选择过程提供透明、可解释的审计轨迹。

Conclusion: 所提出的MCL框架通过结构化感知实现了比现有基于嵌入方法更优的数据剪枝效果，同时提供了选择过程的透明性和可解释性，有效解决了语义覆盖不足的问题。

Abstract: Existing data pruning methods predominantly rely on high-dimensional feature embeddings to measure sample importance. However, these compressed vectors often obscure fine-grained semantic interactions, leading to suboptimal coverage of rare semantic concepts in the pruned subsets. In this paper, we propose Mapping the Concept Landscape (MCL), a novel structural perception framework for transparent data pruning. Instead of abstract embeddings, we represent each image-caption pair as an explicit sample-level graph comprising entities, events, and attributes. By integrating these individual graphs into a comprehensive dataset-level graph, we characterize the global distribution of semantic concepts and quantify their rarity across the entire corpus. Based on this structured perception, we develop a greedy concept-coverage maximization algorithm that iteratively selects samples to maximize the marginal gain of high-value, under-represented concepts. Experimental results on various benchmarks demonstrate that our method not only achieves superior pruning efficiency compared to state-of-the-art methods but also provides a transparent and interpretable audit trail for the selection process.

</details>


### [336] [Stochastic Separability of Embedding Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22874)
*Liqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了高维神经空间中嵌入流形的投影集中性和随机可分性定理，为实验观测到的物体流形线性可分现象提供了严格的理论验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经生物学研究和表示学习观察到，同类物体在高维神经空间中呈现低维流形特征，且不同物体流形线性可分，但这一实验现象缺乏严格的理论证明。

Method: 提出新的双层测度集中分析技术，通过全期望定律统一两个估计界限，推导测度集中不等式；在此基础上证明嵌入流形的随机可分性定理。

Result: 证明了嵌入流形在高维空间中的投影集中性质；若两个数据集均值不同且有界总方差，当投影方向满足非奇异性条件时，其样本以高概率线性可分。

Conclusion: 该定理揭示了物体嵌入流形的几何与统计特性，为深度网络中的表示学习提供了新机制，填补了实验现象与理论验证之间的空白。

Abstract: Neurobiological studies and representation learning have observed that representations of objects belonging to the same category in high-dimensional neural spaces exhibit low-dimensional object manifold characteristics, and different object manifolds are linearly separable in these neural spaces. However, these experimentally observed phenomena lack rigorous theoretical validation to date.
  This paper proposes a new stochastic separability theorem for embedding manifolds of two different object categories. First, we establish a projection measure concentration theorem for embedding manifolds under general conditions. We develop a new two-layer measure concentration analysis technique, which unifies two estimation bounds via the law of total expectation to derive measure concentration inequalities.
  Based on the measure concentration theorem, we further prove a stochastic separability theorem for embedding manifolds of two different object categories. If two datasets have distinct means and bounded total variances, their samples become linearly separable with high probability, provided that the projection direction satisfies a non-singularity condition.
  The main contributions of this paper are twofold:
  1. We prove the projection concentration properties of embedding manifolds in high-dimensional spaces by using two-lawyer tail-bound inequalities.
  2. We identify a non-singularity condition for the stochastic separability between embedding manifolds, and rigorously prove the stochastic projection separability theorem.
  The theorem not only uncovers geometric and statistical properties of the object embedding manifolds, but also provides a novel mechanism for representation learning in deep networks.

</details>


### [337] [The Mask Is Not the Model: Auditing Prefix Invariance in Attention, State-Space, and Hybrid Sequence Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22876)
*Taebong Kim,Youngsik Hong,Minsik Kim,Sunyoung Choi,Jaewon Jang,Minseo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级因果性审计方法，通过两次前向传播精确检测大模型中的因果性破坏，发现了现有注意力掩码检查方法的不足，并在多个模型中发现了新的缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型的广泛应用，确保其因果性（即当前位置的表示不应依赖于未来输入）至关重要。现有的注意力掩码检查方法可能无法发现所有因果性违规，例如通过扫描操作或归一化层可能发生信息泄露。因此需要一种更可靠的因果性验证方法。

Method: 论文首先形式化了前缀不变性概念，然后提出了一种轻量级审计方法。该方法仅需要两次前向传播，无需训练或梯度计算。核心思想是对比同一序列在不同前缀下的表示，通过精确的定位技术识别因果性破坏的具体位置。在实验中，作者在8个检查点上进行了192次故障注入试验。

Result: 实验结果显示，传统的注意力掩码检查方法在所有192次故障注入试验中均未检测到任何问题，而本文提出的审计方法成功定位了全部192/192个故障点。更重要的是，该方法在两个实际的模型架构中发现了新的缺陷：Zamba2和Nemotron-H模型存在因果性破坏问题。

Conclusion: 本文证明了注意力掩码检查作为因果性验证方法是不充分的，因为即使掩码正确，信息泄露仍可能通过其他机制（如扫描或归一化）发生。所提出的轻量级审计方法为模型开发和部署提供了一种可靠的因果性验证工具，有助于确保模型的因果特性。

Abstract: We formalize prefix invariance: representations at position t must not depend on future inputs. We give a lightweight audit, two forward passes, no training or gradients, that localizes exactly where causality breaks. Attention-mask inspection is incomplete: leaks can occur via scans or normalization despite correct masks. Across 192 injected-fault trials on eight checkpoints, mask inspection found none, while our audit localized all 192/192, also finding a defect in Zamba2 and Nemotron-H.

</details>


### [338] [Safety Hacking in Constrained Best-of-$N$ Inference-time Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22915)
*Akifumi Wachi,Takumi Tanabe,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了推理时管道中“安全黑客”问题：当使用不完美的安全代理模型筛选输出时，不安全输出可能通过筛选，并在奖励最大化过程中被放大，导致即使在小错误率下，随着采样数N增大，安全黑客也会渐近确定发生。覆盖控制可以限制放大但无法修复被污染的可行集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时管道通常采用“采样-安全筛选-奖励排序”的三阶段流程。然而，这种组合可能存在安全隐患：不完美的安全代理可能让不安全输出进入可行集，而奖励最大化会选择这些输出。作者旨在分析这种两阶段失效机制，定义“安全黑客”现象，并量化其发生条件与概率界限。

Method: (1) 定义“安全黑客”：选择通过学习约束但违反真实安全标准的输出；(2) 对约束Best-of-N采样推导有限N界限，界限由安全与不安全输出的奖励尾部联合决定；(3) 证明若不安全可行输出具有更重的奖励尾部，则当N→∞时安全黑客渐近确定发生；(4) 提出覆盖控制原则：在χ²散度约束下，安全黑客界限与N无关；(5) 实例化覆盖控制方法——约束悲观采样；(6) 在玩具实验和语言模型上验证理论预测。

Result: (1) 给出了有限N下安全黑客概率的理论上界，由可行集中安全/不安全输出的奖励上尾控制；(2) 证明了关键结论：即使假阳性质量、平均安全代理错误和奖励代理错误任意小，只要不安全可行输出的奖励尾更重，安全黑客随N增大渐近必然；(3) χ²约束下的覆盖控制可提供与N无关的安全黑客界限；(4) 覆盖控制虽能限制放大效应，但无法修复可行集污染；(5) 实验验证了污染与奖励尾部放大的存在，揭示了学习安全模型下推理时扩展的内在困难。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了推理时扩展与学习安全模型组合的根本性困境：安全代理的不完美会导致可行集污染，奖励最大化会放大残留不安全输出。这种两阶段失效无法通过简单的覆盖率控制完全解决，暗示需要更深层的架构或训练方法改进，而非仅依赖推理时管道组合。

Abstract: Inference-time pipelines often sample multiple outputs, filter them with a learned safety model, and return the proxy-feasible output with the highest learned reward. We show that this composition creates a two-stage failure: an imperfect safety proxy first contaminates the feasible set with unsafe outputs, and reward maximization can then amplify this residual contamination. We define \emph{safety hacking} as selecting an output that passes the learned constraint but violates the true safety criterion. For constrained Best-of-$N$ sampling, we derive finite-$N$ bounds governed by the joint upper reward tails of safe and unsafe outputs within the proxy-feasible set. If unsafe-but-feasible outputs have the heavier tail, safety hacking becomes asymptotically certain as $N$ grows, even when false-positive mass and average safety- and reward-proxy errors are arbitrarily small. We also show that policies within a bounded $χ^2$ divergence from the proxy-feasible reference distribution admit an $N$-independent safety-hacking bound, and instantiate this general coverage-control principle with constrained pessimistic sampling. Coverage control limits amplification but cannot repair a contaminated feasible set: admitted unsafe outputs may still be favored, and regularized selection is not necessarily safer than constrained Best-of-$N$ for every reward proxy. Toy and language-model experiments characterize both contamination and its reward-tail amplification, which exposes an inherent difficulty in inference-time scaling with learned safety models.

</details>


### [339] [A Momentum-Based Variance-Reduced Algorithm for Federated Multiobjective Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22945)
*Yong Zhao,Chunlin You,Minh N. Dao,Zai-Yun Peng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于动量的方差缩减算法来解决联邦多目标优化问题，收敛率达到O(T^{-2/3})，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统联邦学习主要作为单目标优化问题，专注于最大化模型效用。然而，在实际应用中，机器学习模型通常需要同时优化多个可能相互冲突的目标。这促使了联邦多目标优化(FMOO)的研究，它为在联邦学习中联合处理多个特定任务目标提供了自然框架。

Method: 提出一种基于动量的方差缩减算法用于联邦多目标优化。该方法将动量驱动的梯度估计器整合到本地更新中，以减少随机更新的方差，从而提高收敛速率。

Result: 建立了理论保证，表明随机选择的输出迭代的期望帕累托平稳性度量以O(T^{-2/3})的速率衰减，相比FSMGDA和FedCMOO等现有方法建立的O(T^{-1/2})收敛率有显著改进。在联邦多目标优化基准上的数值实验证明了所提算法的有效性和竞争性能。

Conclusion: 本文成功提出了一种改进的联邦多目标优化算法，通过动量方差缩减技术实现了更快的收敛速率，在理论和实验层面都验证了方法的有效性，为联邦学习中的多目标优化问题提供了更好的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated learning has traditionally been formulated as a single-objective optimization problem, primarily focused on maximizing model utility. In real-world applications, however, machine learning models often need to optimize multiple and potentially conflicting objectives simultaneously. This motivates federated multiobjective optimization (FMOO), which provides a natural framework for jointly handling multiple task-specific objectives in federated learning. In this paper, we propose a momentum-based variance-reduced algorithm for federated multiobjective optimization. The method incorporates a momentum-driven gradient estimator into the local updates to reduce the variance of stochastic updates, leading to an improved convergence rate. We establish theoretical guarantees showing that the expected Pareto stationarity measure of a randomly selected output iterate decays at a rate of $\mathcal{O}(T^{-2/3})$, improving upon the $\mathcal{O}(T^{-1/2})$ rates established for existing methods such as FSMGDA and FedCMOO. Numerical experiments on federated multiobjective optimization benchmarks demonstrate the effectiveness and competitive performance of the proposed algorithm.

</details>


### [340] [Stochastic gradient descent with initial regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22953)
*Nabil Kahalé*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了带初始正则化的随机梯度下降变体（SGDIR），在平方损失下推导了期望过剩风险的免维度上界，证明了在特定条件下SGDIR的期望过剩风险不超过岭回归（最多相差多对数因子），并通过数值实验验证了理论结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究SGDIR算法的理论性质，特别是在期望过剩风险方面与经典岭回归方法的比较，为理解随机梯度下降方法的泛化性能提供理论保证。

Method: 在无噪声和有噪声两种情况下进行分析：无噪声情形在矩条件、源条件和容量假设下推导了平均和非平均SGDIR的上界和匹配的下界；有噪声情形在一般假设和正则化参数的温和下界条件下，建立了SGDIR与岭回归的实例化比较。

Result: 无噪声情形：对于特定源参数值，获得阶为$m^{-2}\log^{2}m$的界（m为训练样本数）；对另一源参数值，当容量参数超过$ε^{-1}$时，获得阶为$m^{-3+ε}$的界；建立了与上界匹配的下界（相差多对数因子）。有噪声情形：证明了SGDIR的期望过剩风险不超过岭回归（相差多对数因子）。

Conclusion: SGDIR方法在期望过剩风险方面具有良好的理论保证，在所考虑的框架下其性能可媲美或优于岭回归，数值实验与理论发现一致，为SGDIR的实际应用提供了坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: We analyze a variant of stochastic gradient descent with initial regularization (SGDIR) and derive dimension-free upper bounds on its expected excess risk for the squared loss. In the noiseless case, we obtain new bounds for both averaged and non-averaged SGDIR under moment, source, and capacity assumptions. For a particular value of the source parameter, these bounds are of order $m^{-2}\log^{2}m$, where the number of training samples is of order $m$. For another value of the source parameter, we obtain, for any $ε>0$, bounds of order $m^{-3+ε}$, provided that the capacity parameter exceeds $ε^{-1}$. We also establish a lower bound that matches our upper bounds in certain regimes up to a polylogarithmic factor. In the noisy case, we provide an instance-based comparison between SGDIR and ridge regression. Under general assumptions and a mild lower bound on the regularization parameter, we show that the expected excess risk of SGDIR is no larger than that of ridge regression, up to a polylogarithmic factor. Numerical experiments on synthetic and real data are consistent with our theoretical findings.

</details>


### [341] [Do Time-Series Foundation Models Pay Off for Industrial Monitoring? A Cost-Aware Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22968)
*Guan-Hua Wen,Kuan-Yu Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）在工业监测任务中的部署价值进行了实证评估。在多个基准测试中，轻量级模型（如TCN-AE、OCSVM）在性能和资源效率上优于TSFMs。TSFMs在零样本和冻结设置下是任务依赖的部署选项，而非轻量级模型的默认替代品。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业监测模型需要在满足数据、校准和资源约束的同时检测操作相关的偏差。尽管时间序列基础模型（TSFMs）承诺可重用表示和零样本预测，但在任务定义异构且轻量级基线具有竞争力的场景下，其部署价值的证据仍然混杂。

Method: 提出了一种协议感知的实证评估方法，涵盖三个设置：C-MAPSS退化风险代理、MIMII异常声音检测的正常训练、以及带有合成目标扰动的BDG2预测残差诊断。评估了经典单类方法、紧凑神经自编码器、残差预测器、MOMENT-small、Chronos-T5和TimesFM 2.5，从异常排序性能、风险范围敏感性、残差预测和扰动敏感性以及本地实现成本四个方面进行比较。

Result: 在C-MAPSS上，TCN-AE的AUROC/AUPRC达到0.9570/0.8960，而MOMENT重构仅为0.7310/0.3080，差异显著。在MIMII上，OCSVM在AUROC和AUPRC上也超过MOMENT重构。在BDG2上，TimesFM 2.5预测误差最低，合成AUROC点估计最高，但合成AUPRC与拟合残差模型相似。资源消耗方面，MOMENT在延迟、VRAM和序列化状态字典大小上均高于TCN-AE。

Conclusion: 在评估的冻结和零样本设置下，TSFMs是任务依赖的部署选项，而非拟合轻量级模型的默认替代品。轻量级模型在特定任务上表现更优，且资源消耗更低。

Abstract: Industrial monitoring models must detect operationally relevant deviations while satisfying target-specific data, calibration, and resource constraints. Time-series foundation models (TSFMs) promise reusable representations and zero-shot forecasts, yet evidence for their deployment value remains mixed when task definitions are heterogeneous and lightweight baselines are competitive. This work presents a protocol-aware empirical assessment across three settings: a C-MAPSS degradation-risk proxy, normal-only training for anomalous-sound detection on MIMII, and BDG2 forecasting-residual diagnostics with synthetic target perturbations. We assess classical one-class methods, compact neural autoencoders, residual forecasters, MOMENT-small, Chronos-T5, and TimesFM 2.5 in terms of anomaly-ranking performance, risk-horizon sensitivity, residual forecasting and perturbation sensitivity, and local implementation cost. Across 100 C-MAPSS engines evaluated out of fold, TCN-AE reaches fold-weighted AUROC/AUPRC 0.9570/0.8960, compared with 0.7310/0.3080 for MOMENT reconstruction; paired engine-cluster bootstrap confidence intervals exclude zero for both differences. Across five matched MIMII pump evaluations, OCSVM also exceeds MOMENT reconstruction in AUROC and AUPRC. On a fixed 12-meter BDG2 panel, TimesFM 2.5 has the lowest aligned forecast error and the highest synthetic AUROC point estimate, although synthetic AUPRC is similar across TSFM and fitted residual models. Same-device measurements show that MOMENT incurs higher latency, peak allocated VRAM, and serialized state-dictionary size than TCN-AE. Under the evaluated frozen and zero-shot settings, TSFMs are task-dependent deployment options rather than default replacements for fitted lightweight models.

</details>


### [342] [Hierarchy-Aware Semantic Losses for Knowledge Graph Link Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22981)
*Filip Kronström,Ross D. King*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出将基于盒嵌入的层次感知语义损失与图神经网络相结合用于知识图谱链接预测，在三个基准数据集上MRR分别提升7.6%、2.4%和15.5%，且显著优于将子类关系作为边加入图谱的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识图谱通常具有包含宝贵语义信息的本体类层次结构，但现有链接预测方法要么忽略这种层次结构，要么仅通过添加额外的图边间接融合。层次感知GNN在生物回归任务中已显示潜力，但其在知识图谱链接预测中的有效性尚未被研究。

Method: 将图神经网络编码器与基于盒嵌入的语义损失相结合，该语义损失通过鼓励学习到的表示更好地满足源自本体的类层次结构，从而在基于GNN的表示学习过程中保持子类关系的一致性。

Result: 在AIFB、CoDEx和BioKG三个基准数据集上进行评估，层次感知语义损失显著提升了平均倒数排名(MRR)，相比基线GNN模型分别提升了7.6%、2.4%和15.5%。该方法在所有数据集上均持续优于将子类关系作为边添加到图谱中的替代方法。

Conclusion: 源自本体的类层次结构为图谱结构提供了补充信息，通过语义损失鼓励层次一致性是一种有效且相对参数高效的机制，可用于提升知识图谱链接预测性能。

Abstract: Knowledge graphs are often accompanied by ontological class hierarchies that encode valuable semantic information, yet many link prediction methods either ignore such hierarchies or incorporate them indirectly through additional graph edges. Recent work introduced hierarchy-aware graph neural networks (GNNs), which use semantic losses derived from box embeddings to encourage satisfaction of subclass relationships during GNN-based representation learning. While this approach has shown promise for biological regression tasks, its effectiveness for knowledge graph link prediction has not been investigated.
  In this paper we evaluate hierarchy-aware semantic losses on link prediction across three benchmark datasets: AIFB, CoDEx, and BioKG. We combine graph neural network encoders with box-embedding-based semantic losses that encourage learned representations to better satisfy ontology-derived class hierarchies, and compare this approach to both standard link prediction models and models incorporating subclass relations as graph edges. Across all datasets, hierarchy-aware semantic losses significantly improve mean reciprocal rank (MRR) and consistently outperform models that incorporate hierarchy information through additional subclass edges. Relative to the baseline GNN models, MRR improved by 7.6%, 2.4%, and 15.5% on AIFB, CoDEx, and BioKG, respectively. Furthermore, semantic losses consistently outperform the alternative of augmenting the graph with subclass edges.
  These results are consistent with ontology-derived class hierarchies providing complementary information to graph structure, and suggest that encouraging hierarchical consistency through semantic losses is an effective and comparatively parameter-efficient mechanism for improving knowledge graph link prediction.

</details>


### [343] [A Physical Response-and-Memory Model for Muon Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.22994)
*Yinze Hu,Hongjun Xiang,Xingao Gong,Hongyu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从物理建模角度解析了Muon优化器中半正交化方向有效性的原因，提出了双时间尺度的Bi-Maxwell优化器，在LLM训练基准上显著减少了达到目标损失所需的步数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Muon优化器通过半正交化动量矩阵实现了优异的训练效果，但两个关键问题缺乏理论解释：（1）为何半正交化方向有效？（2）动量应平均多长的历史？现有解释主要依赖工程直觉和经验。

Method: 将训练过程中的权重矩阵视为具有记忆的响应介质，建立物理模型。在该框架下，半正交化方向被解释为输出侧安全约束下的最大耗散响应；动量对应介质内部累积应力；动量平均时长由应力弛豫过程决定，而真实介质在多时间尺度上弛豫（最简形式为快慢两个时间尺度）。据此提出Bi-Maxwell优化器，并预测最优记忆长度应随训练阶段增长。

Result: 在8条独立训练轨迹上用只读探针测量记忆长度代理量，结果与预测一致（训练早期梯度方向变化快，后期变化慢，最优记忆长度随训练阶段增长）。仅将记忆核从单时间尺度替换为双时间尺度，在公共LLM优化器基准上即可在明显更少的步数内达到目标损失。

Conclusion: 物理建模方法成功解释了Muon优化器的工作原理，半正交化方向的有效性源于其在安全约束下的最大耗散特性。基于双时间尺度弛豫过程的Bi-Maxwell优化器验证了理论框架的正确性，为优化器设计提供了物理洞见。

Abstract: Training large language models is costly. How low a loss the same compute can ultimately reach depends on how each step's gradient is converted into a weight update; the rule that performs this conversion is the optimizer. From SGD and AdamW to the recent Muon, effective update rules have mostly been shaped by engineering intuition and then selected on benchmarks. Muon semi-orthogonalizes the momentum matrix before applying the update and has kept breaking records on public training benchmarks; yet why the semi-orthogonalized direction works, and over how long a history the momentum should average, are two questions at present answered mainly by experience. Here we treat the weight matrix during training as a responsive medium with memory and build a physical model for it, in which both questions find answers: the semi-orthogonalized direction is the maximally dissipative response under an output-side safety budget, which explains why it works; momentum is the internal stress accumulated by the medium; how long it should average is set by the relaxation of this stress, and a real medium relaxes on more than one timescale, the simplest form being one fast and one slow. On this basis we propose the Bi-Maxwell optimizer. The framework further yields a testable consequence: gradient directions change fast early in training and more slowly later, so the optimal memory length should grow with training stage; step-by-step measurements of a proxy for it by a read-only probe across 8 independent training trajectories are consistent with this consequence. Replacing the memory kernel alone, from a single timescale to two, brings training to the target loss in noticeably fewer steps on a public large-language-model optimizer benchmark.

</details>


### [344] [SplitLite: Low-Rank Residual Compression for Split Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23018)
*Tao Li,Yulin Tang,Qi Guo,Xianhao Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SplitLite方法，利用LoRA更新导致的激活和梯度残差的低秩结构，通过量化的截断SVD分解显著降低联邦LLM微调中的通信成本，激活通信减少高达93.5%，总通信减少83.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设备端大语言模型的联邦微调面临巨大计算负担，拆分学习(SL)虽能将训练负载卸载到服务器，但需要在客户端和服务器间交换高维激活值和梯度，导致极高的通信成本。

Method: 发现LoRA使用秩r更新时，相邻epoch间激活残差和梯度残差分别呈现秩2r和秩4r的低有效秩结构；基于此特性，SplitLite仅传输量化的截断SVD残差因子，从而大幅减少激活上行和梯度下行通信流量。

Result: 在GLUE基准测试上对多个先进设备端LLM的实验表明：激活上行通信成本降低高达93.5%，总通信成本降低高达83.7%，且无性能损失。

Conclusion: SplitLite成功解决了拆分联邦学习在LLM微调场景下的通信瓶颈问题，为资源受限设备上的联邦LLM微调提供了高效可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning of on-device large language models (LLMs) faces a significant computing burden. To overcome this limitation, split learning (SL) has emerged as a promising solution, which offloads the primary training workload to a powerful server. However, SL requires exchanging high-dimensional activations and gradients between clients and the server, resulting in prohibitive communication costs. To overcome this challenge, we propose SplitLite, a communication-efficient split federated LoRA fine-tuning method that exploits the low effective rank structure of consecutive-epoch activation and gradient residuals. Our key finding is that, when LoRA uses rank $r$ updates in parameter space, the activation and gradient residuals of the same data sample between adjacent epochs also exhibit effective rank-$2r$ and rank-$4r$ structures, respectively. By revealing this property, SplitLite transmits only quantized truncated singular value decomposition (SVD) residual factors, thereby significantly reducing both activation uplink and gradient downlink traffic. Extensive experiments on the GLUE benchmark across a series of advanced on-device LLMs demonstrate that our method reduces activation uplink communication costs by up to 93.5\% and total communication costs by up to 83.7\%, without performance degradation.

</details>


### [345] [FedCC: Towards Addressing Label Distribution Skews in Distillation-Based Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23031)
*Wenxuan Ye,Onur Ayan,Xueli An,Georg Carle*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FedCC通过引入"未知"类别和校准伪标签来缓解联邦学习中的标签偏斜问题，在极端数据异构场景下将准确率提升至67.3%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于蒸馏的联邦学习中，客户端的异构本地数据会导致标签分布偏斜，使客户端模型偏向多数类并产生不准确的预测。同时，公共数据集缺乏真实标签，阻碍了服务器校准预测的能力，最终降低了整体性能。

Method: 提出FedCC算法，允许客户端将模糊样本标记为"未知"类别而非强制分类，从而避免错误传播。结合公共数据上的校准伪标签，该算法在多数类的置信度与少数类的不确定性之间取得平衡。

Result: 实验表明FedCC显著优于现有方法，尤其在严重标签偏斜情况下表现突出。在极端场景（每个客户端仅持有十类中的一类样本）中，FedCC达到67.3%准确率，而基线方法降至接近随机水平。

Conclusion: FedCC是一种简单有效的方法，通过允许"未知"标签和校准伪标签成功缓解了客户端误分类问题，为解决联邦学习中的标签偏斜挑战提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed clients to collaboratively train models without sharing raw data, making it promising for leveraging massive devices in communication networks. In distillation-based FL, each client applies its local model on an unlabeled public dataset, and shares only prediction results with the server. While heterogeneous local data introduces label distribution skew, thus biasing client models toward majority classes and leading to potentially inaccurate predictions. The lack of ground-truth labels in the public dataset hampers the server's ability to calibrate predictions, which ultimately degrades overall performance. To address this, we propose FedCC, a simple and effective algorithm for mitigating client misclassification. Instead of being forced to classify and risking error propagation, clients are allowed to tag ambiguous samples as 'unknown'. This additional class, together with calibrated pseudo-labels on the public data, balances confidence in majority classes against uncertainty in under-represented ones. Extensive experiments demonstrate that FedCC significantly outperforms existing methods, especially under severe label skew. In the extreme scenario where each client holds samples from only one of ten classes, FedCC achieves 67.3% accuracy, while baselines collapse to near-random results.

</details>


### [346] [ST$^2$U: Stateful Test-Time Unlearning via Restricted Knowledge Boundary Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23034)
*Xunlei Chen,Qinghui Gong,Ruini Xue,Yaodong Hu,Tian Lan,Wenhong Tian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ST²U方法，通过轨迹级边界控制实现大语言模型的测试时遗忘，有效解决现有方法中受限知识重新进入的问题，在保持非目标能力的同时实现更持久的遗忘效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有点式激活编辑方法忽视了自回归生成过程中隐藏状态的持续重构，导致局部成功修正后的后续状态可能返回受限知识区域，造成受限知识重新进入问题。

Method: 提出ST²U（Stateful Test-Time Unlearning）方法：1）在低维可逆坐标中建模受限知识边界；2）在推理过程中监控轨迹风险，应用最小边界修正；3）传播历史修正状态以缓解知识重新进入。

Result: 在三个基准测试和三个模型家族上，ST²U实现了最佳的总体平衡，受限知识重新进入率仅为13.76%-19.84%（相比基线的46.50%-59.10%），同时保持竞争性遗忘效果和非目标能力保持。

Conclusion: 轨迹级边界控制比传统的点式修正更有效，能够在保持非目标能力和限制推理开销的同时实现更持久的遗忘效果。

Abstract: Controlling restricted knowledge in large language models is essential for model alignment and safe deployment. Test-time unlearning avoids costly retraining and parameter updates by intervening only during inference. However, existing activation-editing methods apply isolated pointwise corrections, overlooking how autoregressive generation continually reconstructs hidden states from the prompt, cache, and generated prefix. Consequently, later states may return to restricted knowledge regions after a locally successful correction, causing restricted knowledge re-entry. In this work, we propose Stateful Test-Time Unlearning via restricted knowledge boundary control (ST$^2$U), which formulates test-time unlearning as trajectory-wide boundary control. ST$^2$U first models restricted knowledge boundaries in low-dimensional invertible coordinates while leaving orthogonal non-target components unchanged. During inference, ST$^2$U monitors risk along the trajectory, applies minimal boundary corrections with contextual anchoring, and propagates historical correction states across tokens to mitigate knowledge re-entry. This trajectory-wide control enables more persistent forgetting while preserving non-target capabilities and limiting inference overhead. Across three benchmarks and three model families, ST$^2$U delivers the strongest overall balance, combining best or second-best retention with competitive forgetting and substantially less restricted-knowledge re-entry than test-time baselines (13.76%-19.84% versus 46.50%-59.10%).

</details>


### [347] [Reservoir of Importance: Learning Semi-Structured Sparsity with Differentiable Subset Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23048)
*Ha Dinh,Xuan Duy Ta,Khoat Than,Khac-Hoai Nam Bui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RoI框架，通过紧凑参数化和可微分子集采样，将半结构化剪枝的可学习参数从组合复杂度降至O(M)，实现轻量化、硬件友好的LLM加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可学习掩码方法在半结构化N:M稀疏化中存在参数和内存开销大、难以扩展到大模型和高稀疏度的问题，亟需兼顾效率和可扩展性的解决方案。

Method: 提出重要性储水池框架，采用紧凑logit参数化建模稀疏掩码，通过无放回采样选择掩码，避免对全部分类分布建模，将可学习参数复杂度从组合级降至线性级。

Result: 在Qwen2.5系列(0.5-7B参数)上验证，RoI可学习参数减少1.5-8.75倍，内存开销显著降低，在强内存效率、稳定性和可扩展性下达到竞争性能，支持更激进的N:M稀疏模式。

Conclusion: RoI框架在保持硬件友好稀疏模式的同时实现了轻量化和可扩展性，为高效LLM部署提供了实用路径，证明了参数效率与剪枝性能可以兼得。

Abstract: Semi-structured $N$:$M$ sparsity has emerged as a practical direction for accelerating large language models (LLMs). However, existing learnable-mask approaches incur substantial parameter and memory overhead, limiting their scalability to large models and aggressive sparsity regimes. In this work, we revisit semi-structured pruning from a perspective that reconciles efficiency with scalability. We propose Reservoir of Importance (RoI), a lightweight semi-structured pruning framework that learns sparsity masks through differentiable subset sampling. Unlike prior methods that model full categorical distributions over all feasible $N$:$M$ patterns, RoI introduces a compact-logit parameterization for sparsity mask learning and performs sampling without replacement to select masks, thereby reducing trainable parameters from combinatorial complexity to $\mathcal{O}({M})$. As a result, RoI requires 1.5-8.75$\times$ fewer learnable parameters and significantly lower memory cost, while remaining fully aligned with hardware-friendly sparsity patterns. Extensive evaluations across multiple scales of the Qwen2.5 LLM family (0.5-7B parameters) demonstrate that RoI achieves competitive performance with strong memory efficiency, stability, and scalability to more aggressive $N$:$M$ sparsity patterns, offering a practical path toward efficient LLM deployment.

</details>


### [348] [Graph Representation Learning of Lightweight IoT Ciphers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23054)
*Jonathan Cook,Sabih ur Rehman,M. Arif Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次将机器学习引导的图表示学习应用于轻量级密码算法的差分分析，通过特征工程提取pDDT中的差分属性，构建可视化差分聚类图，实现了零误报的高概率差分识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SIMON和SIMECK等基于Feistel结构的轻量级密码算法易受差分密码分析攻击，需要严格的安全性评估。现有技术主要依赖启发式方法和采样来提高效率，但很少有研究应用机器学习引导的图表示学习方法来高效识别和可视化高概率差分聚类，存在研究空白。

Method: 提出一种高效的特征工程策略，从部分差分分布表（pDDT）中提取四个差分属性，揭示隐藏在原始差分数据中的结构信息。利用这些丰富特征，使用K近邻（KNN）、决策树（DT）和随机森林（RF）三种机器学习方法，为SIMON32和SIMECK32构建并比较有向图模型。

Result: 三种模型在识别高概率差分时均达到1.0的精确率，确认零误报。KNN实现了最强的聚类分离效果、最高的F1分数和约2.3秒的最短图构建时间。决策树和随机森林产生具有近乎完美回归的最优路径。框架首次实现了差分聚类效应的基于图的可视化，高概率单比特差分在学习嵌入中形成几何上接近的聚类。

Conclusion: 该框架在两个轻量级密码算法上结果一致，证明了其对其他AND-旋转类轻量级密码算法家族的适用性。机器学习引导的图表示学习方法能够有效识别高概率差分聚类，为密码算法的安全性分析提供了新的可视化和分析工具。

Abstract: SIMON and SIMECK belong to a family of Lightweight Cryptographic Algorithms (LCAs) based on the Feistel block cipher, designed for Internet of Things (IoT) devices. As with all Feistel ciphers, they are susceptible to differential cryptanalysis, necessitating rigorous resilience evaluations. While state-of-the-art techniques leverage heuristics and sampling to improve efficiency, little work has applied Machine Learning (ML) guided Graph Representation Learning (GRL) to efficiently identify and visualise high-probability differential clusters. We address this gap by introducing an efficient feature engineering strategy that extracts four differential attributes from a partial Difference Distribution Table (pDDT), revealing structural information concealed in raw differential data. Utilising the enriched features, we construct and compare three ML-guided directed graphs for SIMON$32$ and SIMECK$32$ using K-Nearest Neighbour (KNN), Decision Trees (DT), and Random Forests (RF). To the best of our knowledge, our framework produces the first graph-based visualisation of the differential clustering effect, in which high-probability single-bit differentials form geometrically close clusters in the learned embedding. All three models achieve a precision of $1.0$ in identifying high-probability differentials, confirming zero false positives. KNN achieves the strongest cluster separation, the highest F1 score and the lowest graph construction time of approximately $2.3$ seconds, while DT and RF produce optimal paths with near-perfect regression. The results are consistent across both LCAs, demonstrating the applicability of the framework to other AND-rotation LCA families.

</details>


### [349] [Macro-Action Topological Navigation under Noisy Localization using Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23055)
*Simon Hakenes,Tobias Glasmachers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个仅依赖原始像素和相机输入的智能体，在大型真实感3D环境中进行导航，通过局部一致的姿态估计替代传统SLAM系统，成功实现了目标对象的序列搜索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为纯强化学习无法在大型真实感3D公寓中从原始像素进行导航，且现有方法依赖模拟器提供的真实姿态和物体检测信息。本文旨在打破这一限制，构建一个完全依赖自身相机输入进行姿态估计的智能体，更接近真实机器人和生物导航的工作方式。

Method: 提出了一种基于物体中心的姿态估计系统：为每个物体维护ORB特征库，当物体再次被观测时获得粗略姿态测量，通过最小扩展卡尔曼滤波器(EKF)融合运动模型。利用姿态漂移与附近物体漂移一致的特性，只需局部一致的姿态即可执行短边导航并视觉对准目标，从而用小型模型替代完整SLAM系统。

Result: 在真实感Habitat模拟器中，智能体仅通过视觉输入成功导航到达目标物体，证明了局部一致姿态估计在复杂3D环境导航中的有效性。

Conclusion: 局部一致的姿态估计足以支持复杂3D环境中的目标导航任务，无需全局一致的SLAM系统。这种方法模型更小、更接近生物导航机制，为纯视觉导航提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Navigating large, photorealistic 3D apartments from raw pixels is widely considered infeasible for plain reinforcement learning. We build an agent that does it anyway, estimating its own pose from the camera alone. The agent has to reach several target objects in sequence, and their positions change between episodes, so it must explore to find them. It builds on our earlier object-centric topological controller, which still read the agent's true pose and its object detections from the simulator. Here we replace that true pose with an onboard, object-centric estimate. For each object we keep a bank of ORB features that, when the object is seen again, yield a rough pose measurement, which a minimal Extended Kalman Filter (EKF) fuses with a motion model. As on a real robot, the executed motions are noisy. The estimate drifts, but the agent and the nearby objects drift together, so a locally consistent pose is enough to follow each short edge and then home in visually on the target, which lets us replace full SLAM with a much smaller model, closer to how biological navigation appears to work. In the photorealistic Habitat simulator, the agent reaches its target objects from vision alone, with a pose that only needs to be locally consistent.

</details>


### [350] [PolyChirp: Multi-Species Birdsong Classification Using TinyML on Low-Power Acoustic Sensors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23101)
*Nathan Duboisset,Zhaolan Huang,Felix Bießmann,Roudy Dagher,Antoine Lavandier,Emmanuel Baccelli*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PolyChirp实现了在低功耗微控制器上对多达10种鸟类的同时实时监测，突破了现有技术仅能进行单一物种二分类的限制，同时满足整个繁殖季节单次充电运行的资源约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的TinyML技术在低功耗微控制器上的鸟类声学监测仅限于单一物种的二分类，而实际的生态监测部署通常需要同时监测多个物种，这构成了技术瓶颈。

Method: 提出PolyChirp方法，结合生物学领域知识、自动化数据集构建、神经架构优化和新型硬件（配备NPU的微控制器），设计适用于资源受限环境的微型多类分类模型。

Result: PolyChirp不仅在单一物种二分类任务上超越了现有最优方法，还成功实现了对多达10种鸟类的同时鲁棒分类，同时满足内存占用、延迟和能耗等计算性能要求，可在单次充电下维持整个繁殖季节的野外运行。

Conclusion: 通过跨学科方法整合和硬件加速技术的应用，PolyChirp证明了在极低功耗边缘设备上实现复杂多类物种监测的可行性，为野外长期生态监测提供了实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Recent progress in the field of TinyML has demonstrated that low-power hardware based on microcontrollers can achieve bird species monitoring in real time based on acoustic sensor data for an entire breeding period on a single battery charge. However, the state of the art on low-power microcontrollers was so far limited to binary classification of a single species. In contrast, real fauna monitoring deployments often target multiple species simultaneously. To address this challenge we develop PolyChirp, an approach combining biological domain expertise, automated dataset curation, neural architecture optimization and novel hardware to achieve multiclass bird species detection in the wild. PolyChirp is based on newly designed tiny multiclass models that leverage recent microcontrollers and hardware acceleration with a neural processing unit (NPU). We evaluate the predictive performance of these models, and we measure their computational performance -- memory footprint, latency, energy consumption -- on common microcontroller hardware. Our results demonstrate that PolyChirp not only outperforms state-of-the-art on single species binary classification, but also achieves robust classification of up to 10 species simultaneously, while still fitting with the resource envelope of a sensor that must remain operational in the field for a full season on a single battery charge.

</details>


### [351] [DeMixPert: Decomposed Response Modeling with Gaussian Mixtures for OOD Single-Cell Perturbation Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23114)
*Jiawen Liu,Xuechenxiao Cao,Yutong Li,Bing Liu,Jiaming Liang,Tinghe Zhang,Xiaoqi Sheng,Hongmin Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DeMixPert提出了一种新的单细胞基因扰动响应预测框架，通过将扰动诱导的变化解耦为基础状态依赖的系统响应、扰动特异性响应和群体水平变异三个分量，有效解决了现有方法中主导共享模式掩盖弱扰动特异性信号的问题，在未见扰动设置下实现了优异的预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测未见基因扰动的全转录组响应是一个重大计算挑战，因为准确预测需要同时恢复扰动特异性的转录变化和异质性细胞响应。现有方法将确定性响应结构与随机性群体水平变异耦合在一起，导致主导的共享模式掩盖了较弱的扰动特异性信号，并损害了分布建模能力。

Method: DeMixPert将扰动诱导的变化分解为三个分量：(1)基础状态依赖的系统响应，从对照细胞表达编码的基础状态推导；(2)扰动特异性响应，从预训练目标嵌入推断以实现未见目标泛化；(3)群体水平变异，使用高斯原型可逆网络建模。该方法根据基础状态和扰动条件自适应组合可复用的高斯原型，将得到的混合映射到条件特异性变异分布，然后将采样变异与系统分量和扰动特异性分量整合，并与基础状态联合解码重构扰动细胞基因表达。

Result: 实验结果表明，DeMixPert有效捕获了异质性单细胞扰动响应，在未见扰动设置下实现了优异的预测性能。该方法成功解决了现有方法中共享模式掩盖扰动特异性信号的问题，并提升了分布建模能力。

Conclusion: DeMixPert通过创新的解耦框架和混合原型建模方法，为单细胞扰动响应预测提供了一个有效的解决方案，能够在未见扰动条件下实现准确预测，代码已公开发布。

Abstract: Predicting transcriptome-wide responses to unseen genetic perturbations remains a major computational challenge because accurate prediction requires recovering both perturbation-specific transcriptional shifts and heterogeneous cellular responses. Existing methods often entangle deterministic response structure with stochastic population-level variation, causing dominant shared patterns to mask weaker perturbation-specific signals and impair distributional modeling. To address these challenges, we propose \textbf{DeMixPert}, an approach for Decomposed response Modeling with Gaussian Mixtures for Out-Of-Distribution (OOD) single-cell Perturbation prediction. DeMixPert decomposes perturbation-induced changes into a basal-state-dependent systematic response, a perturbation-specific response, and population-level variation. The systematic component is derived from the basal state encoded from control-cell expression, whereas the perturbation-specific component is inferred from pretrained target embeddings for unseen-target generalization. DeMixPert models population-level variation using a Gaussian prototype Invertible Network and adaptively combines reusable Gaussian prototypes according to the basal state and perturbation condition. The resulting mixture is mapped to a condition-specific variation distribution. Sampled variations are integrated with the systematic and perturbation-specific components, followed by joint decoding with the basal state to reconstruct perturbed-cell gene expression. Experimental results show that DeMixPert effectively captures heterogeneous single-cell perturbation responses and achieves superior performance across unseen-perturbation settings. The source code is made publicly available upon publication.

</details>


### [352] [Activation-Weighted Seeded Residual Coding for Low-Bit LLM Weight Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.23144)
*Zehao Liu,Chuangchuang Fang,Yang Ren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AWSRC，一种用于修复低比特量化误差的紧凑编解码器。通过种子选择器、低比特系数和尺度存储残差，而非显式码本。在Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct上，仅增加0.162比特/权重即可填补88.2%、78.9%和71.3%的PPL、KL和准确率差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
